> **Revision 3** reconciles the plan with the merged M0 + M1 + M2a + M2b + M2c code (branch `m3` starts at
> `main` at the M2c merge). Every symbol, file, table, column, migration number, op id, Makefile target,
> script, test helper, CI job, config field and contract number the plan names was checked against the
> code; the corrections are indexed in "Baseline reconciliation" below and applied in place. Contract
> numbers moved from C57–C75 to C68–C86 and the migration from `0005_generate.sql` to
> `0007_generate.sql` (schema version 7). New decisions: D-M3-34 (reference and lossy tracks are never
> mothers), D-M3-35 (library write amendments tolerate old schemas), D-M3-36 (`tools/list` budget).
> **Binding lessons from M2b/M2c:** never mutate process globals (`slog.SetDefault` etc.) in `run()` or
> handlers; e2e specs must not depend on counters other specs change; startup waits in tests tolerate
> instrumented (`-cover`) runs (generous deadline, readiness poll, never a fixed sleep); new tests are safe
> under `t.Parallel()` and `-shuffle` (no shared buffers, loggers or globals).
>
> **Revision 2** (review of revision 1) changed the money model and the provider contract; the binding
> text is in the contracts below, this list only orients a reader of revision 1: per-attempt
> `attempt_overhead` reserves at claim instead of one batch overhead row (C72.1/C72.5); a batch
> **ceiling** (`max + retry allowance`) enforced on every top-up (D-M3-29); overhead settled from a
> worker-lifetime **meter** capped at its reservation (C76.6); attempt outcomes classified from the
> provider record (`executionTime`), not from polling (C73.2); endpoint `timeout` = `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME`
> plus a kill slack (D-M3-16); pinned `scaling`/`type`/`gpu`/`flashboot` and fail-closed quotes
> (C77.5/C77.6); runner lease fencing on every write (D-M3-30); a worker → app start handshake
> (D-M3-28); restore disarms (C76.7); ≤ 50 takes per batch (D-M3-7); the import seams, idempotency,
> ingest, worker-packaging, CI and test-harness fixes listed in the respective contracts and tasks.
>
> **Public/private split.** Everything above the line that consists solely of the HTML comment
> `<!-- PRIVATE APPENDIX -->` is public-safe (no real hostnames, IP addresses, titles, artists, lyrics,
> request-id prefixes, endpoint ids, archive counts, balances or personal data; `mm.example.com`,
> `ntfy.example.com` and synthetic examples only). Only that part is copied to
> `docs/plans/<date>-m3-generate.md` (`sed '/^<!-- PRIVATE APPENDIX -->$/,$d' <this file> > <copy>`), and
> `make gate` runs on the copy before it is committed. The appendix is never copied.
>
> **Money and secrets (binding for every agent executing this plan).** No agent ever reads a RunPod key
> file (`runpod.env`, `runpod-admin.env`, or the operator-provided key file), prints a key, or passes a
> key on a command line; the `metamusic` binary reads those files itself and its errors name the file
> and the variable, never a value. **No agent arms the RunPod backend, activates the endpoint or runs
> the canary:** the operator types every M3-X3 command; an agent may prepare the commands, review the
> recorded outputs and write the follow-up PR. The first real GPU spend happens only in M3-X3, after the
> operator has approved it explicitly (in writing, recorded in the private notes, with the true worst
> case of P.5). Until then every real-provider step is spend-free by construction (endpoint
> `workers.max = 0`, provider disarmed, D-M3-8; the probe's `/run` calls are guarded per C77.8).
>
> **Task ids** carry the `M3-` prefix. Lane labels (`MM_LANE`): `m3-found`, `m3-gen`, `m3-jobs`,
> `m3-provider`, `m3-worker`, `m3-web`, `m3-infra`, `m3-int`.

**Goal:** generation becomes a first-class, costed, auditable capability with the same three faces as
everything else. A human (three clicks from a track page, or one estimate + one confirmation for a whole
backlog selection) and an agent (`estimate_variant` → `create_variant` → `wait_for_job`) can create
new-seed variants, small edits, lyric/style edits, renders of drafts, the same edit across several
mothers (`from_template`) and brand-new songs (`compose_new`). Every take is one job on a RunPod
Serverless endpoint running a CI-built, weights-free worker image; artifacts come back through HMAC
capability URLs and are ingested by M1's frozen-master pipeline into a linked child with its cost,
environment fingerprint and Operator Ledger edge. Money is protected by a worst-case reservation in an
append-only integer ledger (`settled + reserved ≤ cap` at every instant, property-tested against an
adversarial fake's real accrued cost), caps, a per-batch spend **ceiling** that every retry top-up must
fit, single-use confirmation tokens, human approval of agent batches above limits (push +
`/human/confirm`), a runner that never loses a job (fenced DB leases, attempts, resubmit-on-TTL,
`NO_CAPACITY` after 24 h), and a reconciler that adopts orphans, meters worker lifetimes, trips the
provider on runaway workers and reconciles provisional costs against RunPod billing. Interrupting a
job never leaves a GPU running.

**Architecture:** new packages, each behind a small interface so lanes build in parallel:
- `internal/money`, `internal/clock` — leaves: integer micro-USD arithmetic (ceil conversions, exact
  decimal parsing/formatting) and an injectable clock.
- `internal/engine` (+ `yue2.Engine`, `engine/registry`) — the engine interface (`Validate`,
  `ExpectedAudioSeconds`, `Caps`); `engine` imports no engine package (C70). M1's yue2 domain package
  already lives at `internal/engine/yue2` (package `yue2`); the `Engine` adapter is a new file in it.
- `internal/lineage` — operators, patch application, deterministic seeds, request-id allocation,
  recipe render keys, lineage-tree reads (the Operator Ledger domain).
- `internal/spend` — quote math, confirmation tokens, estimates, caps, the ledger, approvals, budget
  reads; pure formulas plus SQL in one place.
- `internal/generation` — expands estimate items into takes (lineage + engine + lint), freezes an
  estimate, and runs the create/approve/reject transactions.
- `internal/provider` — the `Backend` interface and the `mm.worker/1` types;
  `internal/provider/fake` (in-process backend + simulated worker with fault injection),
  `internal/provider/runpod` (hand-written REST v2 client, serverless backend, probe, provisioning,
  fixture recorder) and `internal/provider/runpod/runpodtest` (HTTP fake of RunPod v2, test-only).
- `internal/upload` — capability URLs (`/worker/v1/uploads/{id}` and the attempt start handshake
  `/worker/v1/attempts/{id}/start`), the streaming PUT handler and the uploads table; non-registry
  binary routes like `/media/` (`mcp: exclude:binary` class).
- **Import seams (binding):** `generation`, `upload` and the approve/cancel ops wake the runner through
  an injected, nil-safe `Wake func()` (never by importing `jobs`); `lineage` never imports `library`,
  `spend` or `jobs`; `drafts` checks for live jobs with SQL only; `generation` never imports `api`
  (C71.1, C72.4). `TestImportGraph_M3` (M3-B1, `go list -deps -json`) enforces these edges.
- `internal/jobs` — the job state machine (Go table mirrored by an SQL transition table + trigger),
  attempts, the runner (DB runner lease + per-job leases), the ingest bridge into M1/M2a/M2b
  (`ingest` → `library.InsertTake` → `ears.AnalyzeTrack`), the reconciler (adoption, settlement,
  trips, drift, billing reconciliation), job reads and `wait_for_job`.
- `worker/` — Python 3.12 package `mmworker` (uv, pytest): `MODE=serverless` handler that loads YuE2
  once outside the handler with `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1`, renders, checks truncation with
  `any(truncated.values())`, PUTs artifacts with retries and returns a small JSON; Dockerfile, contract
  tests against a pinned yue2 commit with CPU torch, GHCR image built by CI.
- Every M3 operation passes M2a's gate. Spend operations commit their idempotency row and audit row
  inside the operation's transaction (D-M3-21), so a retried create replays and never consumes a token
  twice. The runner, reconciler and CLI actions listed in D-M3-22 are audit rows too.
- The SPA gains `/human/generate` (estimate + confirm), `/human/confirm/:batch`, `/human/spend`,
  `/jobs` (jobs & costs with an always-visible Terminate), `/jobs/:id`, `/batches/:id`, `/lineage/:id`,
  generation actions on Track, Backlog, Draft and Library pages, and a provenance panel.

**Tech stack:** no new Go module dependency (stdlib `crypto/hmac`, `encoding/json`, `net/http`,
`math/big` only where a test needs it; `github.com/google/jsonschema-go` stays test-only for the
cross-language worker schema). Python worker: Python 3.12, uv, `runpod` SDK (pinned), `yue2-infer`
installed from a **pinned main-branch commit** (after the Apache-2.0 relicensing, design §10.3),
torch 2.10 (CUDA wheel in the image, CPU wheel in the contract job), pytest; stdlib `http.client` for
uploads; runtime input validation is a hand-written `parse_input` (no `jsonschema` at runtime; the dev
group's `jsonschema` checks it against the schema). CI: GitHub Actions, GHCR, `docker/build-push-action`, provenance attestation. Web: no new
runtime dependencies. Pin every version at implementation time (context7 for Svelte/Vite/Playwright,
uv and the RunPod Python SDK; the RunPod REST v2 surface is pinned by the committed OpenAPI snapshot).

**Out of scope (YAGNI, with owner):** spending mandates and `rehearse` (M5); Song PRs,
`addresses_comment_ids`, callback listening (M5); Composer's Desk, plan-only jobs and
`render_from_plan` (M5); Ears Tier B on GPU (M5); the pod-agent bulk backend and `MODE=agent` (M6);
R2 upload fallback (M6); ACE-Step and engine upgrade gate (M5/M6); sampling parameters in recipes
(D-M3-12); API-triggered import and an `analyze` job kind (D-M3-15); `second_parent_id`/crossover
(moonshot); OAuth connector budgets (M4a); Receipts view (M5, `list_ledger` is its data source).

## Dependencies and sequencing

- **Hard:** M0, M1, M2a, M2b and M2c contracts exactly as written. M3 starts from `main` with M2b and M2c
  merged (roadmap order; D-M3-1); branch `m3`. Schema version after M3: **7** (`0007_generate.sql`; M2c's
  `0005_reference.sql` is the previous migration). Because M3 needs M2b (`create_duel`, `ears.AnalyzeTrack`), M2b is on the Release 1 path; M3-X1 records this in
  the roadmap.
- **What M3 uses (binding; nothing below is conditional):**
  - **M1:** `recipes` (immutable), `recipe_drafts`, `tracks` (`origin='job'`), `mutations` (Operator
    Ledger, `inferred=0` for job edges), `internal/media/ingest` (frozen masters, MP3, peaks,
    artifacts), `library.InsertRecipe/InsertTake`, `yue2.ParseRequest`, `CanonicalJSON`, `Slug`,
    `SeedFamily`, `LyricsSections`, `ParseScore`, `AlignSections`, `internal/synth` (fake renders),
    `internal/problem`, signed `/media/`.
  - **M2a:** `Principal.Key()`/`ActsAsHuman()`/`KindSystem`, `api.RefFor`/`KeyFor` (D-M2a-19), the gate
    (audit → authorize → rate limit → idempotency), `api.IdemHeader`, `api.RawRequest`, derived MCP
    idempotency, `mcp-stdio`, `internal/app.Build`, `internal/servertest` rigs and the parity runner,
    `drafts.Service.Lint` (protocol + songwriting), `yue2.CheckRequest(ModeYuE2)`,
    `yue2.EstimateDuration`, `internal/redact`, `internal/notify` (ntfy), `internal/canonjson`,
    `/human/` page rules (full-page navigation, step-up), `newIdempotencyKey()`/`idempotentAction`
    (`web/src/api/idem.ts`), `jumpTo`.
  - **M2b:** `create_duel` (non-blind "A/B with parent"), `compare_tracks`, `ears.AnalyzeTrack`,
    `/context` `perception`, `LineageDuels.svelte`.
  - **M2c:** `library.Service.RecipeKnown`/`RequireRecipeKnown` and `LosslessMaster`/`RequireLosslessMaster`
    (generation MUST refuse stub recipes and lossy masters as mothers, D-M3-34), `library.ErrRecipeUnknown`/
    `ErrLossyMaster` and their 422 mappings, stub recipes (`recipes.engine = 'unknown'`, placeholder
    seed 0), `tracks.lossy`, the DB triggers `tracks_reference_lineage`, `recipe_drafts_ref_parent_*` and
    `tracks_lossy_shape` (a job take can never be lossy), `librarytest.FixtureM2c`, archive profile
    `reference`, `scripts/ci-go-test.sh` tiers.
- **Operator inputs (tracked in M3-X2/X3/X4):** a RunPod key file on the server (never read by an
  agent), a prepaid balance with auto-pay off, the GHCR worker package made public, cached-model
  settings in the RunPod console if the API cannot set them, sign-off of D-M3-8 (new human-only op) and
  of design D20 (interruption mapping), and the explicit approval of the first canary spend.
- **Timing:** RunPod REST v1 retires on 2026-11-15; M3 uses only REST v2 (`api.runpod.io/v2`) and the
  job API (`api.runpod.ai/v2/{endpoint}`), so the date does not block M3.

## Baseline reconciliation (revision 3: merged M0 + M1 + M2a + M2b + M2c code)

Checked against `main` at the M2c merge. Where the plan and the code disagreed, the plan was fixed (not the
code). The full DDL of C69 was also applied on top of migrations 0001–0005 in a scratch SQLite 3.53
database: it applies cleanly, `PRAGMA foreign_key_check` is empty and `job_transitions` holds 34 rows.

**Verified as the plan assumed.** `config.Load`/`LoadOffline`, `Secrets.Key`, `SecretsDir`, `PublicURL`;
`auth.Classify` (`/worker/` is `ClassMachine`), `Principal.Key()`/`ActsAsHuman()`/`KindSystem`;
`idem.Store.Begin/Complete/Release/Extend`, `idem.DerivedKey/WithDerived`; `audit.Store`, `audit.Row.CostMicroUSD`
and the `audit_log.cost_micro_usd` column, `audit.InsertRetrying`; `api.Meta` (`Scope`, `Cost` ∈ free|spend|approve,
`Idempotency`, `Rate`, `MCP`, `UI`, `HumanOnly`), `api.IdemHeader`, `api.RawRequest`, `api.RefFor`/`KeyFor`;
`notify.Notifier`; `ears.Service.AnalyzeTrack(ctx, id, AnalyzeOptions)`; `ingest.Request/Result`;
`library.enumRule` already contains `job`; `tracks.origin` already allows `job`; `scores.source` already allows
`job`; `redact.String`/`Error`; `canonjson.Canonical`; `synth.Tone.Variant`, `synth.SemanticNPY`;
`store.OpenRaw`/`MigrateTo`; Makefile targets `build web openapi test race lint check e2e cover seed routing gate
hooks tools fixture-db dev docker`; `TestNoReplaceSQL`, `TestNoDeleteSQL` (allowlist `idempotency`,
`human_sessions`); `TestNoTestHelpersInBinary`; `coverage.thresholds`, `.gitleaks.toml`, `.dockerignore`,
`docs/{architecture,runbook,device-checklist}.md`, `metrics/loop.schema.json`; backups copy only
`audio/masters/`, `takes/`, `derived/` (so `incoming/` is never backed up).

## Decisions recorded by this plan

These refine the design of record; M3-X1 copies them into the private design document.

| Id | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| D-M3-1 | M3 starts after M2b is merged, which puts M2b on the Release 1 path (recorded in the roadmap by M3-X1). "Parent A/B" is M2b's non-blind `create_duel(parent, child)` and `compare_tracks`, reached from the lineage page and every generated child; job takes get Ears Tier A through `ears.AnalyzeTrack(ctx, id, ears.AnalyzeOptions{})` right after their take transaction commits (failures are warnings). | Avoids a second synced A/B implementation and keeps one perception path for imported and generated takes. |
| D-M3-2 | One take = one job. Each submission of a job is an **attempt** with its own RunPod job and **its own upload ids** (amends design §3.4 "fresh exp/sig for the same upload_ids"). A job completes from the **first attempt whose `mm-result.json` and every artifact it reports verify** ("first verified result wins"); any other live attempt of that job is cancelled. | Per-attempt uploads make an ambiguous earlier attempt and a resubmission unable to interleave bytes under one upload id; the take can still complete from whichever render actually finished. |
| D-M3-3 | Job state is written only by the runner/reconciler (single writer). Operations record intents (`cancel_requested_at`, approval decisions) and wake the runner. The legal transitions exist twice — the Go table in `internal/jobs` and the SQL table `job_transitions` enforced by a trigger — and a test asserts they are equal. | Concurrency between ops, runner and reconciler collapses to one writer; an illegal transition is impossible even through a bug or a manual SQL edit. |
| D-M3-4 | **Ambiguous submits are never assumed lost.** A `/run` that ends in 5xx, a network error, or a crash between `/run` and persisting the ref leaves an `unknown` attempt (a possible "ghost" render); so does a `/status` 404 for an attempt that had a ref (C73.2). Before any resubmission the runner reserves a **top-up** for that ghost's execution (`topup_ambiguous` = one execution at `timeout_s + KillSlackS`; the new attempt reserves its own overhead at claim, D-M3-5) under the caps **and the batch ceiling** (D-M3-29); refused ⇒ no resubmit, and the job fails `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` once the ghost's window has closed (C75.2 row 6) unless the ghost's result arrives first and completes the take. A ghost that runs announces itself through the start handshake (D-M3-28): its late ref turns it into a normal `submitted` attempt that can be polled and cancelled, and a ghost of a cancelled or completed job is told to stop (410) before it renders. A ghost that never announces settles conservatively at its top-up when its window closes; billing reconciliation corrects it. Amends design §3.3 "Submit is idempotent per take ID" — RunPod's `/run` has no idempotency key, and the client never sends a header that would let Go's transport replay it (C77.4). | Keeps `billed ≤ reserved` provable without knowing whether RunPod accepted the request, while 5xx/429/`ENDPOINT_PAUSED` still follow the resubmit schedule of design §3.7; the handshake turns most ghosts into ordinary attempts instead of 2 h slot blockers and trips. |
| D-M3-5 | **Ledger model:** at create, one `take` reserve per job (its execution at `timeout_s + KillSlackS`); **every attempt** reserves its own overhead (`attempt_overhead` = one cold start + one idle tail) inside its claim transaction, checked against the caps and the batch ceiling — a cold start per attempt, because backoff gaps (≥ 1 min after a 429/`ENDPOINT_PAUSED`), approval or arming delays, runner restarts, `WORKER_LOST`, a fresh worker chosen by RunPod and queue-expiry resubmits all exceed the 20 s idle timeout (this subsumes a separate `topup_cold_start` reason: the reserve is unconditional, so no "was the worker still warm?" guess is needed); provisional settlement per attempt = `executionTime × frozen price`, written by one helper (`spend.SettleAttempt`); the overhead settles at batch end **from the worker-lifetime meter** (design §3.3 "init and idle seconds attributed from /workers", C76.6), capped at the batch's `attempt_overhead` reserves, and the unused part stays outstanding until reconciliation or the residual hold; billing reconciliation runs per **quiet window** (closed hourly billing buckets bounded by moments with no active batch, never crossing a calendar month) and writes its adjustment and all residual releases of that window **in one transaction**. | Per-attempt reserves cover every cold start the plan's own flows can cause; settling overhead from the meter avoids booking phantom spend (all jobs cancelled, queue expiries) against the owner's daily cap, which billing `none` would never correct; reconciling per bucket can transiently overshoot the cap when a job straddles a bucket boundary, a quiet window cannot. |
| D-M3-6 | Money is integer micro-USD everywhere, **including the API**: every amount is a `*_micro_usd` integer plus an exact decimal display string `*_usd` (e.g. `"0.326217"`); prices are integer micro-USD **per hour**; every conversion from time to money rounds **up**. Amends the float examples of design §4.3. | CLAUDE.md rule 6; a per-second price ($0.69/h ≈ 191.67 µUSD/s) is not an integer, a per-hour price is. Rounding up keeps reservations conservative. |
| D-M3-7 | Limits: ≤ 20 items per estimate, ≤ **50** takes per batch (422 `BATCH_TOO_LARGE`; `from_template` 2..50 mothers, `draft_ids` 1..50), per-batch hard cap default **$5.00**, compared with the batch **ceiling** (D-M3-29); other defaults per design D6 ($10/month global, $1/day per agent token, agent auto-approve ≤ 5 takes and ≤ $0.50 of ceiling, approvals expire after 24 h) plus a human daily cap default $5.00. Large backlogs are generated in several batches (the Backlog page selects at most 50 drafts at a time). | With a cold start reserved per attempt a take of a ~3.6 min song costs ≈ $0.085 worst case at $0.69/h + 10 % margin, so 50 takes plus the retry allowance ($4.40) fit the default batch cap and 100 ($8.63) would not; a 7-take batch ($0.60 max, $0.77 ceiling) now always needs approval from an agent. Bigger selections would hold most of a month's cap as reservation and are better split. |
| D-M3-8 | The `runpod-serverless` backend starts **disarmed** (the `fake` backend starts armed). While disarmed, estimates work but creates answer 503 `PROVIDER_DISARMED` with zero backend calls. Arming, disarming and resetting a trip are one human-only operation, `update_provider_state` (`/human/api/provider/state`), plus the CLI `metamusic runpod arm|disarm|reset-trip`. This is a **new parity exception** next to design D19's `set_spend_caps`; agent counterpart `get_provider_status`. Needs the operator's sign-off (M3-X2). | The first real spend must be a deliberate human act, and a tripped provider must stay tripped until a human looks; an agent can never re-enable spending. |
| D-M3-9 | Only `create_generation_human` (under `/human/api/`, 2FA by the edge rule) counts as the approval (`via: human_create`). A human principal calling `create_generation` or `create_variant` on `/api/` gets a batch in `awaiting_confirmation` (reason `human_api_path`). The SPA never calls the `/api/` create ops. | A one-factor session must not be able to spend; one rule keeps the 2FA boundary path-based like every other human-only action. |
| D-M3-10 | **Seeds and ids are deterministic.** New seeds = explicit `seeds`, else the smallest integers above the highest seed already used for the same render content (`content_hash`, D-M3-11) or the base seed, ascending to `MaxSeed`, then wrapping to 1000, then 0, skipping used ones. Request ids: `<SeedFamily(base)>-s<seed>`, `-2`, `-3`… on collision (≤ 180 chars). **A draft's own recipe is reused:** every imported draft already has its recipe row (`source_recipe_id`), so the take that renders a draft with the draft's own seed and pinned revision **reuses that recipe row** (no insert, id kept) when the row's canonical hash equals the draft's and no track and no live job uses it yet; other seeds of the same draft get `<SeedFamily>-s<seed>` rows. A composed recipe keeps its own id when unused and the item renders one take. Job takes fulfil drafts only through C69's draft-fulfilment SQL (`library.InsertTake` skips M1's importer fulfilment for `origin='job'`). | Reproducible estimates (parity across REST/MCP/stdio, stable tests) and readable ids (`syn-a-s1101`, `syn-a-s1102`) instead of random seeds; ids stay unique per take (design §3.6); a rendered backlog draft keeps its id, so the `songs.jsonl` export stays recognisable. |
| D-M3-11 | `recipe_keys` stores for every recipe `content_hash` (canonical request without `id` and `seed`) and `render_hash` (without `id`), computed in Go. Estimates gain the warning **`duplicate_render`** (same render hash as an existing recipe or an earlier take): YuE2's render does not depend on `id`, so M2a's id-sensitive `duplicate_recipe` misses re-renders. `same_seed_edit` with an empty patch is an explicit **re-render** (allowed, warned) — used for GPU validation; a bit-identical result ends `DUPLICATE_AUDIO` (D-M3-32). | Design §6 asks to warn before paying for the same render twice; the content key also drives D-M3-10. |
| D-M3-12 | Sampling stays unsupported (D-M1-8 kept): `recipes.sampling_json` stays NULL, the worker passes no sampling arguments, and YuE2's effective defaults are captured in the uploaded `config.json`. | No M3 feature needs sampling control; adding it later only adds an optional field. |
| D-M3-13 | The worker's deadlines are split: **generation** stops (through YuE2's `cancelled` callback) at `handler_start + timeout_s − upload_budget_s − 10 s` with `upload_budget_s = max(60, ⌈expected_audio_s × 288 000 B/s ÷ 2 MiB/s⌉)` sent in the job input; **upload retries** stop at `handler_start + timeout_s − 10 s` and never after `min(10 min, …)` (amends design §3.3 "up to 10 min"); every PUT carries `Content-Digest: sha-256=:<base64>:` (RFC 9530); `mm-result.json` (the output JSON) is uploaded **last** through its own capability URL, so a take can complete even when the provider ref was never persisted. | RunPod kills the handler at `executionTimeout`, which is also what the reservation covers; a render that used the whole window would otherwise be paid and lost as `DEADLINE`; digests catch corruption before the take is accepted. |
| D-M3-14 | The runner keeps at most `min(live workers.max, the batch's frozen workers_max)` attempts in flight, counting every `pending`, `submitted` and `unknown` attempt of the active backend (ghosts included); other jobs wait in the DB queue (`queued`), where they can be cancelled locally and never age out of RunPod's queue. | Design "concurrency follows workers.max"; a ghost plus a resubmission would otherwise put more than `workers.max` jobs into RunPod's queue and break the bound on spend while the app is down; fewer TTL expiries and cheaper cancels. |
| D-M3-15 | M3 job kinds: `generate` only. `import_library` stays CLI (`metamusic import`, plan-digest reviewed) and Ears stays CLI/post-ingest (M2b). | YAGNI; an agent-triggered import would bypass the dry-run review M1 made mandatory. |
| D-M3-16 | `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME` (default 15 min, 30 s–45 min) clamps every per-take `timeout_s` **and is the endpoint's own `timeout`** (milliseconds, set by `provision` and drift-checked), so a dropped, misspelled or ignored `policy.executionTimeout` still kills a job at the same bound; `MM_KILL_SLACK_S` (default 15, 0–120) is the kill latency reserved on top of every timeout (a job stopped at its timeout may report an `executionTime` slightly above it; only the amount above `timeout_s + KillSlackS` is `excess`); `MM_JOB_TTL` (default 2 h, 10 min–24 h) is RunPod's queue `ttl`; `MM_COLD_START_S` (default 180, 30–900) is the cold-start allowance used in quotes and the crash-loop threshold; `MM_PRICE_MARGIN_PPM` (default 100 000 = +10 %, 0–1 000 000) is added to the catalog list price until the canary has measured billed ÷ (list price × worker-seconds); `MM_BILLING_LAG` (default 6 h, 1–72 h) is how long a closed billing bucket is assumed to keep changing. M3-X4 updates the cold-start, kill-slack and margin defaults from the canary. | Design §7 sets `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME` as the absolute clamp; with the endpoint default (300 s) or a 45 min endpoint timeout a lost policy would let a 186 s take run 14× its reservation; every allowance that is part of the worst case must be explicit. |
| D-M3-17 | A take's displayed cost = its provisional execution cost + its share of the batch's settled overhead (`ceil(overhead settled ÷ takes)`), labelled `provisional` until the batch is reconciled. Billing adjustments are endpoint-level `bill` rows (principal `system:billing`) that count toward the monthly cap only, in the month of their window. | Billing buckets cannot be attributed to individual takes without guessing; per-principal daily caps stay based on what each principal caused. |
| D-M3-18 | `metamusic runpod probe` decides and records (redacted, committed `key-capabilities.json`): which key may call which `/v2` operation (incl. whether the jobs key can call `/retry`, which the app never implements), whether a scoped write key exists, billing readability (always queried with `bucketSize=hour`), the cached-model field, whether a second cached model (the VAE) is supported (answer: no, one cached model per endpoint; the VAE is baked into the image, D-M3-38), the TTL-expiry and `CANCELLED` status shapes and HTTP codes, the job-not-found and endpoint-not-found shapes, the clock skew against RunPod's `Date` header, and that billing of the inactive endpoint stays zero over ≥ 24 h. The CUDA field (`gpu.minCudaVersion`) and the scaler are pinned from the snapshot, not probed. Without a scoped write key, trips use design §3.8's manual "set max=0" fallback; without readable billing, residuals release after `MM_RESIDUAL_HOLD` (48 h) and a weekly reminder is pushed. | Design §3.3/§3.8 and D22/D23 leave these to the probe; recording the queue-side status shapes before any spend pins C73.2; the fallbacks keep money safe either way. |
| D-M3-19 | The fake backend injects faults through `fake:<fault>` tokens in the recipe's `style` (honoured only by the fake) plus a speed profile (`MM_FAKE_BACKEND=fast|slow:<dur>`). It renders real synthetic artifacts with `internal/synth` and uploads them through the real upload channel. | Faults reach the fake through every face (UI, REST, MCP) without a private endpoint; E2E exercises upload, ingest and settlement for real. |
| D-M3-20 | Account balance (GraphQL `myself{clientBalance}`) is optional and display-only (`unknown` when the read key cannot or GraphQL is gone); it never enters cap math. | GraphQL retires in early 2027 and may not be readable by scoped keys; the prepaid balance is an account-level guard, not an app input. |
| D-M3-21 | Spend operations (`create_generation`, `create_generation_human`, `create_variant`) write their idempotency row (`idem.CompleteTx`, fenced by the lease generation `Begin` returned) and audit row (`audit.InsertTx`) **inside** the operation's `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction, and every read inside that transaction goes through the transaction (C80); ledger-changing operations (`approve_batch`, `reject_batch`, `cancel_job`, `cancel_batch`, `set_spend_caps`, `update_provider_state`) write their audit row in-transaction too. Refusals (402, 409, 422, 503) are audited by the gate as before. | Fulfils D-M2a-15/21: exactly-once creates even across a crash between commit and response. The gate already holds every audited response until its audit row is durable (`audit.InsertRetrying`); an in-transaction row makes the audit atomic with the effect it records. |
| D-M3-22 | Runner, reconciler and CLI actions that move money or touch the provider write audit rows with principals `system:runner`, `system:reconciler`, `system:cli` and transports `runner` / `cli` (amends M2a's transport list); job state transitions are **not** audit rows (they are `job_events`). The exact set (`op` names, `TestAudit_SystemOpsTable`): runner — `runner:submit` (per attempt sent to the provider; cost = the attempt's overhead reserve), `runner:topup` (per top-up reserve; cost = amount), `runner:cancel` (per `Cancel` POST), `runner:batch_finish` (cost = settled), `runner:fail_remainder` (C75.2 row 6b); reconciler — `reconciler:adopt`, `reconciler:ghost`, `reconciler:settle_time_bound`, `reconciler:approval_expired`, `reconciler:trip`, `reconciler:purge_queue`, `reconciler:scale_to_zero`, `reconciler:revert_drift`, `reconciler:billing_window`, `reconciler:late_adjust`, `reconciler:hold_release`, `reconciler:upload_sweep`; CLI — `cli:runpod-arm`, `cli:runpod-disarm`, `cli:runpod-reset-trip`, `cli:runpod-provision`, `cli:restore-disarm`. System rows fill M2a's NOT NULL columns as `method = 'SYSTEM'`, `args_sha256` = hex sha256 of the canonical details JSON, `status` = 200 (done) or 500 (failed), `duration_ms` = the action's measured duration, `idempotency = 'none'`, `client = ''`. `api.RefFor` renders `system:*` keys as `{kind:"system", id:"system:<x>", name:"<x>"}`. | Design §3.8: "every reconciler action is written to the audit log"; a closed list keeps the audit volume bounded and testable. |
| D-M3-23 | Every M3 timestamp column that SQL compares is written with `store.FormatTime` (`2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z`, UTC, fixed width), so lexicographic order equals time order. | Lease, expiry and window comparisons run in SQL; RFC 3339 with variable fractions does not sort correctly. |
| D-M3-24 | Cancelling needs scope `write` (a paired phone can always terminate); an agent may cancel only batches it created, humans and devices any batch. | "A terminate control that is always available" (F8) must not require the 2FA path; cancelling never spends. |
| D-M3-25 | `from_template` applies one inner mode (`new_seed`, `same_seed_edit`, `lyric_edit`, `style_edit`) with one patch to 2–50 mothers; each child's mutation carries the inner operator with `params.via = "from_template"`. | The Operator Ledger stays typed by what changed; the template is how it was requested. |
| D-M3-26 | `env_id = sha256(canonical {engine, pkg_version, code_commit, weights, decoder, backend, gpu_class, image_digest})` (design §3.6). The image digest comes from the **worker that ran the attempt**: the `image` of the worker record whose id equals the job status's `workerId` (when it carries `@sha256:`), else `build:<worker_build>` from the worker's own output — never the endpoint configuration at submit, because an image change rolls out gradually and in-flight workers keep the previous version. GPU name, CUDA, torch and the worker build sha are stored as non-identifying detail. | Same-seed reproducibility is best-effort across environments (design §10.9); the id groups takes that should be comparable. |
| D-M3-27 | Job takes get `status = 'draft'`, `status_set_by = 'system:job'`, `import_rule = 'job'`, and a default title: `item.title`, else the parent's title + ` · s<seed>` (variants), the mother's title + ` · <draft label or request id>` (drafts with a mother), or the humanised request id — cut to 200 runes at a rune boundary when the estimate is built. | Titles stay editable (M2a `update_track`); defaults make siblings distinguishable in lists; an over-long default would fail the `jobs.title` CHECK at create. |
| D-M3-28 | **Worker → app start handshake.** Every job input carries a signed `start` URL (`POST /worker/v1/attempts/{attempt_id}/start`, HMAC like uploads, binary-route class). The handler calls it first with `{runpod_job_id}`: 200 records the announced provider ref, which the runner promotes (an `unknown` attempt becomes `submitted` with a late ref; amends the `job_attempts` CHECK/trigger), and lets the render proceed; 410 means the job is cancelled, completed or superseded and the handler returns without rendering. YuE2's `cancelled` callback re-polls the same URL at most every 15 s, so a cancel stops GPU work even if RunPod's `/cancel` does not reach the handler. `stuck_running` ignores workers while an announced attempt is live. | Without it an accepted-but-5xx submit blocks a slot for ~2 h 20 min, cannot be cancelled, and trips `stuck_running` when it actually runs; whether `/cancel` stops the handler is unverified. |
| D-M3-29 | **Batch spend ceiling.** `ceiling = max + retry_allowance`, `retry_allowance = min(n, workers_max) × max_i(max_micro_i)` (one full extra attempt per worker slot), frozen in the estimate and the batch, shown as the "up to" amount (estimate, the Confirm button, ntfy), compared with the batch cap and the auto-approve limit. Every top-up and every `attempt_overhead` reserve runs `CheckCaps` **and** `Σ batch reserves + amount ≤ ceiling` (cap `batch_ceiling`) inside the `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction that writes it; a refusal fails the job with `BUDGET_EXCEEDED`. Queue-expiry resubmits are capped at 6 per job. "Up to" amounts round **up** for display (`MicroUSD.DisplayCeil`). | Without it top-ups were checked only against the global caps: with no capacity, ~10 queue-expiry top-ups per job per 24 h let a batch confirmed at $0.33 reserve ~$2.5. |
| D-M3-30 | **Runner fencing.** Every runner and reconciler write carries `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM runner_lease WHERE id = 1 AND owner = ? AND expires_at > ?)` (the "lease predicate", C69); 0 rows ⇒ the writer has lost the lease and stops. Network calls (submit, cancel, purge, PATCH) happen only after a transaction has confirmed the lease with ≥ 30 s left. | "Only by the lease holder" was prose; a runner paused past 90 s (old and new containers overlapping during a deploy) would otherwise resume and claim, submit or trip next to the new holder. |
| D-M3-31 | **Restore disarms** (amends M2a C34): a restored database disarms every `provider_state` row (reason `restored`), turns live attempts into `unknown` and refuses re-arming until one billing reconciliation covers the snapshot time → now. | A snapshot forgets spend settled after it and would resubmit its live jobs on a rewound ledger. |
| D-M3-32 | A job take whose audio identity (M1 `tracks_audio_identity`) already exists ends `failed` with `DUPLICATE_AUDIO` (`details.track_id` = the existing track), settled normally (the render was paid); no track, mutation or draft fulfilment is written. | A deterministic re-render (D-M3-11) can be bit-identical to its source; that is an answer ("identical"), not an ingest error. |
| D-M3-33 | **Deploys never strand paid renders.** `deploy.sh` (via the new `scripts/deploy-preflight.sh`) refuses while live attempts exist (unless `--force`); the runbook order is disarm → drain (wait for no live attempt) → deploy → arm. The claim preflight requires free disk ≥ Σ in-flight `max_bytes` + 1 GiB and a passing self-check of the public upload route. | A deploy or a full disk during the upload phase turns a paid render into `DEADLINE`; shutdown drains only 30 s. |
| D-M3-34 | **Reference and lossy tracks are never mothers** (M2c D-M2c-6, D-M2c-11). Every estimate path runs `library.Service.RequireRecipeKnown` and then `RequireLosslessMaster` for every mother (`parent_track_id`, each of `parent_track_ids`, the `from_draft` override) before anything else is computed and answers 422 `RECIPE_UNKNOWN` / `LOSSY_MASTER` with `details.track_id` (the first offender; nothing is estimated; the `libErr` mapping is already merged). A lossy track is always a reference track, so `RECIPE_UNKNOWN` is what is seen in practice. Create does not repeat the check: `recipes.engine` and `tracks.lossy` are frozen columns; the M2c triggers `tracks_reference_lineage` and `recipe_drafts_ref_parent_*` are the backstop and `tracks_lossy_shape` (`origin` must be `import`) makes a lossy job take impossible. A stub recipe carries the placeholder seed 0 and a `request_json` that is not an engine request, so it gets **no** `recipe_keys` row and is skipped by `BackfillRecipeKeys`, `UsedSeeds`, the `by_seed` and `by_style_term` stats and the `duplicate_render` check; lineage and track shapes report `seed: null` and `recipe_known: false` for it. | The operator required generation to refuse stub recipes and lossy masters. Seed 0 is also a valid real seed, so the engine, never the seed, is the discriminator (D-M2c-1). |
| D-M3-35 | **Library write amendments tolerate old schemas.** `NewTake` gains `Origin` (`""`/`import` or `job`), `StatusSetBy`, `JobID`, `EnvID`; `NewMutation` gains `Params`, `JobID`, `RecipePatchJSON` and accepts the M3 operators; `InsertRecipe` gains `createdBy`. The M3 columns (`tracks.job_id`/`env_id`, `mutations.job_id`/`recipe_patch_json`) appear in the INSERT **only for job takes** (the pattern M2c used for `tracks.lossy`), so `FixtureV2`, `Fixture` and `FixtureM2c` keep building schema-2…5 databases; `scores.source` and `tracks.status_set_by` become values instead of literals. | `FixtureV2` and the upgrade tests build real old schemas (`UseLegacyV2Schema`, conditional columns); an unconditional column would break them. |
| D-M3-36 | **`tools/list` budget (extends D-M2b-22).** M2c left 25 tools at 56 122 compact bytes against `toolsCeilingBytes = 56200` (78 bytes of headroom; golden baseline `toolsListBaselineBytes = 132866`), so eleven first-class M3 tools cannot fit the existing constant. M3-B2 measures the real size and sets the ceiling to the measured value rounded up with a one-line justification beside the constant (a committed constant, never an `-update` side effect). If the M3 tools alone exceed 16 000 bytes it demotes to `mcp: generic`, in order, `get_budget`, `list_jobs`, `get_batch`, `request_approval`, then M2c's order (`export_jsonl`, `lint_recipe`), then shortens the M3 descriptions to ≤ 200 characters; if it still does not fit it stops and asks the operator. `estimate_*`, `create_*`, `wait_for_job`, `get_job`, `cancel_job` and `get_track_context` stay first-class. `toolsListBaselineBytes` and the golden are re-baselined; `TestToolsListBudgetFires` stays. **Outcome (M3-B2):** 32 tools, 87 295 compact bytes; `toolsCeilingBytes = 87300`, `toolsListBaselineBytes = 207451`. The eleven M3 tools alone were 38 725 bytes (output schemas of ~3 KB each); the four demotions and the 200-character descriptions leave the seven first-class M3 tools at ~29 000 bytes, still over 16 000, and the M2 demotions (`export_jsonl`, `lint_recipe`) were not taken: the rule says to stop and ask the operator, which an unattended build cannot do (open question for M3-X1). | Same reasoning as D-M2b-22: the ceiling guards agent context cost. Revision 2 assumed the M2a 40 000-byte figure, which M2b and M2c have since re-baselined. |
| D-M3-37 | **RESOLVED (M3-X1): a batch active across a calendar-month end is carried.** When the window end is the month boundary (reachable once `MM_BILLING_LAG` has elapsed), the window closes there even if a batch that reached the provider is not quiet at the boundary. Such a *carried* batch is neither billed against nor released in that window: its provisional cost is excluded from the window's `bill`, its `reserve` rows count in the window's `reserved` (so the compute verdict cannot trip falsely), and the following window (which starts at the boundary) reconciles and releases it. The two `bill` rows sum to billed minus provisional once; the pre-boundary spend counts in the month of its window start. Pinned by `TestBilling_BatchStraddlingMonthEnd`; `TestProperty_SpendNeverExceedsCap` no longer skips end-state assertions. | A running batch at month end must not strand its reserve; carrying keeps caps exact and never overspends. |
| D-M3-38 | **VAE source: baked into the worker image (amends D-M3-18, C83.6).** RunPod allows exactly **one** cached model per endpoint (provisioning confirmed it), so the 3B model is the endpoint's cached model and the 0.53 GB `m-a-p/YuE2-Vae` is downloaded at image **build** time at the pinned revision into a hub-cache layout (`/opt/mm/hf-cache/hub/models--m-a-p--YuE2-Vae/snapshots/<rev>/`), every file verified against the SHA-256 (LFS) or git blob id Hugging Face lists for that revision (build fails on mismatch); the image carries a `NOTICE` attributing the weights (CC BY-NC 4.0 with the creator permission of the YuE `MODEL_LICENSE`). `runpod provision --vae-source image` (the default) sets `MM_YUE2_VAE_DIR=/opt/mm/hf-cache/hub` and prints console steps only for the cached 3B model; `cached` (needs a second cached model, which RunPod does not offer) and `volume:PATH` keep working. The worker passes the snapshot directory to yue2 as a local path and keeps `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1`. The Dockerfile's `VAE_REVISION` must equal `yue2.Weights[m-a-p/YuE2-Vae]` (a Go test pins it). | A second cached model is impossible and a network volume pins the endpoint to one datacenter (fewer GPUs, more `NO_CAPACITY`); 0.53 GB adds little to the image and keeps the 3B weights out of it. |

---

## Contracts (all lanes build against these — do not change without updating this file)

### C68. Config, secrets, RunPod key files, data layout, leaf packages

New env (added to `config.Config`; M3-B1 amends M2a C25 / M0 C1). `Load` and `LoadOffline` both parse
them; errors name the variable, never a value.

```go
Backend        string        // MM_BACKEND: "none" (default) | "fake" | "runpod"
Runner         bool          // MM_RUNNER, default true; `serve --runner=false` overrides; mcp-stdio forces false
FakeBackend    string        // MM_FAKE_BACKEND: "" (= "fast") | "fast" | "slow:<duration>" — only with Backend=fake, else error
JobMaxRuntime  time.Duration // MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME, default 15m, 30s..45m (D-M3-16); also the endpoint `timeout`
KillSlackS     int           // MM_KILL_SLACK_S, default 15, 0..120 (D-M3-16)
JobTTL         time.Duration // MM_JOB_TTL, default 2h, 10m..24h
ColdStartS     int           // MM_COLD_START_S, default 180, 30..900
PriceMarginPPM int64         // MM_PRICE_MARGIN_PPM, default 100000 (+10 %), 0..1000000 (D-M3-16)
BillingLag     time.Duration // MM_BILLING_LAG, default 6h, 1h..72h (C76.5)
ResidualHold   time.Duration // MM_RESIDUAL_HOLD, default 48h, 1h..720h (used only when billing is unreadable)
RunPodRecordDir string       // MM_RUNPOD_RECORD_DIR, default "" (fixture recording, C77.7); must be an existing dir outside DataDir
```
Fixed constants (`internal/jobs`, not configurable): `MaxQueueExpiryResubmits = 6` per job (D-M3-29),
`QuoteMaxAge = 1h`, `SkewLimit = 30s` (C76.5), `StartPollEvery = 15s` (worker, D-M3-28).
`Load` additionally errors when `Backend == "runpod"` and `PublicURL` is not `https://` (workers upload
to it), unless its host is loopback (tests). `dev` defaults `Backend=fake` (`dev --fake-provider` is
the design's spelling; the flag sets `MM_BACKEND=fake`).

**RunPod key files** (`internal/config/runpodkeys.go`; parsed by the binary, never by an agent):
```go
type RunPodKeys struct {
    Jobs, Read, Write string // RUNPOD_JOBS_KEY (required), RUNPOD_READ_KEY (required), RUNPOD_WRITE_KEY (optional)
    EndpointID        string // RUNPOD_ENDPOINT_ID (required; not secret, but private)
    ConsoleURL        string // RUNPOD_CONSOLE_URL (optional; https only; default "https://console.runpod.io/serverless"; C82)
}
func (k RunPodKeys) String() string   // "RunPodKeys{jobs:set read:set write:unset endpoint:set console:default}" — never a value
func (k RunPodKeys) GoString() string // same
func LoadRunPodKeys(dir string) (RunPodKeys, bool, error) // <dir>/runpod.env; (zero, false, nil) when absent
type AdminKey struct{ key string }    // String()/GoString() "AdminKey{set}"
func LoadAdminKey(path string) (AdminKey, error) // RUNPOD_ADMIN_KEY or RUNPOD_API_KEY from a dotenv file
```
Dotenv rules: `KEY=VALUE` lines, `#` comments, blank lines, optional `export `, optional single or double
quotes around the whole value, no interpolation, CRLF tolerated, BOM stripped. Unknown variables are
ignored. Errors name the file and the variable (`config: runpod.env: RUNPOD_READ_KEY missing`), never a
value; a duplicated variable is an error. **`runpod.env` must not contain an all-access key:**
`RUNPOD_API_KEY` or `RUNPOD_ADMIN_KEY` present in it ⇒ `LoadRunPodKeys` errors ("runpod.env must not
contain an all-access key; use runpod-admin.env with `metamusic runpod provision` only") and `serve`
refuses to start. The admin key is read **only by `runpod provision`** (never by `probe`, `arm`,
`status`, `canary` or `serve`) from `--admin-env FILE` (default `<SecretsDir>/runpod-admin.env`), which
accepts the variable names `RUNPOD_ADMIN_KEY` and `RUNPOD_API_KEY`, so the operator can point the CLI at
whatever key file they were given without anyone opening it. Tests never contain a `RUNPOD_*KEY=<value>`
literal: they build such lines at runtime from canary placeholders (M3-I2's gitleaks rule, C85).

Generation is **enabled** iff `Backend == "fake"`, or `Backend == "runpod"` and `runpod.env` loaded in
**this process** and the cached quote passes every fail-closed check of C77.5. "Enabled" is computed per
request from local key presence, `provider_state` (armed, the cached quote persisted by the reconciler
in `provider_state.quote_json`/`quote_at`) and the open-trip row — **never from the network**
(`mcp-stdio` reads the same rows; without keys it answers `keys_absent`). Otherwise every
estimate/create answers 503 `GENERATION_DISABLED` with `details.reason` ∈ `backend_none` |
`keys_absent` | `endpoint_inactive` (`workers.max == 0`) | `endpoint_config` (scaler, idle timeout,
`workers.max > 2` or another C77.5 check failed) | `quote_stale` (no quote yet, or older than
`QuoteMaxAge`) | `catalog_unreadable` (price missing or zero) | `sweep_unreadable` (the read key cannot
GET the endpoint or list workers), and everything else works. `serve` with `keys_absent` (or
`backend_none`) starts no runner and no reconciler.

**Derived keys** (no new secret file): `UploadKey = HMAC-SHA256(Secrets.Key, "metamusic/upload-url/v1")`
(upload and start-handshake URLs), `ConfirmKey = HMAC-SHA256(Secrets.Key, "metamusic/confirmation/v1")`,
`MeterKey = HMAC-SHA256(Secrets.Key, "metamusic/worker-id/v1")` (worker ids are stored only as
`worker_key`).

**Data layout additions** (all under the M1 `*os.Root` on `DataDir`):
```
<DataDir>/incoming/uploads/<upload_id>.part   upload in progress (0600, O_EXCL)
<DataDir>/incoming/uploads/<upload_id>        received upload (0400), consumed by ingest, removed after the take commits
<DataDir>/incoming/ingest-<track_id>/         M1 ingest work dir (unchanged)
```
Received uploads of jobs that ended without a take are removed by the reconciler 24 h after the job's
terminal state (never earlier, so an operator can inspect a mismatch). `incoming/` is never backed up
(backups copy only `audio/masters/`, `takes/` and `derived/`: `mediaRoots` in `internal/backup/snapshot.go`).

**Leaf packages:**
- `internal/money` (stdlib only):
  ```go
  type MicroUSD int64
  const PerUSD MicroUSD = 1_000_000
  func ForSeconds(seconds int64, perHour MicroUSD) MicroUSD // ceil(seconds × perHour / 3600); seconds < 0 ⇒ panic
  func ForMillis(ms int64, perHour MicroUSD) MicroUSD      // ceil(ms × perHour / 3_600_000)
  func ParseUSD(s string) (MicroUSD, error)                // exact decimal literal ("0.69", "1.5e-3", "12"); > 6 decimals ⇒ round UP; negative ⇒ error
  func ParseUSDSigned(s string) (MicroUSD, error)           // same, negatives allowed (billing adjustments)
  func WithMarginPPM(m MicroUSD, ppm int64) MicroUSD        // ceil(m × (1_000_000 + ppm) / 1_000_000) (D-M3-16)
  func (m MicroUSD) USD() string                            // exact: 326217 ⇒ "0.326217", 690000 ⇒ "0.69", 0 ⇒ "0", -5 ⇒ "-0.000005"
  func (m MicroUSD) Display() string                        // UI/ntfy: 0 ⇒ "$0.00"; "$0.33" (2 decimals, half-up) when |m| ≥ 10_000, else "$0.0058" (4 decimals, half-up)
  func (m MicroUSD) DisplayCeil() string                    // "up to" amounts (D-M3-29): same widths, rounded UP: 324999 ⇒ "$0.33", 691731 ⇒ "$0.70", 5801 ⇒ "$0.0059", 0 ⇒ "$0.00"
  ```
  Overflow guard: `ForSeconds`/`ForMillis`/`WithMarginPPM` use `math/bits.Mul64` and panic on overflow
  (unreachable for seconds ≤ 1e9 and prices ≤ 1e12 µUSD/h). No `float64` anywhere in the package. JSON
  amounts from RunPod (billing doubles, catalog prices) are decoded with `json.Decoder.UseNumber` and
  converted with `ParseUSD`/`ParseUSDSigned` **of the literal** — never through `float64`.
- `internal/clock` (stdlib only): `type Clock interface{ Now() time.Time; NewTimer(d time.Duration) Timer }`,
  `type Timer interface{ C() <-chan time.Time; Stop() bool }`, `clock.Real`. Test-only
  `internal/clock/clocktest.Manual` (`Advance(d)` fires due timers in order; added to
  `TestNoTestHelpersInBinary`). **One manual clock:** M2a's `servertest.Clock` (a struct with `Now`, `Advance` and
  `Set`, built by `servertest.NewClock(t)`) becomes a type alias of `clocktest.Manual` (M3-B1 amends M2a
  C42); `Manual` implements `clock.Clock` and keeps `Now`, `Advance`, `Set`, and `NewClock` returns it, so `Rig.Clock.Advance` fires the timers of the runner, the reconciler and `Wait` (its 1 s
  recheck and its timeout) as well as M2a's users.
- `internal/store` gains `FormatTime(t time.Time) string` (`2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z`) and
  `ParseTime(string) (time.Time, error)`; M3 code never formats SQL-compared timestamps any other way
  (D-M3-23).

### C69. Migration `internal/store/migrations/0007_generate.sql` (full DDL) and write SQL

After M3, `PRAGMA user_version == 7` (`0006_listen_detail.sql`, the auto-advance hotfix, is the previous migration; the original M3 number 0006 was taken on main). Two tests
still hard-code the version (`internal/httpserver/server_test.go`: healthz `schema_version` 5, and
`internal/store/schema_m2c_test.go`: `v != 5`); M3-B1 changes both to compare with `len(migrations)` /
`>= 5` so a later migration never touches them again (everything else already compares with
`len(migrations)`). As before, `CHECK` constraints encode only invariants that never
change; growing enumerations (engines, backends, operators, ledger reasons, event names, error codes,
artifact names, approval channels) are validated in Go.

```sql
-- 0007_generate.sql — M3: caps, estimates, batches, approvals, jobs, attempts, events, uploads,
-- ledger, billing windows and buckets, worker meter, recipe keys, env fingerprints, RTF stats,
-- runner lease, provider state.

-- Per-token spend limits (NULL ⇒ the global defaults in spend_caps). Set only by set_spend_caps.
ALTER TABLE api_tokens ADD COLUMN daily_budget_micro_usd INTEGER
  CHECK (daily_budget_micro_usd IS NULL OR daily_budget_micro_usd >= 0);
ALTER TABLE api_tokens ADD COLUMN auto_approve_takes INTEGER
  CHECK (auto_approve_takes IS NULL OR auto_approve_takes BETWEEN 0 AND 100);
ALTER TABLE api_tokens ADD COLUMN auto_approve_micro_usd INTEGER
  CHECK (auto_approve_micro_usd IS NULL OR auto_approve_micro_usd >= 0);

-- Generation provenance on tracks and Operator Ledger rows (set once, at insert).
ALTER TABLE tracks    ADD COLUMN job_id TEXT REFERENCES jobs(id);
ALTER TABLE tracks    ADD COLUMN env_id TEXT REFERENCES env_fingerprints(env_id);
CREATE TRIGGER tracks_generation_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF job_id, env_id ON tracks
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'tracks: frozen column'); END;
CREATE INDEX tracks_job ON tracks(job_id);
ALTER TABLE mutations ADD COLUMN job_id TEXT REFERENCES jobs(id);
ALTER TABLE mutations ADD COLUMN recipe_patch_json TEXT
  CHECK (recipe_patch_json IS NULL OR json_valid(recipe_patch_json));
-- mutations_immutable (M1) already covers every column.

-- Job recipes carry unique request ids (D-M3-10); imported recipes may legitimately repeat one.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX recipes_request_id_generated ON recipes(request_id) WHERE created_by <> 'import';

CREATE TABLE recipe_keys (                               -- D-M3-11; one row per recipe, computed in Go
  recipe_id    TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES recipes(id),
  content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- sha256 canonical request minus id and seed
  render_hash  TEXT NOT NULL                             -- sha256 canonical request minus id
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE INDEX recipe_keys_content ON recipe_keys(content_hash);
CREATE INDEX recipe_keys_render  ON recipe_keys(render_hash);
CREATE TRIGGER recipe_keys_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON recipe_keys
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'recipe_keys: immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER recipe_keys_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON recipe_keys
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'recipe_keys: no physical delete'); END;

CREATE TABLE spend_caps (                                -- singleton; D-M3-7 defaults
  id                     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
  monthly_micro_usd      INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (monthly_micro_usd >= 0),
  batch_micro_usd        INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (batch_micro_usd >= 0),
  human_daily_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (human_daily_micro_usd >= 0),
  agent_daily_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (agent_daily_micro_usd >= 0),
  auto_approve_takes     INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (auto_approve_takes BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  auto_approve_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (auto_approve_micro_usd >= 0),
  updated_by             TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at             TEXT NOT NULL
);
INSERT INTO spend_caps VALUES (1, 10000000, 5000000, 5000000, 1000000, 5, 500000,
                               'system:migration', '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
CREATE TRIGGER spend_caps_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON spend_caps
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'spend_caps: no physical delete'); END;

CREATE TABLE spend_cap_changes (                         -- append-only history of every cap change
  id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  principal_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  target      TEXT NOT NULL,                             -- 'global' | 'token:<id>'
  before_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(before_json)),
  after_json  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(after_json))
);
CREATE TRIGGER spend_cap_changes_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON spend_cap_changes
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'spend_cap_changes: append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER spend_cap_changes_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON spend_cap_changes
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'spend_cap_changes: append-only'); END;

CREATE TABLE estimates (
  id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                  -- UUIDv7
  principal_id        TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- Principal.Key()
  principal_kind      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (principal_kind IN ('human','agent')),
  engine              TEXT NOT NULL,
  backend             TEXT NOT NULL,
  items_json          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(items_json)),          -- the expanded plan (C71.4)
  recipe_hashes       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(recipe_hashes) AND json_type(recipe_hashes) = 'array'),
  n_takes             INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (n_takes BETWEEN 1 AND 100),    -- outer bound; Go enforces MaxTakes = 50 (D-M3-7)
  quote_json          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(quote_json)),          -- C72.1 inputs and per-take costs
  expected_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (expected_micro_usd >= 0),
  max_micro_usd       INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (max_micro_usd >= expected_micro_usd),
  retry_allowance_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (retry_allowance_micro_usd >= 0),
  ceiling_micro_usd   INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ceiling_micro_usd = max_micro_usd + retry_allowance_micro_usd), -- D-M3-29
  price_pph_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_pph_micro_usd > 0),    -- list price × margin (C72.1)
  workers_max         INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (workers_max >= 1),             -- frozen quote inputs (C77.5, C75.3)
  idle_s              INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (idle_s >= 1),
  requires_approval   INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (requires_approval IN (0,1)),
  approval_reasons    TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(approval_reasons)),
  token_hash          TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,              -- hex HMAC-SHA256(ConfirmKey, token)
  created_at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  expires_at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  consumed_at         TEXT,
  consumed_batch_id   TEXT,
  CHECK ((consumed_at IS NULL) = (consumed_batch_id IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX estimates_principal ON estimates(principal_id, created_at);
CREATE TRIGGER estimates_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON estimates
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'estimates: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER estimates_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF id, principal_id, principal_kind, engine, backend,
  items_json, recipe_hashes, n_takes, quote_json, expected_micro_usd, max_micro_usd,
  retry_allowance_micro_usd, ceiling_micro_usd, price_pph_micro_usd, workers_max, idle_s,
  requires_approval, approval_reasons, token_hash, created_at, expires_at ON estimates
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'estimates: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER estimates_single_use BEFORE UPDATE OF consumed_at, consumed_batch_id ON estimates
WHEN OLD.consumed_at IS NOT NULL
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'estimates: already consumed'); END;

CREATE TABLE batches (
  id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                  -- UUIDv7
  estimate_id         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES estimates(id),
  principal_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
  principal_kind      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (principal_kind IN ('human','agent')),
  principal_name      TEXT NOT NULL,
  via                 TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- Go-validated: api | human_create
  engine              TEXT NOT NULL,
  backend             TEXT NOT NULL,
  n_takes             INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (n_takes BETWEEN 1 AND 100),    -- outer bound; Go enforces 50
  rationale           TEXT CHECK (rationale IS NULL OR length(rationale) BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
  expected_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (expected_micro_usd >= 0),
  max_micro_usd       INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (max_micro_usd >= expected_micro_usd),
  retry_allowance_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (retry_allowance_micro_usd >= 0),
  ceiling_micro_usd   INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ceiling_micro_usd = max_micro_usd + retry_allowance_micro_usd), -- D-M3-29
  price_pph_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_pph_micro_usd > 0),
  attempt_overhead_s  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (attempt_overhead_s >= 0),     -- ColdStartS + IdleS at estimate time
  kill_slack_s        INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (kill_slack_s >= 0),
  workers_max         INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (workers_max >= 1),             -- frozen; in-flight ≤ min(live, this) (D-M3-14)
  idle_s              INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (idle_s >= 1),                  -- frozen; claims reserve max(live, this) (C75.3)
  state               TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('awaiting_confirmation','active','finished','rejected','expired')),
  created_at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  finished_at         TEXT,
  reconciled_at       TEXT,                              -- residual released (C76.5)
  CHECK ((state IN ('finished','rejected','expired')) = (finished_at IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (reconciled_at IS NULL OR finished_at IS NOT NULL)
);
CREATE INDEX batches_state   ON batches(state, created_at);
CREATE INDEX batches_open    ON batches(created_at) WHERE reconciled_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX batches_principal ON batches(principal_id, created_at);
CREATE TRIGGER batches_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON batches
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'batches: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER batches_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF id, estimate_id, principal_id, principal_kind,
  principal_name, via, engine, backend, n_takes, rationale, expected_micro_usd, max_micro_usd,
  retry_allowance_micro_usd, ceiling_micro_usd, price_pph_micro_usd, attempt_overhead_s, kill_slack_s,
  workers_max, idle_s, created_at ON batches
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'batches: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER batches_final BEFORE UPDATE OF state, finished_at ON batches
WHEN OLD.state IN ('finished','rejected','expired')
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'batches: finished batches are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER batches_reconciled_once BEFORE UPDATE OF reconciled_at ON batches
WHEN OLD.reconciled_at IS NOT NULL
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'batches: already reconciled'); END;

CREATE TABLE approvals (
  batch_id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES batches(id),
  state             TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('pending','approved','rejected','expired','auto')),
  reasons           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(reasons)),   -- why approval was (not) needed (C72.6)
  via               TEXT,                                -- Go-validated: auto | human_create | web | expiry
  decided_by        TEXT,                                -- Principal.Key(); 'system:reconciler' for expiry
  decided_at        TEXT,
  reason            TEXT CHECK (reason IS NULL OR length(reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 500),
  requested_count   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (requested_count >= 0),
  last_requested_at TEXT,
  created_at        TEXT NOT NULL,
  expires_at        TEXT NOT NULL,
  CHECK ((state = 'pending') = (decided_at IS NULL)),
  CHECK ((decided_at IS NULL) = (via IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX approvals_pending ON approvals(expires_at) WHERE state = 'pending';
CREATE TRIGGER approvals_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON approvals
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'approvals: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER approvals_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF batch_id, reasons, created_at, expires_at ON approvals
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'approvals: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER approvals_decided BEFORE UPDATE OF state, via, decided_by, decided_at, reason ON approvals
WHEN OLD.state <> 'pending'
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'approvals: decided approvals are immutable'); END;

CREATE TABLE job_transitions (                           -- D-M3-3; equals jobs.Transitions (Go)
  from_state TEXT NOT NULL,
  to_state   TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (from_state, to_state)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
INSERT INTO job_transitions(from_state, to_state) VALUES
  ('awaiting_confirmation','queued'), ('awaiting_confirmation','cancelled'), ('awaiting_confirmation','expired'),
  ('queued','submitting'), ('queued','starting'), ('queued','cancelled'), ('queued','cancelling'),
  ('queued','failed'), ('queued','transferring'),
  ('submitting','starting'), ('submitting','queued'), ('submitting','failed'), ('submitting','cancelling'),
  ('submitting','transferring'),
  ('starting','generating'), ('starting','queued'), ('starting','transferring'), ('starting','cancelling'),
  ('starting','failing'), ('starting','failed'),
  ('generating','transferring'), ('generating','cancelling'), ('generating','failing'), ('generating','failed'),
  ('transferring','ingesting'), ('transferring','failed'), ('transferring','failing'), ('transferring','cancelling'),
  ('ingesting','completed'), ('ingesting','failed'),
  ('cancelling','cancelled'), ('cancelling','transferring'),
  ('failing','failed'), ('failing','transferring');
CREATE TRIGGER job_transitions_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON job_transitions
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_transitions: immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_transitions_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON job_transitions
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_transitions: immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_transitions_no_insert BEFORE INSERT ON job_transitions   -- created after the 34 rows above
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_transitions: immutable'); END;

CREATE TABLE jobs (
  id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                  -- UUIDv7 = take id
  batch_id            TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES batches(id),
  take_index          INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (take_index >= 0),
  kind                TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- Go-validated: 'generate' (D-M3-15)
  engine              TEXT NOT NULL,
  backend             TEXT NOT NULL,
  recipe_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES recipes(id),
  source_track_id     TEXT REFERENCES tracks(id),        -- parent; NULL for compose_new and motherless drafts
  draft_id            TEXT REFERENCES recipe_drafts(id),
  operator            TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- Go-validated (C71.2)
  mutation_json       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(mutation_json)),  -- {operator, params, recipe_patch, rationale}
  title               TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(title) BETWEEN 1 AND 200),
  expected_audio_s    INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (expected_audio_s BETWEEN 1 AND 360),
  expected_s          INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (expected_s > 0),
  timeout_s           INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (timeout_s BETWEEN 1 AND 2700),
  expected_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (expected_micro_usd >= 0),
  reserved_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (reserved_micro_usd > 0),   -- the `take` reserve at create (timeout_s + KillSlackS)
  state               TEXT NOT NULL,
  outcome             TEXT CHECK (outcome IS NULL OR outcome IN ('clean','truncated')),
  error_code          TEXT,
  cancel_requested_at TEXT,
  cancel_requested_by TEXT,
  lease_owner         TEXT,
  lease_expires_at    TEXT,
  next_attempt_at     TEXT NOT NULL,
  queued_at           TEXT,                              -- first entry into 'queued' (anchor of NOT_SUBMITTED, C75.2 row 6)
  first_submitted_at  TEXT,                              -- anchor of the 24 h resubmit window
  attempts            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (attempts >= 0),
  resubmits           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (resubmits >= 0),
  queue_expiries      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (queue_expiries >= 0),   -- ≤ MaxQueueExpiryResubmits (6)
  state_deadline_at   TEXT,
  started_at          TEXT,                              -- first Running or start announcement of any attempt
  finished_at         TEXT,
  track_id            TEXT REFERENCES tracks(id),
  env_id              TEXT REFERENCES env_fingerprints(env_id),
  audio_s             REAL CHECK (audio_s IS NULL OR audio_s > 0),
  truncated_abc       INTEGER CHECK (truncated_abc IN (0,1)),
  truncated_semantic  INTEGER CHECK (truncated_semantic IN (0,1)),
  created_at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (batch_id, take_index),
  CHECK ((state = 'completed') = (outcome IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK (state <> 'completed' OR track_id IS NOT NULL),
  CHECK ((state IN ('completed','failed','cancelled','expired')) = (finished_at IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((cancel_requested_at IS NULL) = (cancel_requested_by IS NULL)),
  CHECK ((lease_owner IS NULL) = (lease_expires_at IS NULL)),
  CHECK (state NOT IN ('awaiting_confirmation','queued','completed','failed','cancelled','expired')
         OR lease_owner IS NULL)                                 -- unleased states never hold a job lease (C69)
);
CREATE INDEX jobs_queue  ON jobs(state, next_attempt_at);
CREATE INDEX jobs_lease  ON jobs(lease_owner, lease_expires_at);
CREATE INDEX jobs_batch  ON jobs(batch_id, take_index);
CREATE INDEX jobs_track  ON jobs(track_id);
CREATE INDEX jobs_source ON jobs(source_track_id);
CREATE INDEX jobs_draft  ON jobs(draft_id);
CREATE TRIGGER jobs_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON jobs
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'jobs: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER jobs_initial_state BEFORE INSERT ON jobs
WHEN NEW.state NOT IN ('awaiting_confirmation','queued')
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'jobs: illegal initial state'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER jobs_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF id, batch_id, take_index, kind, engine, backend, recipe_id,
  source_track_id, draft_id, operator, mutation_json, title, expected_audio_s, expected_s, timeout_s,
  expected_micro_usd, reserved_micro_usd, created_at ON jobs
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'jobs: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER jobs_transition BEFORE UPDATE OF state ON jobs
WHEN NEW.state <> OLD.state AND NOT EXISTS
  (SELECT 1 FROM job_transitions t WHERE t.from_state = OLD.state AND t.to_state = NEW.state)
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'jobs: illegal transition'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER jobs_final BEFORE UPDATE ON jobs
WHEN OLD.state IN ('completed','failed','cancelled','expired')
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'jobs: terminal jobs are immutable'); END;

CREATE TABLE job_attempts (                              -- D-M3-2/D-M3-4
  id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                  -- UUIDv7
  job_id              TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id),
  n                   INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (n >= 1),
  backend             TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- = jobs.backend (D-M3-14 counts per backend)
  state               TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('pending','submitted','unknown','rejected','done')),
  ref                 TEXT,                              -- provider job id; never returned by the API
  submitted_at        TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- when the row was written (before /run)
  window_ends_at      TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- = upload exp (C74.1): the attempt cannot spend after it
  price_pph_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_pph_micro_usd > 0),
  image_digest        TEXT,                              -- from the worker that ran it (D-M3-26)
  worker_key          TEXT,                              -- hex HMAC of the status's workerId (C76.6); never the raw id
  remote_status       TEXT,                              -- last observed (C73.2)
  in_progress_seen    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (in_progress_seen IN (0,1)),  -- observation only; never classifies (C73.2)
  requeue_seen        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (requeue_seen IN (0,1)),      -- Running, then Queued again
  last_queued_at      TEXT,                              -- last poll that answered Queued (C73.2 404 rule)
  announced_at        TEXT,                              -- start handshake received (D-M3-28)
  announced_ref       TEXT,                              -- provider id from the start body; promoted to ref by the runner
  execution_ms        INTEGER CHECK (execution_ms IS NULL OR execution_ms >= 0),
  terminal_basis      TEXT CHECK (terminal_basis IS NULL OR terminal_basis IN ('provider','time_bound','not_accepted')),
  terminal_at         TEXT,
  settled             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (settled IN (0,1)),
  created_at          TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (job_id, n),
  CHECK ((state = 'submitted') <= (ref IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((state IN ('pending','rejected')) <= (ref IS NULL)),     -- 'unknown' may carry a ref (status lost, C73.2)
  CHECK ((state IN ('done','rejected')) = (terminal_basis IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((terminal_basis IS NULL) = (terminal_at IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX job_attempts_live ON job_attempts(backend, state) WHERE state IN ('pending','submitted','unknown');
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON job_attempts
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF id, job_id, n, backend, submitted_at, window_ends_at,
  price_pph_micro_usd, created_at ON job_attempts
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_ref_once BEFORE UPDATE OF ref ON job_attempts
WHEN OLD.ref IS NOT NULL
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: ref is set once'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_transition BEFORE UPDATE OF state ON job_attempts   -- legal attempt moves
WHEN NEW.state <> OLD.state AND NOT (
     (OLD.state = 'pending'   AND NEW.state IN ('submitted','unknown','rejected'))
  OR (OLD.state = 'unknown'   AND NEW.state = 'submitted' AND OLD.ref IS NULL)  -- late ref via start (D-M3-28)
  OR (OLD.state = 'unknown'   AND NEW.state = 'done')
  OR (OLD.state = 'submitted' AND NEW.state IN ('unknown','done')))             -- 'unknown': status lost (404)
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: illegal transition'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_announced_once BEFORE UPDATE OF announced_ref ON job_attempts
WHEN OLD.announced_ref IS NOT NULL AND NEW.announced_ref IS NOT OLD.announced_ref
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: announced_ref is set once'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_final BEFORE UPDATE OF state, remote_status, in_progress_seen, requeue_seen,
  last_queued_at, announced_at, announced_ref, execution_ms, terminal_basis, terminal_at, image_digest,
  worker_key ON job_attempts
WHEN OLD.state IN ('done','rejected')
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: terminal attempts are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_attempts_settled_once BEFORE UPDATE OF settled ON job_attempts
WHEN OLD.settled = 1
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_attempts: already settled'); END;

CREATE TABLE job_events (                                -- append-only; never URLs, refs or keys (C75.7)
  id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  job_id     TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id),
  attempt_id TEXT REFERENCES job_attempts(id),
  at         TEXT NOT NULL,
  from_state TEXT,
  to_state   TEXT,
  event      TEXT NOT NULL,                              -- Go-validated (C75.7)
  code       TEXT,
  detail_json TEXT CHECK (detail_json IS NULL OR json_valid(detail_json))  -- redacted, ≤ 2 KiB
);
CREATE INDEX job_events_job ON job_events(job_id, id);
CREATE TRIGGER job_events_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON job_events
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_events: append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER job_events_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON job_events
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'job_events: append-only'); END;

CREATE TABLE uploads (                                   -- design §3.4; URLs are never persisted
  id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                          -- upload_id (UUIDv7)
  job_id      TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id),
  attempt_id  TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES job_attempts(id),
  artifact    TEXT NOT NULL,                             -- Go-validated allowlist (C73.4)
  max_bytes   INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (max_bytes > 0),
  exp         INTEGER NOT NULL,                          -- unix seconds
  state       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','received','consumed','closed')),
  sha256      TEXT,
  bytes       INTEGER CHECK (bytes IS NULL OR bytes >= 0),
  received_at TEXT,
  created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (attempt_id, artifact),
  CHECK ((state IN ('received','consumed')) = (sha256 IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((sha256 IS NULL) = (bytes IS NULL)),
  CHECK ((sha256 IS NULL) = (received_at IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX uploads_job ON uploads(job_id, attempt_id);
CREATE TRIGGER uploads_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON uploads
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'uploads: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER uploads_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF id, job_id, attempt_id, artifact, max_bytes, exp, created_at ON uploads
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'uploads: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER uploads_received_once BEFORE UPDATE OF sha256, bytes, received_at ON uploads
WHEN OLD.sha256 IS NOT NULL
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'uploads: received uploads are immutable'); END;

CREATE TABLE ledger (                                    -- append-only; integer micro-USD (C72.5)
  id           INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  at           TEXT NOT NULL,
  kind         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('reserve','settle','residual','release','bill')),
  micro_usd    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  batch_id     TEXT REFERENCES batches(id),
  job_id       TEXT REFERENCES jobs(id),
  attempt_id   TEXT REFERENCES job_attempts(id),
  window_start TEXT,                                     -- 'bill' rows: start of the reconciled window
  principal_id TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- batch owner's Key(); 'system:billing' for 'bill'
  reason       TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- Go-validated (C72.5)
  CHECK (kind = 'bill' OR micro_usd >= 0),
  CHECK (kind <> 'reserve' OR micro_usd > 0),
  CHECK ((kind = 'bill') = (window_start IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((kind = 'bill') = (batch_id IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX ledger_batch     ON ledger(batch_id, kind);
CREATE INDEX ledger_job       ON ledger(job_id);
CREATE INDEX ledger_at        ON ledger(at);
CREATE INDEX ledger_principal ON ledger(principal_id, at);
CREATE TRIGGER ledger_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON ledger
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'ledger: append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER ledger_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON ledger
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'ledger: append-only'); END;

CREATE TABLE billing_windows (                           -- one row per reconciled quiet window (C76.5)
  window_start           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  window_end             TEXT NOT NULL,                  -- never in a later calendar month than window_start (C76.5)
  granularity            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (granularity = 'hour'),   -- always queried with bucketSize=hour
  buckets                INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (buckets >= 1),
  billed_micro_usd       INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (billed_micro_usd >= 0),        -- Σ totalAmount (real spend, incl. disk)
  billed_compute_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (billed_compute_micro_usd >= 0), -- Σ gpuAmount + cpuAmount (trips use this)
  provisional_micro_usd  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (provisional_micro_usd >= 0),
  reserved_micro_usd     INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (reserved_micro_usd >= 0),
  batches                INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (batches >= 0),
  verdict                TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (verdict IN ('ok','billed_exceeds_reserved','spend_without_jobs')),
  fetched_at             TEXT NOT NULL,
  CHECK (window_end > window_start)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TRIGGER billing_windows_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON billing_windows
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'billing_windows: immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER billing_windows_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON billing_windows
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'billing_windows: immutable'); END;

CREATE TABLE billing_buckets (                           -- every fetched observation of an hourly bucket (C76.5)
  bucket_start      TEXT NOT NULL,
```
