```sql
  fetched_at        TEXT NOT NULL,
  total_micro_usd   INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (total_micro_usd >= 0),     -- totalAmount, ParseUSD of the literal (round up)
  compute_micro_usd INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (compute_micro_usd >= 0),   -- gpuAmount + cpuAmount
  disk_micro_usd    INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (disk_micro_usd >= 0),      -- recorded; never tripped on
  PRIMARY KEY (bucket_start, fetched_at)                 -- window membership follows billing_windows ranges
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TRIGGER billing_buckets_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON billing_buckets
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'billing_buckets: append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER billing_buckets_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON billing_buckets
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'billing_buckets: append-only'); END;

CREATE TABLE worker_meter (                              -- C76.6; one row per observed worker lifetime
  worker_key    TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- hex HMAC-SHA256(MeterKey, worker id); never the raw id
  started_at    TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- the worker record's startedAt
  first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  last_seen_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
  uptime_s      INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (uptime_s >= 0),  -- max uptimeSeconds observed (NULL uptime ⇒ not inserted)
  status_last   TEXT NOT NULL,
  image         TEXT,                                    -- the record's image (D-M3-26)
  PRIMARY KEY (worker_key, started_at)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TRIGGER worker_meter_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON worker_meter
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'worker_meter: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER worker_meter_monotonic BEFORE UPDATE OF uptime_s ON worker_meter
WHEN NEW.uptime_s < OLD.uptime_s
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'worker_meter: uptime never decreases'); END;

CREATE TABLE env_fingerprints (                          -- D-M3-26
  env_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  engine        TEXT NOT NULL,
  pkg_version   TEXT NOT NULL,
  code_commit   TEXT NOT NULL,
  weights_json  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(weights_json)),
  decoder       TEXT NOT NULL,
  backend       TEXT NOT NULL,
  gpu_class     TEXT NOT NULL,
  image_digest  TEXT NOT NULL,
  detail_json   TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(detail_json)),   -- first seen: gpu_name, cuda, torch, worker_build, python
  first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TRIGGER env_fingerprints_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON env_fingerprints
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'env_fingerprints: immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER env_fingerprints_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON env_fingerprints
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'env_fingerprints: no physical delete'); END;

CREATE TABLE rtf_stats (                                 -- real-time factor × 1000 per GPU class (C72.1)
  gpu_class  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  rtf_milli  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (rtf_milli BETWEEN 50 AND 5000),
  samples    INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (samples >= 0),
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
) WITHOUT ROWID;
INSERT INTO rtf_stats VALUES ('A5000', 440, 0, '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
                             ('RTX 3090', 440, 0, '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
                             ('RTX 4090', 330, 0, '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
CREATE TRIGGER rtf_stats_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON rtf_stats
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'rtf_stats: no physical delete'); END;

CREATE TABLE runner_lease (                              -- design §3.2 singleton
  id           INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
  owner        TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- random instance id (never a hostname)
  acquired_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
  heartbeat_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  expires_at   TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TRIGGER runner_lease_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON runner_lease
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'runner_lease: no physical delete'); END;

CREATE TABLE provider_state (                            -- one row per backend name (D-M3-8)
  backend    TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  armed      INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (armed IN (0,1)),
  changed_by TEXT NOT NULL,                              -- 'system:default' on first start
  changed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  reason     TEXT CHECK (reason IS NULL OR length(reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 500),
  quote_json TEXT CHECK (quote_json IS NULL OR json_valid(quote_json)),   -- cached provider.Quote (C77.5); never secrets
  quote_at   TEXT,                                       -- when quote_json was observed
  restored_at TEXT,                                      -- set by restore (C76.7): re-arm needs a window_end ≥ this
  CHECK ((quote_json IS NULL) = (quote_at IS NULL))
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TRIGGER provider_state_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON provider_state
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'provider_state: no physical delete'); END;

CREATE TABLE provider_trips (                            -- append-only history; open trip = reset_at IS NULL
  id           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                         -- UUIDv7
  backend      TEXT NOT NULL,
  tripped_at   TEXT NOT NULL,
  reason       TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- Go-validated (C76.3)
  details_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(details_json)),   -- redacted
  actions_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(actions_json)),   -- what the reconciler did
  reset_at     TEXT,
  reset_by     TEXT,
  reset_reason TEXT CHECK (reset_reason IS NULL OR length(reset_reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 500),
  CHECK ((reset_at IS NULL) = (reset_by IS NULL)),
  CHECK ((reset_at IS NULL) = (reset_reason IS NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX provider_trips_one_open ON provider_trips(backend) WHERE reset_at IS NULL;
CREATE TRIGGER provider_trips_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON provider_trips
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'provider_trips: no physical delete'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER provider_trips_frozen BEFORE UPDATE OF id, backend, tripped_at, reason, details_json, actions_json ON provider_trips
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'provider_trips: frozen column'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER provider_trips_reset_once BEFORE UPDATE OF reset_at, reset_by, reset_reason ON provider_trips
WHEN OLD.reset_at IS NOT NULL
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'provider_trips: already reset'); END;
```

**Write SQL (binding; services implement exactly these forms):**
- Runner lease acquire (one statement; a returned row = acquired):
  ```sql
  INSERT INTO runner_lease(id, owner, acquired_at, heartbeat_at, expires_at) VALUES (1, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET owner=excluded.owner, acquired_at=excluded.acquired_at,
    heartbeat_at=excluded.heartbeat_at, expires_at=excluded.expires_at
  WHERE runner_lease.expires_at <= excluded.heartbeat_at OR runner_lease.owner = excluded.owner
  RETURNING owner
  ```
  Heartbeat (its own goroutine, never behind `Step`, C75.3): `UPDATE runner_lease SET heartbeat_at=?,
  expires_at=? WHERE id=1 AND owner=? AND expires_at > ?` (0 rows ⇒ lease lost: stop claiming, stop
  reconciling, abandon in-flight network work). Graceful release: `UPDATE runner_lease SET expires_at=?
  WHERE id=1 AND owner=?` with `expires_at = now`. Lease length 90 s, heartbeat every 30 s.
- **Lease predicate (D-M3-30; binding on every runner and reconciler write, `TestSQL_LeasePredicate`
  greps the write statements of `internal/jobs`):** `AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM runner_lease WHERE id = 1
  AND owner = ?owner AND expires_at > ?now)`, rendered by `store.LeaseFence{Owner string; Now
  time.Time}` (M3-B1, `internal/store/lease.go`), which carries the arguments into `spend`, `upload` and
  `jobs`. `INSERT`s take the form `INSERT … SELECT … WHERE EXISTS (…)`; a statement that affects 0 rows
  because of the predicate ⇒ `ErrLeaseLost`, the transaction rolls
  back and the runner stops. A transaction that will be followed by a network call additionally checks
  `expires_at > now + 30 s` and refuses otherwise (`ErrLeaseShort`; the heartbeat renews first).
- Job claim (inside `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; only when the provider of the **active backend** is armed and not
  tripped, the cached quote is fresh (C77.5) and the preflight passed (D-M3-33)):
  ```sql
  UPDATE jobs SET state='submitting', lease_owner=?, lease_expires_at=?, updated_at=?
   WHERE id = (SELECT j.id FROM jobs j JOIN batches b ON b.id = j.batch_id
                WHERE j.state = 'queued' AND j.backend = ?backend AND j.next_attempt_at <= ?now
                  AND j.cancel_requested_at IS NULL
                  AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM job_attempts a WHERE a.backend = ?backend
                        AND a.state IN ('pending','submitted','unknown')) < min(?liveWorkersMax, b.workers_max)
                ORDER BY b.created_at, j.take_index LIMIT 1)
     AND state = 'queued'
     AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM runner_lease WHERE id = 1 AND owner = ?owner AND expires_at > ?now)
  RETURNING id, batch_id
  ```
  (A queued job may still have a live `unknown` ghost: its `topup_ambiguous` was reserved in the
  transaction that requeued it, and the ghost counts toward the in-flight limit.) The claim is
  followed in the same transaction by the `attempt_overhead` reserve through `spend.ReserveTopUp`
  (caps + batch ceiling, C72.3; refused ⇒ the whole transaction rolls back and a second transaction
  fails the job `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` via row 6), the attempt insert (`state='pending'`, `backend`), its
  `uploads` rows (`state='open'`), `attempts = attempts + 1`, `first_submitted_at =
  COALESCE(first_submitted_at, ?)`, and a `job_events` row `claim` — every statement with the lease
  predicate.
- Job lease renewal (every heartbeat): `UPDATE jobs SET lease_expires_at=? WHERE lease_owner=? AND state
  NOT IN ('awaiting_confirmation','queued','completed','failed','cancelled','expired') AND EXISTS (lease)`.
- Adoption (lease predicate): `UPDATE jobs SET lease_owner=?, lease_expires_at=?, updated_at=? WHERE
  state IN ('submitting','starting','generating','transferring','ingesting','cancelling','failing') AND
  (lease_expires_at IS NULL OR lease_expires_at <= ?) AND EXISTS (lease) RETURNING id, state`.
- Every state change of a **leased** job (`submitting` … `failing`): `UPDATE jobs SET state=?, … ,
  updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND state=? AND lease_owner=? AND EXISTS (lease)`. Every state change of an
  **unleased** job (`awaiting_confirmation`, `queued`): `… WHERE id=? AND state=? AND lease_owner IS NULL
  AND EXISTS (lease)`. Every transition **to** `queued` or to a terminal state sets `lease_owner = NULL,
  lease_expires_at = NULL` in the same statement (enforced by the `jobs` CHECK). 0 rows ⇒ another writer
  won or the lease is gone; re-read and re-plan, never overwrite. Plus one `job_events` row in the same
  transaction.
- Cancel intent (ops): `UPDATE jobs SET cancel_requested_at=?, cancel_requested_by=? WHERE id=? AND
  cancel_requested_at IS NULL AND state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancelled','expired')`.
- Token consumption: `UPDATE estimates SET consumed_at=?, consumed_batch_id=? WHERE token_hash=? AND
  consumed_at IS NULL AND expires_at > ? RETURNING id` (0 rows ⇒ re-read to tell
  `CONFIRMATION_INVALID` from `CONFIRMATION_EXPIRED`; a consumed token of the same principal returns
  `details.batch_id = consumed_batch_id`, C72.4).
- Draft fulfilment by a job take: `UPDATE recipe_drafts SET status='fulfilled',
  fulfilled_by_track_id=?, updated_by='system:job', updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND status='backlog'` (any
  revision: the estimate pinned the revision and `update_draft` refuses drafts with a live job, C81.1).
  This is the **only** draft-fulfilment path for job takes (`library.InsertTake` skips M1's importer
  fulfilment for `origin='job'`).
- Worker meter upsert (lease predicate): `INSERT INTO worker_meter(…) SELECT ?, …, ? WHERE EXISTS (lease)
  ON CONFLICT(worker_key, started_at) DO UPDATE SET uptime_s = max(uptime_s, excluded.uptime_s),
  last_seen_at = excluded.last_seen_at, status_last = excluded.status_last, image =
  COALESCE(excluded.image, image)`.
- Ledger, approvals, uploads, billing: exact forms in C72.5, C72.6, C74.2, C76.5.
- **M2c interplay.** The 0005 triggers stay in force: `tracks_reference_rule` and `tracks_lossy_shape` pass a
  job take (`import_rule='job'`, `lossy` default 0, `origin='job'`); `tracks_reference_lineage` and
  `recipe_drafts_ref_parent_*` make a reference mother impossible even for a buggy writer (D-M3-34).
  `recipe_keys` holds rows for `engine = 'yue2'` recipes only (stubs get none).
  `recipes_request_id_generated` is unaffected by stubs (`created_by = 'import'`).
- M1/M2a rules hold: no `INSERT OR REPLACE`/`REPLACE INTO` (`TestNoReplaceSQL`), no frozen column in
  any SET list, and `TestNoDeleteSQL`'s allowlist stays `{idempotency, human_sessions}`. Every write path
  above has a run-it-twice test.

### C70. Engines: `internal/engine` and `engine/yue2.Engine`

```go
package engine // imports no engine implementation (not internal/engine/yue2, not engine/registry)
type Caps struct {
    Text2Song, PlanScore, RenderFromScore, RepaintInterval, Stems, ReferenceAudio, LoRA bool
    MaxSeconds, SampleRate int
}
type Issue struct { // engine-neutral; mirrors yue2.Issue field for field
    Code, Field, Severity, Message string // Severity "error" | "warning"
    Line int; Section string; Fatal bool
}
type Engine interface {
    Name() string                                            // "yue2"
    Caps() Caps
    Validate(request json.RawMessage) []Issue                // exactly what the engine's own batch tooling accepts (ModeYuE2)
    ExpectedAudioSeconds(request json.RawMessage) (int, error) // whole seconds, clamped to [30, Caps().MaxSeconds]
    Artifacts(request json.RawMessage) (required, optional []string) // upload allowlist for one take (C73.4)
}
type Registry map[string]Engine

package registry // internal/engine/registry: the only package importing both engine and its implementations
func Default() engine.Registry // {"yue2": yue2.Engine{}}  (yue2 = internal/engine/yue2)
```
`internal/engine/yue2` (M1's existing domain package, package `yue2`; the new file `engine.go` adds the
type `yue2.Engine`, which imports `engine`, never the reverse): `Caps{Text2Song: true, PlanScore: true, RenderFromScore: true, MaxSeconds: 360, SampleRate:
48000}`; `Validate` = `yue2.CheckRequest(raw, ModeYuE2, nil)` filtered to errors and converted field by
field from M2a's `yue2.Issue` to `engine.Issue` (`TestIssueConversion_Lossless`); `ExpectedAudioSeconds`
= `ceil(seconds)` of `seconds, _ := yue2.EstimateDuration(parsed.Request)` (`ParseRequest(raw, ParseOptions{})`; the
function returns `(seconds float64, basis string)`) clamped to [30, 360] (design §3.3: "lyric
sections, or ABC bars × meter / Q:, clamped ≤ 360"); `Artifacts` per C73.4. `app.Build` takes the
registry from `registry.Default()`. An unknown `engine` in an estimate ⇒ 422 `VALIDATION_FAILED`
(`details.field = "engine"`). `TestImportGraph_M3` (M3-B1; runs `go list -deps -json ./internal/...`):
`engine` imports neither `engine/yue2` nor `engine/registry`; `engine/yue2` imports `engine` and no other
M3 package; `lineage` does not import `library`, `spend` or `jobs`; `generation`, `upload`, `spend`
and `drafts` do not import `jobs` or `api`; `provider` does not import `jobs`.

### C71. Generation planner and Operator Ledger (`internal/lineage`, `internal/generation`)

**C71.1 Items** (the one input shape behind every create path; `estimate_variant` builds one item).
The API type lives in `internal/api/types_m3.go`; the handler converts it to the domain type
`generation.Item` (identical fields, `Recipe json.RawMessage`, `Patch generation.Patch` with concrete
`OptString`/`OptNumber` tri-state structs — no generic types with methods), so `generation` never
imports `api`:
```go
// internal/api/types_m3.go
type ItemIn struct {
    Mode           string          `json:"mode"`                       // C71.2
    ParentTrackID  *string         `json:"parent_track_id,omitempty"`  // variants (required); from_draft (optional mother override)
    ParentTrackIDs []string        `json:"parent_track_ids,omitempty"` // from_template: 2..50, unique
    DraftIDs       []string        `json:"draft_ids,omitempty"`        // from_draft: 1..50, unique
    Recipe         api.RawRequest  `json:"recipe,omitempty"`           // compose_new (required)
    Patch          *Patch          `json:"patch,omitempty"`            // *_edit and from_template
    TemplateMode   string          `json:"template_mode,omitempty"`    // from_template: new_seed|same_seed_edit|lyric_edit|style_edit
    NSeeds         int             `json:"n_seeds,omitempty"`          // 1..10, default 1
    Seeds          []int64         `json:"seeds,omitempty"`            // 1..10 unique, each 0..2^53−1; overrides NSeeds
    Title          *string         `json:"title,omitempty"`            // 1..200 (compose_new, single-take items)
}
type Patch struct {
    Style    *string        `json:"style,omitempty"`
    Lyrics   *string        `json:"lyrics,omitempty"`
    Cot      *string        `json:"cot,omitempty"`
    ABC      NullableString `json:"abc,omitempty"`        // string ⇒ set; JSON null ⇒ remove; absent ⇒ keep
    CFGScale NullableNumber `json:"cfg_scale,omitempty"`  // number ⇒ set; null ⇒ remove; absent ⇒ keep
}
// NullableString / NullableNumber: {Set, Null bool; Value string|float64}; UnmarshalJSON distinguishes
// absent / null / value; they implement huma.SchemaProvider returning {"type":["string","null"]} and
// {"type":["number","null"]} (never Huma's empty schema `{}`, the M2a C38 trap), so REST rejects
// `"abc": 5`, the MCP input schema and `schema.d.ts` read `string | null` / `number | null`.
// TestNullablePatch_Schemas: REST 422 for `abc: 5`; MCP tools/list schema; schema.d.ts type text.
```
Field/mode mismatches ⇒ 422 `INVALID_ITEM` (`details: {item, field, rule}`); e.g. `patch` with
`new_seed`, `seeds` containing the parent's seed with `new_seed`, `n_seeds > 1` with `same_seed_edit`,
`draft_ids` with any other mode. Unknown tracks ⇒ 422 `UNKNOWN_TRACK` (M2a code; `details.track_id` names the first unknown id, as `playlists` does);
unknown drafts ⇒ 422 `UNKNOWN_DRAFT`; a `fulfilled`/`archived` draft or one with a live job ⇒ 409
`DRAFT_NOT_GENERATABLE` (`details.draft_ids`, `details.why`); a `discarded` parent is allowed (a human
may revive it on purpose; estimate warning `parent_discarded`). A take whose job input (C73.3, with
maximal-length placeholder URLs) would exceed `MaxRunBody` (2 MiB) ⇒ 422 `INVALID_ITEM` rule
`input_too_large` **at estimate** — never discovered at submit after reserving. Default titles (D-M3-27)
are cut to 200 runes at a rune boundary when the estimate is built.

**Reference and lossy mothers (D-M3-34).** Before anything else is computed, every mother
(`parent_track_id`, each of `parent_track_ids`, the `from_draft` override) goes through
`library.Service.RequireRecipeKnown` and then `RequireLosslessMaster` (M2c): a stub-recipe track ⇒ 422
`RECIPE_UNKNOWN`, a lossy master ⇒ 422 `LOSSY_MASTER`, both with `details.track_id` of the first offender
(the service returns `*library.RecipeUnknownError` / `*library.LossyMasterError`, which `libErr` already maps; a lossy track is always a reference track, so `RECIPE_UNKNOWN`
wins in practice). A draft never has a reference mother (M2c trigger), so only the override can fail.
`TestEstimate_RefusesUnknownRecipeAndLossy` (variant, template with one reference among five mothers ⇒ the
whole estimate is refused, nothing written, zero backend calls). A `compose_new` recipe whose own `id` equals
a stub's request id is not renamed silently: M2c's lint issue `recipe_unknown` (an error) blocks it with 422
`LINT_FAILED` (`TestEstimate_ComposeIdOfStubRefused`); `taken(id)` in C71.3 counts stub request ids too.

**C71.2 Modes → operators** (one row each; `TestModes_Table`):

| Mode | Base recipe | Patch | Seeds (C71.3 rule) | Operator | Parent of the child |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `new_seed` | parent's recipe | forbidden | n new seeds | `reroll_seed` | parent |
| `same_seed_edit` | parent's recipe | optional (empty = re-render, D-M3-11) | `[parent seed]`, n = 1 | `same_seed_edit` | parent |
| `lyric_edit` | parent's recipe | `lyrics` required; `abc`/`cot` allowed; `style` forbidden | `[parent seed]` + n−1 new | `lyric_edit` | parent |
| `style_edit` | parent's recipe | `style` required (BPM lives in style); `cfg_scale`/`cot`/`abc` allowed; `lyrics` forbidden | `[parent seed]` + n−1 new | `style_edit` | parent |
| `from_draft` | each draft's `request_json` (revision pinned) | forbidden | `[draft seed]` + n−1 new | `from_draft` | `parent_track_id` override, else the draft's `parent_track_id`, else none (root) |
| `compose_new` | `recipe` (parsed with `ParseRequest`, no `abc_path` resolver) | forbidden | `[recipe seed]` + n−1 new | `compose_new` | none (root) |
| `from_template` | each mother's recipe | per `template_mode` | per `template_mode`, per mother | `template_mode`'s operator, `params.via = "from_template"` | each mother |

Applying a patch: start from the base canonical request, set/remove the patched keys, set the seed and
the allocated id (C71.3), re-run `yue2.ParseRequest` (AllowTitle false) and take `Parsed.Canonical`.
`Apply(base yue2.Request, op string, params Params, patch *Patch) (yue2.Request, error)` is pure and is
what a replay uses (`TestMutation_ReplayReproducesRender`).

**C71.3 Seeds and request ids** (`internal/lineage`, pure + one read):
```go
func UsedSeeds(ctx context.Context, q Querier, contentHash string) ([]int64, error)
// SELECT r.seed FROM recipes r JOIN recipe_keys k ON k.recipe_id = r.id WHERE k.content_hash = ?
func NextSeeds(base int64, used []int64, n int) []int64
// Candidates in this order, skipping used ones, until n are taken: start = max(base, max(used)) + 1
// ascending to MaxSeed; then 1000 ascending (to start − 1); then 0 ascending to 999. Deterministic.
// Example: base MaxSeed − 1, used {}, n 3 ⇒ [MaxSeed, 1000, 1001].
func AllocateID(base string, seed int64, keep bool, taken func(id string) bool) (string, error)
// keep && !taken(base) ⇒ base. Else stem = SeedFamily(base), cut to ≤ 180 − len("-s<seed>-99") bytes at a
// rune boundary, trailing [-_.] trimmed; candidate = stem + "-s" + seed; then candidate + "-2" … "-99";
// all taken ⇒ ErrNoFreeID (422 INVALID_ITEM rule id_exhausted). Result matches yue2's id regex.
func ReusableDraftRecipe(ctx context.Context, q Querier, draft DraftRow) (recipeID string, ok bool, err error)
// ok iff the draft has source_recipe_id, that recipe's canonical hash equals the draft's pinned revision's
// canonical request (same id and seed), no track has recipe_id = it, and no non-terminal job uses it (SQL only).
```
Seed rule per take list: explicit `seeds` win (validated: unique, in range, `new_seed` excludes the
parent's seed); otherwise the mode's fixed seeds (table) followed by `NextSeeds` over `UsedSeeds(content
of the take's base recipe after the patch) ∪ seeds already planned in this estimate`. **Draft recipes
are reused (D-M3-10):** the `from_draft` take with the draft's own seed uses `ReusableDraftRecipe`;
when `ok`, the take's `recipe_id` is that row, its request id is the draft's id, nothing is inserted at
create, and `items_json` records `"reuse_recipe_id"`; when not `ok` (already rendered, edited since
import, or no source recipe) it allocates like any other take. `keep` (for `AllocateID`) is true only
for `compose_new` items that render exactly one take with their own seed. `taken(id)` = a recipe with
that `request_id` exists (any origin) — except the reused row itself — or an earlier take of the
estimate uses it. A take whose `render_hash` equals an existing recipe's or an earlier take's ⇒
estimate warning `duplicate_render` (`details.where` = `recipe` | `take:<index>`); a reused draft
recipe is not a duplicate of itself.

**C71.4 Expanded takes** (`estimates.items_json`; the frozen plan create executes):
```json
[{"take_index":0,"item_index":0,"mode":"new_seed","operator":"reroll_seed",
  "parent_track_id":"…","draft_id":null,"draft_revision":null,
  "request_json":"{\"cot\":\"full\",\"id\":\"syn-a-s1102\",…}","recipe_hash":"…","content_hash":"…","render_hash":"…",
  "request_id":"syn-a-s1102","seed":1102,"title":"Syn A · s1102","reuse_recipe_id":null,
  "params":{"mode":"new_seed","seed_policy":"auto","n_seeds":3,"via":null},"patch":null,
  "expected_audio_s":216,"expected_s":96,"timeout_s":186,"expected_micro_usd":18400,
  "take_micro_usd":38525,"attempt_overhead_micro_usd":38334,"max_micro_usd":76859,"input_bytes":1834,
  "warnings":[{"code":"italian_unofficial","field":"style","severity":"warning","message":"…"}]}]
```
`seed_policy` ∈ `auto` | `explicit` | `parent` | `draft` | `recipe`. The create transaction trusts only
this JSON (bound by the token to `recipe_hashes`) and re-checks only what can change between estimate
and create (C72.4 step 5).

**C71.5 Operator Ledger rows.** The planned mutation is frozen in `jobs.mutation_json` at create:
`{"operator":"reroll_seed","params":{…},"recipe_patch":null|{…},"rationale":"…"|null,"principal_id":"agent:<id>"}`.
The `mutations` row is inserted **in the take's ingest transaction** (the child row must exist first;
M1 `mutations.child_track_id` is NOT NULL): `parent_track_id` = `jobs.source_track_id`, `child_track_id`
= the new track, `operator`, `inferred = 0`, `params_json = params` (+ `"job_id"`), `rationale`,
`principal_id`, `job_id`, `recipe_patch_json`. One row per completed take; failed takes have none.
Operators are Go-validated: M1's `inferred_sibling` plus `reroll_seed`, `same_seed_edit`, `lyric_edit`,
`style_edit`, `from_draft`, `compose_new`. Today `library.InsertTake` accepts only `inferred_sibling` and
builds `params_json` from `NewMutation.Rule`; D-M3-35 adds `NewMutation.{Params, JobID, RecipePatchJSON}`
and the operator enumeration (M2c's `reference_group`/`mapping` rules stay for import takes).

**C71.6 Lineage reads** (`lineage.Tree`):
```go
func (s *Service) Tree(ctx context.Context, trackID string, limit int) (Tree, error) // limit ≤ 500
type Node struct {
    ID, Title, Status, Operator, RequestID string; ParentID *string; Generation int; Seed *int64 // nil ⇔ !RecipeKnown
    RecipeKnown, Lossy bool // M2c: a reference node has a stub request id and no seed
    Inferred bool; Rationale *string; Stars, CriticStars *int; Origin string; Outcome *string
    CostMicroUSD *money.MicroUSD; CostBasis *string; JobID *string; CreatedAt string; ChildrenCount int
}
type Tree struct{ RootID string; Nodes []Node; Truncated bool; Total int } // nodes in pre-order (generation, created_at)
```
`get_lineage` has no cursor: nodes beyond `limit` **or** beyond the 90 000-byte page cap (M1 C17) are
cut at a node boundary in pre-order, `truncated = true`, and `total` still counts every node
(`TestLineage_TruncatedByBytes`).

**C71.7 Recipe keys.** `lineage.Keys(canonical []byte) (contentHash, renderHash string)` = hex sha256 of
`canonjson.Canonical` of the request object with `id` (render) or `id` and `seed` (content) removed.
`library.InsertRecipe(ctx, tx, p yue2.Parsed, createdBy string) (id string, created bool, err error)`
(M1 C17, amended in M3-B2: `createdBy` replaces the importer's implicit `'import'`; every M1 caller
passes `"import"`) inserts the `recipe_keys` row in the same transaction (`ON CONFLICT(recipe_id) DO
NOTHING`) and, for any `createdBy` other than `import`, returns `ErrRecipeExists` unless `created` is
true (a job recipe is always a new row; reuse goes through `ReusableDraftRecipe`, never through an
insert); `lineage.BackfillRecipeKeys(ctx)` fills rows for `engine = 'yue2'` recipes inserted
before M3 and never for stubs (D-M3-34) (idempotent; run by `serve`, `dev`, `mcp-stdio` and `import` at start;
`TestBackfillRecipeKeys_Idempotent`).

### C72. Spend: quote math, tokens, estimates, caps, ledger, approvals (`internal/spend`)

**C72.1 Quote math (pure, `quote.go`; every number an integer):**
```go
type QuoteParams struct {
    PricePPH     money.MicroUSD // WithMarginPPM(max serverless list price over the allowed GPU types, MM_PRICE_MARGIN_PPM)
    RTFMilli     int            // max rtf_milli over the allowed GPU classes present in rtf_stats (none known ⇒ max over all rows)
    ColdStartS   int            // MM_COLD_START_S
    IdleS        int            // endpoint workers.idleTimeout (required; absent ⇒ GENERATION_DISABLED endpoint_config)
    WorkersMax   int            // endpoint workers.max (1..2, else GENERATION_DISABLED endpoint_inactive / endpoint_config)
    MaxRuntimeS  int            // MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME in seconds (= endpoint timeout, D-M3-16)
    KillSlackS   int            // MM_KILL_SLACK_S
}
type TakeCost struct {
    AudioS, ExpectedS, TimeoutS int
    ExpectedMicro, TakeMicro, MaxMicro money.MicroUSD // TakeMicro = the `take` reserve; MaxMicro = TakeMicro + one attempt overhead
}
type BatchQuote struct {
    Takes []TakeCost; AttemptOverheadS int
    AttemptOverheadMicro, ExpectedMicro, MaxMicro, RetryAllowanceMicro, CeilingMicro money.MicroUSD
    ETAS int
}
func QuoteBatch(p QuoteParams, audioS []int) BatchQuote
```
Formulas (`n = len(audioS)`):
- `expected_s_i = ceil(RTFMilli × audio_s_i / 1000)`
- `timeout_s_i = min(MaxRuntimeS, max(ceil(3 × expected_s_i / 2), expected_s_i + 90))`
- `take_micro_i = ForSeconds(timeout_s_i + KillSlackS, PricePPH)` (the `take` reserve, written at create)
- `attempt_overhead_s = ColdStartS + IdleS`; `attempt_overhead_micro = ForSeconds(attempt_overhead_s,
  PricePPH)` (reserved **per attempt** in its claim transaction, C72.5)
- `max_micro_i = take_micro_i + attempt_overhead_micro`; `expected_micro_i = ForSeconds(min(expected_s_i,
  timeout_s_i), PricePPH)` (a take is killed at its timeout, so expected never exceeds max — the
  `max ≥ expected` CHECK holds even when `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME` clamps)
- `batch.max = Σ max_micro_i` (= Σ take + n × one attempt overhead — the no-retry worst case)
- `retry_allowance = min(n, WorkersMax) × max_i(max_micro_i)`; `ceiling = batch.max + retry_allowance`
  (D-M3-29)
- `batch.expected = Σ expected_micro_i + attempt_overhead_micro` (one warm busy period)
- `eta_s = ColdStartS + ceil(n / WorkersMax) × max_i(expected_s_i)`

Golden example (`TestQuoteBatch_Example`): 7 takes × 216 s audio, `RTFMilli 440`, `PricePPH 690000`,
`ColdStartS 180`, `IdleS 20`, `WorkersMax 2`, `MaxRuntimeS 900`, `KillSlackS 15` ⇒ `expected_s 96`,
`timeout_s 186`, `take_micro_i 38525` (201 s), `attempt_overhead_micro 38334` (200 s), `max_micro_i
76859`, `expected_micro_i 18400`, **max 538013** (displayed "$0.54", above the $0.50 agent
auto-approve), `retry_allowance 153718`, **ceiling 691731** ("up to $0.70"), **expected 167134**,
`eta_s 564`. Clamp example: audio 360 s at `RTFMilli 2000` ⇒ `expected_s 720`, `timeout_s 900`,
`take_micro` = `ForSeconds(915, …)`. `TestQuoteBatch_PerAttemptOverhead`: the overhead in `max` is
`n × attempt_overhead_micro` for every `n` and `WorkersMax` (no `min(n, WorkersMax)` factor).

The price that reaches `QuoteParams` is built by `spend.EffectivePrice(q provider.Quote, marginPPM)`:
`WithMarginPPM(q.ListPricePPH, marginPPM)`; a missing or zero list price never reaches it (C77.5 fails
closed with `catalog_unreadable`).

**RTF update** (after a completed take with a known GPU class): `obs = clamp(round(1000 ×
e2e_seconds / audio_seconds), 50, 5000)` from the worker's timings; `rtf_milli' = (4 × rtf_milli + obs
+ 2) / 5` (integer division; α = 0.2); unknown class ⇒ insert with `obs`. `TestRTF_EWMA` (440, obs 500
⇒ 452; obs 50 clamp; new class).

**C72.2 Confirmation tokens** (`token.go`): `mmc_` + 52 lowercase base32 chars (no padding) of 32
random bytes (M0's gitleaks `mmc_` rule); stored as `hex(HMAC-SHA256(ConfirmKey, token))`; TTL 10 min;
single use (C69 token consumption SQL); bound to the estimate's principal key, recipe hashes and max
cost (all in the estimate row the hash selects). Tokens never appear in logs, audit rows, job events,
idempotency rows or any response other than the issuing estimate (`TestNoSecretLeak`).

**C72.3 Caps and usage** (`caps.go`, `usage.go`):
```go
type Caps struct {
    MonthlyMicro, BatchMicro, HumanDailyMicro, AgentDailyMicro money.MicroUSD
    AutoApproveTakes int; AutoApproveMicro money.MicroUSD
}
var DefaultCaps = Caps{10_000_000, 5_000_000, 5_000_000, 1_000_000, 5, 500_000}
type Usage struct {
    MonthSettled, Outstanding, PrincipalDaySettled, PrincipalOutstanding money.MicroUSD
    MonthStart, DayStart time.Time
}
func (s *Service) Usage(ctx context.Context, q Querier, principalKey string, now time.Time) (Usage, error)
func CheckCaps(u Usage, c Caps, principalDaily, ceiling, newReserve money.MicroUSD) *CapError // create; nil ⇒ fits
func (s *Service) ReserveTopUp(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, t TopUp) (*CapError, error)    // every later reserve
type TopUp struct{ BatchID, JobID string; AttemptID *string; Reason string; Amount money.MicroUSD; Lease LeaseFence }
type TokenLimits struct{ DailyMicro, AutoApproveMicro *money.MicroUSD; AutoApproveTakes *int } // api_tokens columns; nil ⇒ global
```
`Querier` is satisfied by `*sql.Tx`; **inside a write transaction every read goes through that
transaction** (never `st.W`/`st.R`: the writer pool has one connection, a second use deadlocks, and a
reader would see a pre-transaction snapshot) — `TestCreate_ReadsThroughTx` uses a store whose pools
panic while a create transaction is open. Calendar boundaries are local to the process time zone
(`time.Local`, i.e. `TZ`; tests inject a location). SQL (binding):
- per-batch outstanding: `SELECT batch_id, SUM(CASE kind WHEN 'reserve' THEN micro_usd WHEN 'settle'
  THEN -micro_usd WHEN 'release' THEN -micro_usd ELSE 0 END) FROM ledger WHERE batch_id IN (SELECT id
  FROM batches WHERE reconciled_at IS NULL) GROUP BY batch_id`; `Outstanding = Σ max(0, v)`;
  `PrincipalOutstanding` restricts to the principal's batches.
- `MonthSettled = SELECT COALESCE(SUM(micro_usd),0) FROM ledger WHERE (kind = 'settle' AND at >= ?m0
  AND at < ?m1) OR (kind = 'bill' AND window_start >= ?m0 AND window_start < ?m1)` (month bounds
  converted to `store.FormatTime`). **`bill` rows count in the month of their `window_start`**, never of
  `at`: a negative adjustment written on 2 November for October buckets lowers October, not November.
  Quiet windows never cross a calendar-month boundary (C76.5), so a window belongs to one month.
- `PrincipalDaySettled = … WHERE kind = 'settle' AND principal_id = ? AND at >= ? AND at < ?`.

`CheckCaps` (create) order (first failure wins; all checked with `>`):
1. `ceiling > BatchMicro` ⇒ cap `batch` (the batch cap bounds what the batch may ever reserve);
2. `MonthSettled + Outstanding + newReserve > MonthlyMicro` ⇒ cap `monthly`;
3. `PrincipalDaySettled + PrincipalOutstanding + newReserve > principalDaily` ⇒ cap `daily`.
At create `newReserve = batch.max` (Σ take reserves written now + the first attempt overhead of every
job, so that a batch that fits at create normally fits its first claims). `ReserveTopUp` (every
`attempt_overhead`, `topup_ambiguous`, `topup_requeue`, `topup_price`; inside the `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`
transaction that writes it, with the lease predicate) checks, in order: 1. `Σ reserve rows of the batch
+ Amount > batch.ceiling` ⇒ cap `batch_ceiling`; 2. monthly; 3. daily of the **batch owner** — each with
`newReserve = Amount`; a refusal returns the `CapError` and writes nothing; the caller fails the job
`BUDGET_EXCEEDED` (C75.2 row 6). `principalDaily` = `HumanDailyMicro` for humans; for agents the
token's `daily_budget_micro_usd` or `AgentDailyMicro`. `CapError` ⇒ 402 `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` (at create),
`details: {cap, limit_micro_usd, used_micro_usd, requested_micro_usd, limit_usd, used_usd,
requested_usd}` (`used` = the left-hand sum without the request). **Hard caps apply to humans too.**
**Lowering a cap mid-run** (`set_spend_caps`): work already reserved continues (running attempts,
settlements and releases never check caps); every **new** reserve — creates, claims and top-ups — is
checked against the new caps, so a lowered cap stops new claims and resubmits at once.

**C72.4 The create transaction** (`generation.Service.Create(ctx, p, in CreateIn, encode func(Created)
(status int, body []byte, err error)) (Created, error)`, `CreateIn{Token, Rationale, Via string;
VariantTrackID *string}`; one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; exact order). `encode`
is supplied by the op handler (it calls `api.EncodeResponse` on the `Batch` built from `Created`) and is
called **inside** the transaction before commit, so `generation` never imports `api`; the service's
`Wake func()` (nil-safe; `nil` in `mcp-stdio`) is called after commit.
0. (Gate, before the handler, M2a C29) idempotency `Begin` for (principal, key): replay ⇒ stored 201,
   nothing else runs, the token is untouched; different body ⇒ 409 `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`. The gate
   installs the mutable marks (C80) and passes the lease generation in the context.
1. Provider gate, **from DB state and the cached quote only — never the network**: generation enabled
   (C68), backend armed, no open trip, and `estimate.backend` equals the active backend (else 409
   `ESTIMATE_STALE`, `details.reason = "backend_changed"`) — else 503 `GENERATION_DISABLED` /
   `PROVIDER_DISARMED` / `PROVIDER_TRIPPED` (no row written; the token stays valid).
2. Token: hash → estimate row; unknown or another principal's ⇒ 409 `CONFIRMATION_INVALID` (same
   message for both); consumed ⇒ 409 `CONFIRMATION_INVALID` (`details.reason = "consumed"`, and **for
   the same principal** `details.batch_id` = the batch that consumed it, so a client that lost the 201
   and retried with a fresh key finds its batch instead of re-estimating a duplicate); `expires_at ≤
   now` ⇒ 410 `CONFIRMATION_EXPIRED`; for `create_variant` the estimate must be a one-item variant
   estimate of the path's track, else 409 `CONFIRMATION_INVALID`.
3. Consume the token (C69 SQL; same transaction).
4. Caps: `Usage` (through the tx) + `CheckCaps(…, estimate.ceiling_micro_usd, estimate.max_micro_usd)` ⇒
   on failure roll back everything (the token stays unconsumed) and answer 402 `BUDGET_EXCEEDED`. **Zero
   backend calls happen in create**, success or not (`TestAcceptance_M3_OverBudgetZeroBackendCalls`
   counts every backend method incl. `Quote`, and every `runpodtest` request incl. GETs).
5. Re-validate what can drift since the estimate: every `request_id` still free (else 409
   `ESTIMATE_STALE`, `details.request_ids`); every draft still `backlog`, same `revision`, no live job
   (else 409 `ESTIMATE_STALE`); every `reuse_recipe_id` still unrendered and unused (`ReusableDraftRecipe`
   again; else 409 `ESTIMATE_STALE`, `details.draft_ids`); parents still exist (their `recipes.engine` and
   `tracks.lossy` are frozen columns, so the estimate-time reference/lossy refusal cannot drift and is
   not repeated; the M2c triggers are the backstop, D-M3-34). Lint is not re-run (the recipes are frozen).
6. Insert: recipes (`library.InsertRecipe(ctx, tx, parsed, p.Key())`, + `recipe_keys`; reused draft
   recipes are not inserted), the batch (`state = awaiting_confirmation` if approval is needed, else
   `active`; frozen `ceiling`, `workers_max`, `idle_s`, `attempt_overhead_s`, `kill_slack_s`), the
   approval row (C72.6), jobs (`state = awaiting_confirmation` or `queued`, `queued_at = now` when
   `queued`, `next_attempt_at = now`, `backend` = the estimate's), the ledger `take` reserve rows
   (C72.5), `audit.InsertTx` (op, created ids = batch + jobs, `CostMicroUSD` = the ceiling),
   `idem.CompleteTx` with the exact 201 body from `encode`, `estimates.consumed_batch_id`.
7. Commit; `Wake()`; if approval is needed and the principal is an agent, no push yet (the agent
   calls `request_approval`).
`TestCreate_OrderOfChecks` asserts the order with spies (a replay never reaches step 1; an expired token
with exhausted caps answers 410, not 402; a valid token with exhausted caps answers 402 and leaves the
token consumable). `TestCreate_ConsumedTokenSamePrincipalReturnsBatch` (lost 201, retry with a fresh
key ⇒ 409 with `details.batch_id`; another principal's consumed token ⇒ no `batch_id`).
`TestCreate_BackendChangedStale` (estimate under `fake`, active backend `runpod-serverless` ⇒ 409).

**C72.5 Ledger** (`ledger.go`):
```go
type Entry struct {
    Kind string; MicroUSD money.MicroUSD; BatchID, JobID, AttemptID, WindowStart *string
    PrincipalID, Reason string
}
type Ledger struct{ /* now, observer */ }
func (l *Ledger) Append(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, es ...Entry) error
func (l *Ledger) Outstanding(ctx context.Context, q Querier, batchID string) (money.MicroUSD, error)
func (s *Service) SettleAttempt(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, a AttemptSettle) error // the ONE settlement helper
type AttemptSettle struct {
    BatchID, JobID, AttemptID, PrincipalID string
    Basis string                // "measured" (provider executionTime) | "time_bound"
    ExecutionMS *int64          // measured basis only
    TimeoutS, KillSlackS int; PricePPH money.MicroUSD; Lease LeaseFence
}
type Observer func(Usage) // test hook: called after every committed ledger transaction (C86.3)
```
Two pots per batch (`TestLedger_Pots`): the **execution pot of a job** = its reserves with reason
`take`, `topup_ambiguous`, `topup_requeue`, `topup_price`; the **overhead pot of a batch** = its
`attempt_overhead` reserves (rows carry `job_id` and `attempt_id` for traceability). Rows and reasons
(Go-validated; `TestLedger_Reasons` table):

| Kind | Reason | Pot | Amount | When |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| reserve | `take` | execution | `ForSeconds(timeout_s + KillSlackS, price)` per job | create |
| reserve | `attempt_overhead` | overhead | `ForSeconds(ColdStartS + max(live IdleS, batch.idle_s), attempt price)` | every attempt, in its claim transaction (`ReserveTopUp`) |
| reserve | `topup_ambiguous` | execution | `ForSeconds(timeout_s + KillSlackS, price)` | before resubmitting after an `unknown` attempt (ghost or status lost, D-M3-4) |
| reserve | `topup_requeue` | execution | `ForSeconds(timeout_s + KillSlackS, price)` | a provider requeue was observed (C73.2) |
| reserve | `topup_price` | execution | `ForSeconds(timeout_s + KillSlackS, new) − ForSeconds(…, old)` | the catalog price rose since the estimate (C75.3) |
| settle | `execution` | execution | `min(ForMillis(execution_ms, attempt price), remaining execution pot)` | provider record with `executionTime` (any terminal status) |
| settle | `execution_unknown` | execution | `min(ForSeconds(timeout_s + KillSlackS, attempt price), remaining)` | time bound: an `unknown` attempt's window closed, a `submitted` attempt whose cancel was never confirmed reached its window end, or a terminal status without `executionTime` after a run (C73.2) |
| settle | `excess` | — | provisional − the `execution`/`execution_unknown` part | `SettleAttempt` only: provisional exceeded the remaining execution pot (also trips, C76.3) |
| settle | `overhead` | overhead | `min(ForSeconds(meter-attributed overhead seconds, batch price), Σ attempt_overhead reserves)` | batch finished (C76.6); never more than reserved |
| residual | `held` | — | the batch's outstanding at finish (informational; not in sums) | batch finished |
| release | `never_ran` | execution | the job's remaining execution pot | job terminal and every attempt `rejected`, or `done` with basis `provider` **and** `execution_ms = 0` **and** a status pinned by fixtures as a queue expiry (C73.2) |
| release | `not_accepted` | overhead | the attempt's `attempt_overhead` | the attempt ended `rejected` (the provider never accepted it) |
| release | `rejected` / `expired` | — | the batch's entire outstanding | `reject_batch` / approval expiry (same transaction sets `reconciled_at`, C72.6) |
| release | `reconciled` | — | the batch's outstanding | its quiet window was reconciled (C76.5) |
| release | `hold_elapsed` | — | the batch's outstanding | billing unreadable and `finished_at + ResidualHold` passed |
| bill | `reconcile` | — | billed − provisional of the window (may be negative) | C76.5 |
| bill | `late_adjust` | — | the change of an already-reconciled bucket (may be negative) | C76.5 re-fetch of the last 72 h |

`SettleAttempt` is the only writer of `execution`, `execution_unknown` and `excess`: it computes
`provisional` (`ForMillis(execution_ms)` for `measured`, `ForSeconds(timeout_s + KillSlackS)` for
`time_bound`), writes `settle = min(provisional, remaining execution pot)` and, when positive, `excess =
provisional − settle`, sets `job_attempts.settled = 1`, all in the caller's transaction. Because the
`take` reserve already includes `KillSlackS`, a job stopped at its timeout with `executionTime` up to
`timeout_s + KillSlackS` never writes `excess` (`TestSettle_KillSlackNoExcess`). Unused overhead stays
outstanding (counted against the caps) until reconciliation or the residual hold
(`TestSettle_OverheadFromMeter`, `TestBatch_NothingRanSettlesZeroOverhead`: every job cancelled while
queued at RunPod or expired in its queue, no worker ever seen ⇒ `overhead` settle 0, nothing charged to
the owner's daily cap).
`Append` enforces, in the same transaction, per pot, per job and per batch: `Σ settle (except excess) + Σ
release ≤ Σ reserve` (`ErrLedgerInvariant`; property-tested), and that `bill` rows carry `principal_id =
'system:billing'` and a `window_start`. **Every ledger change that affects caps happens inside the
transaction that causes it** (create, claim, top-up, approval expiry, reject, settlement,
reconciliation), and every runner/reconciler ledger write carries the lease predicate (D-M3-30).

**C72.6 Approvals** (`approval.go`):
```go
type ApprovalNeed struct{ Needed bool; Reasons []string }
func NeedsApproval(p auth.Principal, via string, takes int, ceiling money.MicroUSD, tok TokenLimits, c Caps) ApprovalNeed
```
- `via = human_create` (human principal on `/human/api/`) ⇒ not needed; approval row `state='approved',
  via='human_create', decided_by = p.Key(), decided_at = created_at` (the CHECK requires `decided_at`).
- human principal on `/api/` ⇒ needed, reason `human_api_path` (D-M3-9).
- agent ⇒ needed iff `takes > limitTakes` (reason `takes_over_limit`) or `ceiling > limitMicro`
  (`cost_over_limit`), where `limitTakes = tok.AutoApproveTakes ?? c.AutoApproveTakes` and `limitMicro =
  tok.AutoApproveMicro ?? c.AutoApproveMicro`; not needed ⇒ row `state='auto', via='auto', decided_by =
  p.Key()` (within the limits a human granted it), `decided_at = created_at`.
- needed ⇒ row `state='pending'`, `expires_at = created + 24 h`.
- `approve_batch` (human-only), **one transaction**: `UPDATE approvals SET state='approved', via='web',
  decided_by=?, decided_at=?, reason=? WHERE batch_id=? AND state='pending' AND expires_at > ?` and
  `UPDATE batches SET state='active' WHERE id=? AND state='awaiting_confirmation'`; its jobs →
  `queued` (setting `queued_at`) on the runner's next step (D-M3-3: job state is the runner's); `Wake()`.
  Already approved ⇒ 200 (idempotent); rejected/expired ⇒ 409 `INVALID_TRANSITION`.
- `reject_batch`, one transaction: approval `rejected` + batch `rejected` (`finished_at = now`) +
  `release` (`rejected`) of the batch's outstanding + `reconciled_at = now`; jobs → `cancelled`
  (`REJECTED`) by the runner.
- expiry (reconciler, C76), one transaction with the lease predicate: approval `state='expired',
  via='expiry', decided_by='system:reconciler'`, batch `expired` (`finished_at = now`), `release`
  (`expired`), `reconciled_at = now`; jobs → `expired` by the runner. (Nothing of such a batch ever
  reached the provider, so no billing window has to wait for it, C76.5.)

**Batch states and their writers** (`TestBatchTransitions_Writers`; Go table `spend.BatchTransitions`):

| From | To | Writer (transaction) |
|---|---|---|
| — | `active` / `awaiting_confirmation` | create (C72.4) |
| `awaiting_confirmation` | `active` | `approve_batch` (with the approval decision) |
| `awaiting_confirmation` | `rejected` | `reject_batch` (with the release and `reconciled_at`) |
| `awaiting_confirmation` | `expired` | reconciler approval expiry (with the release and `reconciled_at`) |
| `active` | `finished` | runner batch finish: every job terminal, every attempt `done`/`rejected` and settled (with the overhead settle and `residual`) |
| `finished` | — (`reconciled_at` set) | reconciler: quiet-window reconciliation or residual hold |

`cancel_batch` changes no batch state: it records a cancel intent on each non-terminal job; the batch
finishes through the runner like any other.
- `request_approval` (agent, batch creator only, else 403 `NOT_BATCH_OWNER`): pending ⇒ push one ntfy
  message (C82; "up to" = `DisplayCeil(ceiling)`) unless the last push was < 10 min ago; `UPDATE approvals SET requested_count =
  requested_count + 1, last_requested_at=? WHERE batch_id=? AND state='pending' AND (last_requested_at IS
  NULL OR last_requested_at <= ?)` — 0 rows ⇒ `notified: false` with `next_request_allowed_at`.

### C73. Backends and the worker contract (`internal/provider`, `mm.worker/1`)

**C73.1 Interface:**
```go
package provider
type Backend interface {
    Name() string                                                // "fake" | "runpod-serverless"
    Quote(ctx context.Context) (Quote, error)                     // list price, pools, endpoint config (reconciler only; never in a request path)
    Submit(ctx context.Context, t TakeSpec) (Ref, error)          // one provider job; NOT idempotent, never replayed (D-M3-4)
    Poll(ctx context.Context, r Ref) (RemoteStatus, error)
    Cancel(ctx context.Context, r Ref) error                      // returns after the POST was acknowledged; confirmation comes from Poll
    Sweep(ctx context.Context) (SweepReport, error)               // workers, endpoint config, drift, Date-header skew
    PurgeQueue(ctx context.Context) error
    ScaleToZero(ctx context.Context) error                        // ErrNotPermitted without a scoped write key
    RevertDrift(ctx context.Context, d []Drift) error             // ErrNotPermitted likewise
    Billing(ctx context.Context, from, to time.Time) ([]BillBucket, error) // hourly; ErrNotPermitted when unreadable
    Capabilities() Capabilities                                   // from key presence + key-capabilities.json
}
var (
    ErrNotPermitted      = errors.New("provider: not permitted with the configured keys")
    ErrAccess            = errors.New("provider: access refused (401/403/1010)")        // trips provider_access (C76.3)
    ErrEndpointNotFound  = errors.New("provider: endpoint not found")                   // a provider fault, never job loss
)
type Quote struct {
    Backend string; ListPricePPH money.MicroUSD; Pools, GPUClasses []string
    Endpoint EndpointConfig; ObservedAt time.Time
}
type EndpointConfig struct { // as read from the provider (C77.5); the desired values live in runpod.DesiredSpec
    Name, Type string                    // Type "QUEUE"
    ScalerType string; ScalerValue string // "QUEUE_DELAY", decimal literal of queueDelay
    WorkersMin, WorkersMax int; IdleTimeoutS *int // nil ⇒ absent (fails closed)
    TimeoutMS int64; GPUCount int; MinCUDA string; Pools, ExcludedTypes []string
    Flashboot string; ImageDigest string; RequestURLs map[string]string // run, status, cancel, … (host checked, C77.5)
}
type UploadTarget struct{ URL string; MaxBytes int64 } // URL never persisted, logged or returned
type WorkerExpect struct{ Weights map[string]string }  // repo ⇒ revision; from internal/engine/yue2/weights.go (C83)
type Output struct {   // C73.5, decoded strictly (DisallowUnknownFields)
    Contract, TakeID, AttemptID, Status string; Error *OutputError
    Artifacts map[string]ArtifactDigest; AudioSeconds *json.Number
    Truncated map[string]bool; Timings map[string]*json.Number; Env OutputEnv
}
type OutputError struct{ Code, Message string }
type ArtifactDigest struct{ SHA256 string; Bytes int64 }
type OutputEnv struct{ Engine, PkgVersion, CodeCommit, Decoder, GPUName, CUDA, Torch, WorkerBuild, Python string; Weights map[string]string }
type TakeSpec struct {
    JobID, AttemptID, Engine string
    Request   json.RawMessage           // canonical engine-native request
    TimeoutS  int; TTL time.Duration; UploadBudgetS int
    Uploads   map[string]UploadTarget   // artifact ⇒ target; minted at submit, never persisted or logged
    StartURL  string                    // D-M3-28; minted at submit, never persisted or logged
    Expect    WorkerExpect              // pinned weight revisions
    Deadline  time.Time                 // = window_ends_at
}
type Ref struct{ Backend, ID string } // ID never leaves the server (API, events, logs)
type RemoteState string // Queued | Running | Completed | Failed | Cancelled | TimedOut | NotFound | Unknown
type RemoteStatus struct {
    State RemoteState; Raw string        // Raw = the provider's status string (Unknown keeps it for the event)
    ExecutionMS, DelayMS *int64          // nil ⇒ absent in the record
    WorkerID string                      // opaque; hashed to worker_key before storage (C76.6)
    Output json.RawMessage               // ≤ 64 KiB; the worker output (C73.5) when present
    ErrorText string                     // redacted, ≤ 500 bytes; never stored outside job_events.detail_json
}
type Capabilities struct{ ScaleToZero, RevertDrift bool; Billing string /* "hour" | "none" */ }
type Worker struct {
    ID string /* opaque; hashed */; Status string; UptimeS *int /* nil until placed */
    StartedAt *time.Time; Image string; IsStale bool
}
type Drift struct{ Field, Want, Got string }
type SweepReport struct{ Workers []Worker; Endpoint *EndpointConfig; Drift []Drift; ObservedAt time.Time; Skew *time.Duration }
type BillBucket struct{ Start, End time.Time; Total, Compute, Disk money.MicroUSD } // Compute = gpuAmount + cpuAmount
```
Design §3.3's `Quote(ctx, e, takes)` becomes `Quote(ctx)`: the per-take math lives once in
`spend.QuoteBatch` for every backend. **Pinned weights:** the revisions the runner sends in
`expect.weights` and that `provision` writes as `MM_YUE2_3B_REVISION`/`MM_YUE2_VAE_REVISION` come from one
committed Go file, `internal/engine/yue2/weights.go` (`var Weights = map[string]string{"m-a-p/YuE2-3B":
"<40 hex>", "m-a-p/YuE2-Vae": "<40 hex>"}`); the worker-contract job and `TestWeights_ProvisionMatches`
read the same values.

**C73.2 Status mapping and attempt classification.** RunPod `IN_QUEUE` ⇒ `Queued`; `IN_PROGRESS` ⇒
`Running`; `COMPLETED` ⇒ `Completed`; `FAILED` ⇒ `Failed`; `CANCELLED` ⇒ `Cancelled`; `TIMED_OUT` ⇒
`TimedOut`; any other string ⇒ `Unknown` (non-terminal, job event `unknown_status` with the raw string,
polled again). An HTTP 404 on `/status` is followed by `GET /health`: health 2xx ⇒ `NotFound` (the
**job** is gone); health 404 ⇒ `ErrEndpointNotFound` (a provider fault: trip `provider_access`, never
job loss). **Whether an attempt ran is decided from the provider record, never from which polls saw
`Running`** (a job can run entirely between two polls or while the app is down, and `/run` results
are kept only ~30 min). Classification, applied in this order on every observation
(`jobs.Classify`, `TestClassify_AttemptTable`):
1. A received, verified `mm-result.json` with `status: "ok"` for the attempt ⇒ the take completes from
   it (row 7) whatever the status says; the status is then used only for settlement.
2. `executionTime > 0` in the record ⇒ **it ran**: settle `execution` from it (any terminal status,
   including a `TIMED_OUT` from an `executionTimeout` kill).
3. Provider-terminal (`Completed`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`, `TimedOut`) **without** `executionTime` while
   there is evidence of a run (`in_progress_seen`, `announced_at`, an upload, or a non-empty output) ⇒
   settle `execution_unknown` at the time bound (`timeout_s + KillSlackS`).
4. Provider-terminal with `executionTime` 0/absent, no evidence of a run, and a shape that the probe
   fixtures (C77.8 (c), `key-capabilities.json` `never_ran_shapes`) pin as "never ran": the TTL-expiry
   shape at or after `submitted_at + TTL` ⇒ **queue expiry** (row 13); the cancelled-while-queued shape
   ⇒ the attempt is `done` (basis `provider`) with nothing to settle. Any other provider-terminal record
   without `executionTime` ⇒ rule 3 (time bound), even without evidence of a run.
5. `NotFound` for an attempt with a ref ⇒ the attempt becomes `unknown` (time-bound, settled
   `execution_unknown` at `window_ends_at`, full `topup_ambiguous` before any resubmission) — **never a
   queue expiry** — unless all of: the probe pinned the TTL-expiry answer as 404, the attempt's
   `last_queued_at` is less than 30 min old (inside result retention, so a run would still be visible),
   `now ≥ submitted_at + TTL`, and no evidence of a run ⇒ queue expiry (row 13).
6. Seen `Running` and later `Queued` again (a **provider requeue**) ⇒ `requeue_seen = 1`, a
   `topup_requeue` reserve (refused ⇒ trip `provisional_exceeds_reserved` — spend may exceed the
   reservation), `Cancel` at once; the job goes to `failing` and ends `failed WORKER_LOST`; the attempt
   settles as one execution through rule 2 or 3, and the second possible execution is covered by the
   top-up (`TestRunner_ProviderRequeueCancelled`).
7. Anything else (`Queued`, `Running`, `Unknown`) ⇒ non-terminal; `Queued` sets `last_queued_at`,
   `Running` sets `in_progress_seen` (observations only).
`release never_ran` is written only when every attempt of the job is `rejected`, or `done` through rule
4 or rule 5's queue-expiry branch (C72.5). Until the probe has pinned the TTL-expiry shape, rules 4/5 never
fire and every such case is time-bound (conservative). `TestRunner_RanBetweenPolls` (never seen
`Running`, `COMPLETED` with `executionTime` ⇒ settled, no resubmit, no `never_ran`),
`TestRunner_404AfterDowntimeIsUnknown` (runner down 40 min, 404 ⇒ `unknown`, ambiguous top-up before the
resubmit), `TestRunner_TimedOutUnseenSettlesTimeout` (`TIMED_OUT` without `executionTime` after an
upload ⇒ `execution_unknown`).

**C73.3 Job input** (`worker/src/mmworker/schema/mm-worker-1.input.schema.json`, `additionalProperties: false`;
validated by the worker and, in Go tests, against every `TakeSpec` the backends build):
```json
{"contract":"mm.worker/1",
 "take_id":"0190…","attempt_id":"0190…",
 "engine":"yue2",
 "request":{"cot":"full","id":"syn-a-s1102","lyrics":"[Verse]\nla la","seed":1102,"style":"pop, 100 BPM"},
 "timeout_s":186,
 "upload_budget_s":60,
 "deadline_unix":1760000000,
 "start":{"url":"https://mm.example.com/worker/v1/attempts/0190…/start?exp=…&sig=…"},
 "uploads":{"audio.flac":{"url":"https://mm.example.com/worker/v1/uploads/0190…?exp=…&sig=…","max_bytes":167772160},
            "mm-result.json":{"url":"…","max_bytes":65536}},
 "expect":{"weights":{"m-a-p/YuE2-3B":"<rev>","m-a-p/YuE2-Vae":"<rev>"}}}
```
RunPod request body: `{"input": <job input>, "policy": {"executionTimeout": timeout_s × 1000, "ttl":
TTL in ms}}`, at most `MaxRunBody` = 2 MiB (inline ABC up to M1's 1 MiB plus the request and URLs;
RunPod's limit is 10 MB). The estimate computes `input_bytes` with maximal-length placeholder URLs and
refuses larger takes (C71.1), so a submit never discovers the limit after reserving
(`TestEstimate_InputTooLarge`, `TestBackend_SubmitBodyWithinEstimate`).

**C73.4 Artifacts.** Allowlist (Go `provider.Artifacts`, Python `mmworker.contract.ARTIFACTS`, one test
each asserting equality with the schema enum):

| Artifact | Required | max_bytes |
|---|---|---|
| `audio.flac` | yes | 160 MiB |
| `request.json`, `config.json`, `result.json` | yes | 4 MiB each |
| `score.abc` | optional-but-verified until observed (below); then iff `cot` ≠ `off` | 1 MiB |
| `plan.json`, `plan_manifest.json` | optional-but-verified until observed | 4 MiB each |
| `abc_tokens.npy`, `prefix.npy`, `semantic.npy` | no | 64 MiB each |
| `mm-result.json` | yes, uploaded last | 64 KiB |

`latent.npy` is never uploaded (design §3.3, D-M1-1). A hand-built `SongResult` in the contract job
cannot establish which files a real render writes (that would be circular), so the per-`cot` required
set comes from **`internal/provider/artifacts.observed.json`**, recorded from real GPU renders in M3-X3
(the canary's `cot: melody` and `cot: full` takes) and committed with the fixtures; until that file
lists a `cot`, `score.abc` and the plan files are optional-but-verified-if-reported for it
(`TestArtifacts_RequiredFollowsObserved`). Everything not required is optional-but-verified-if-reported.

**C73.5 Worker output** (`mm-worker-1.output.schema.json`; returned as the RunPod job output **and**
uploaded as `mm-result.json`):
```json
{"contract":"mm.worker/1","take_id":"…","attempt_id":"…","status":"ok",
 "error":null,
 "artifacts":{"audio.flac":{"sha256":"…","bytes":31457280},"request.json":{"sha256":"…","bytes":212}},
 "audio_seconds":215.8,
 "truncated":{"abc":false,"semantic":false},
 "timings":{"load_s":null,"generate_s":70.1,"save_s":1.2,"upload_s":3.4,"handler_s":75.0,"e2e_seconds":71.0},
 "env":{"engine":"yue2","pkg_version":"0.1.6","code_commit":"<40 hex>","weights":{"m-a-p/YuE2-3B":"<rev>","m-a-p/YuE2-Vae":"<rev>"},
        "decoder":"m-a-p/YuE2-Vae","gpu_name":"NVIDIA RTX A5000","cuda":"12.8","torch":"2.10.0",
        "worker_build":"<git sha>","python":"3.12.12"}}
```
`status: "error"` ⇒ `error: {code, message}` with `code` ∈ `BAD_INPUT`, `CONTRACT_VERSION`,
`WEIGHTS_MISMATCH`, `PIPELINE_ERROR`, `TRUNCATED_NOT_DICT`, `ARTIFACT_MISSING`, `ARTIFACT_TOO_LARGE`,
`UPLOAD_FAILED`, `UPLOAD_CONFLICT`, `UPLOAD_CLOSED`, `ATTEMPT_CLOSED` (start handshake or a cancel poll
answered 410; no `mm-result.json` upload is attempted), `START_FAILED` (the start call failed for longer
than 60 s; the handler does not render), `DEADLINE`; `message` ≤ 500 chars with every URL
query string removed. On error the handler returns `{"error": "<CODE>: <message>", "mm": <output>}` (so
RunPod marks the job `FAILED`) after a best-effort `mm-result.json` upload. `artifacts` never lists
`mm-result.json` itself.

### C74. Upload channel and start handshake (`internal/upload`)

**C74.1 URLs.**
```go
type Signer struct{ /* UploadKey */ }
func NewSigner(secretKey []byte) *Signer // key = HMAC-SHA256(secretKey, "metamusic/upload-url/v1")
func (s *Signer) URL(publicURL, uploadID, takeID, artifact string, maxBytes, exp int64) string
func (s *Signer) Verify(uploadID, takeID, artifact string, maxBytes, exp int64, sig string) bool
func (s *Signer) StartURL(publicURL, attemptID, takeID string, exp int64) string // artifact field = "start", max_bytes = 0
func (s *Signer) VerifyStart(attemptID, takeID string, exp int64, sig string) bool
```
`<PublicURL>/worker/v1/uploads/<upload_id>?exp=<unix>&sig=<b64url>`; `sig =
base64url_nopad(HMAC-SHA256(UploadKey, "v1|" + upload_id + "|" + take_id + "|" + artifact + "|" +
max_bytes + "|" + exp))` (43 chars, compared with `hmac.Equal`); the start URL
`<PublicURL>/worker/v1/attempts/<attempt_id>/start?exp=…&sig=…` signs `"v1|" + attempt_id + "|" +
take_id + "|start|0|" + exp`. `exp = window_ends_at = submit_time + TTL + timeout_s + KillSlackS + 15 min`
(design §3.4). Only `upload_id`, artifact, `max_bytes` and `exp` are persisted (`uploads`); URLs exist
only in the provider request and are never logged, stored in `job_events`, or returned by any operation
(`TestNoSecretLeak`: `get_job`, `list_jobs`, `get_batch`, job events, audit, slog).

**C74.0 Service** (frozen in M3-B2; `upload.NewService(o ServiceOptions)`, `Data *os.Root, Signer,
Store, Clock, NewID, PublicURL, Wake func()`):
```go
func (s *Service) OpenAttempt(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, job JobRef, attemptID string, artifacts map[string]int64, exp int64, lease LeaseFence) error // uploads rows 'open'
func (s *Service) Mint(job JobRef, attemptID string, exp int64) (uploads map[string]provider.UploadTarget, startURL string, err error) // in memory only
func (s *Service) Received(ctx context.Context, q Querier, attemptID string) ([]Received, error)
func (s *Service) Consume(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, uploadIDs []string) error         // 'received' ⇒ 'consumed'
func (s *Service) CloseAttempt(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, attemptID string) error      // 'open' ⇒ 'closed'
func (s *Service) RemoveConsumedFiles(ctx context.Context, uploadIDs []string) error         // after the take commits
func (s *Service) SweepFiles(ctx context.Context, now time.Time) (removed int, err error)    // C68 24 h rule
func (s *Service) Handler() http.Handler      // PUT /worker/v1/uploads/{id}
func (s *Service) StartHandler() http.Handler // POST /worker/v1/attempts/{id}/start
type Received struct{ UploadID, Artifact, SHA256 string; Bytes int64; Path string /* relative to Data */ }
type JobRef struct{ JobID, BatchID string }
```
Every method called on the runner's behalf (`OpenAttempt`, `Consume`, `CloseAttempt`) takes the
caller's `store.LeaseFence` (or runs in a transaction whose statements already carried it); the HTTP
handlers are not runner writes and never take the runner lease.

**C74.2 Handler** (`Handler(o Options) http.Handler`, mounted at `/worker/v1/uploads/` by httpserver;
ClassMachine — anonymous; the capability is the auth; problem bodies via `internal/problem`). Checks in
order:
1. method `PUT` (else 405, `Allow: PUT`); path `^/worker/v1/uploads/[0-9a-f-]{36}$` (else 404
   `NOT_FOUND`);
2. `exp` and `sig` present and well-formed; row lookup by id; MAC over the row's take id, artifact and
   max_bytes with the query `exp` — unknown id or bad MAC ⇒ 403 `UPLOAD_LINK_INVALID` (one message for
   both); `exp < now` ⇒ 403 `UPLOAD_LINK_EXPIRED`; `exp > row.exp` ⇒ 403 `UPLOAD_LINK_INVALID`;
3. job terminal, attempt `rejected`, or another attempt of the job already delivered a verified result
   ⇒ 410 `UPLOAD_CLOSED`;
4. `Content-Length` absent ⇒ 411 `LENGTH_REQUIRED`; `> max_bytes` ⇒ 413 `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE`;
   `Content-Digest` absent or not one `sha-256=:<44 base64 chars>:` ⇒ 400 `DIGEST_REQUIRED`;
5. row already `received`: digest equals the stored sha ⇒ 200 (body not read); differs ⇒ 409
   `UPLOAD_CONFLICT`;
6. a PUT for the same id in flight (in-process lock) ⇒ 409 `UPLOAD_IN_PROGRESS` (`Retry-After: 2`);
7. stream the body (`http.MaxBytesReader(max_bytes)`, read deadline 10 min via
   `http.ResponseController`) into `incoming/uploads/<id>.part` (`O_CREATE|O_EXCL`, 0600) while hashing;
   byte count ≠ `Content-Length` ⇒ 400 `LENGTH_MISMATCH`; sha ≠ digest ⇒ 422 `UPLOAD_DIGEST_MISMATCH`;
   both remove the part file;
8. fsync, `Chmod(0400)`, rename to `incoming/uploads/<id>`; `UPDATE uploads SET state='received',
   sha256=?, bytes=?, received_at=? WHERE id=? AND state='open'`; one `job_events` row `upload` with
   `{upload_id, artifact, bytes}`; 201 `{"upload_id","artifact","sha256","bytes"}`; `artifact ==
   "mm-result.json"` ⇒ `Wake()` (the injected, nil-safe func).
Logs: method, path without query, status, bytes — never `sig`/`exp`. Responses: `Cache-Control:
no-store`.

**C74.3 Start handshake** (`StartHandler`, mounted at `/worker/v1/attempts/` by httpserver; ClassMachine,
binary-route class like uploads, `mcp: exclude:binary`; D-M3-28). `POST
/worker/v1/attempts/<attempt_id>/start?exp&sig`, body `{"runpod_job_id": "<id>"}` (≤ 1 KiB,
`[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,128}`), checks in order:
1. method/path/MAC/expiry exactly as C74.2 steps 1–2 (`ATTEMPT_LINK_INVALID` / `_EXPIRED`, 403);
2. **410 `ATTEMPT_CLOSED`** when the job is terminal, has a cancel intent, another attempt delivered a
   verified result, or this attempt is `rejected`/`done` — the handler must not render (and stops if it
   already does: the `cancelled` callback re-polls this URL at most every 15 s);
3. one statement records the observation (the handler never changes attempt or job state — D-M3-3's
   single writer): `UPDATE job_attempts SET announced_at = COALESCE(announced_at, ?), announced_ref =
   COALESCE(announced_ref, ?) WHERE id = ? AND state IN ('pending','submitted','unknown')`, plus a job
   event `start`; an earlier `announced_ref` or a `ref` that differs from the body ⇒ 409
   `ATTEMPT_REF_CONFLICT` + event (both ids are then treated as live; a provider requeue onto a new id
   is cancelled per C73.2 rule 6);
4. 200 `{"continue": true}`; `Wake()`.
On its next step the runner promotes the observation (lease predicate): a `pending` or ref-less
`unknown` attempt gets `ref = announced_ref`, state `submitted` (a late ref: the ghost becomes a normal
attempt that is polled and can be cancelled), and a job still `queued` because of that ghost moves
`queued → starting` (row 8b, `ghost_started`), so its pending resubmission is no longer claimable. The
provider ref arrives only in this body and is stored exactly like a `/run` ref (never returned, logged
or put in events). Logs as C74.2. `TestUpload_StartHandshake` (matrix: 403/410/409/200, repeated calls
idempotent, observation only), `TestRunner_PromotesAnnouncedGhost` (late ref on an `unknown` attempt;
`queued → starting`), `TestChaos_GhostAdoptedViaStart` (5xx on `/run`, the ghost calls start ⇒ promoted,
no second execution, the take completes from the ghost, the unused `topup_ambiguous` stays outstanding
until reconciliation).

### C75. Jobs: state machine, attempts, runner, ingest bridge (`internal/jobs`)

**C75.1 States.** `awaiting_confirmation`, `queued`, `submitting`, `starting`, `generating`,
`transferring`, `ingesting`, `completed` (outcome `clean` | `truncated`), `cancelling`, `cancelled`,
`failing`, `failed`, `expired`. Terminal: `completed`, `failed`, `cancelled`, `expired`. **Invariant
(design §3.7):** `failed` and `cancelled` are reachable only when no attempt of the job can still spend:
every attempt is `rejected` or `done` (provider-terminal, or its window has closed).

**C75.2 Transition table** (`jobs.Transitions`; equal to `job_transitions`, `TestTransitions_GoEqualsSQL`;
every row has a `TestTransition_*` case, every non-row pair a refusal case in
`TestTransitions_IllegalRefused`, both through the Go guard and the SQL trigger. Because inserts are
limited to `awaiting_confirmation`/`queued`, the refusal test reaches every source state through a
legal path of this table first, and for a terminal source expects the `jobs: terminal jobs are
immutable` message, not `illegal transition`). Lettered rows were added in revision 2; the pair list is
the 34 rows of the DDL:

| # | From | To | Event | Guard / effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | awaiting_confirmation | queued | `approved` | approval `approved`; `queued_at = now` |
| 2 | awaiting_confirmation | cancelled | `cancel` / `rejected` | `CANCELLED_BY_USER` / `REJECTED`; release handled by the op's transaction |
| 3 | awaiting_confirmation | expired | `approval_expired` | `APPROVAL_EXPIRED` |
| 4 | queued | submitting | `claim` | C69 claim SQL: lease predicate, active backend, armed, not tripped, quote ≤ 1 h old, preflight passed (D-M3-33), live attempts of the backend < `min(live workers.max, batch.workers_max)`, `next_attempt_at ≤ now`; `attempt_overhead` reserved by `ReserveTopUp` (refused ⇒ row 6 `BUDGET_EXCEEDED`); inserts attempt + uploads |
| 5 | queued | cancelled | `cancel` | no live attempt (`pending`/`submitted`/`unknown`) |
| 5b | queued | cancelling | `cancel` | a live attempt exists (a ghost of an earlier submit): `Cancel` for every attempt with a ref; the job stays `cancelling` until every attempt is terminal (ghosts by their time bound, or earlier through their start announcement + `Cancel`) |
| 6 | queued | failed | `no_capacity` / `budget` / `price` / `not_submitted` | `NO_CAPACITY` (now − `first_submitted_at` ≥ 24 h, or `queue_expiries` would exceed 6), `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` (a reserve refused), `PRICE_CHANGED`, `NOT_SUBMITTED` (`first_submitted_at IS NULL` and `queued_at ≤ now − 24 h`: never claimed, e.g. disarmed or no capacity for the batch's earlier takes); no live attempt; execution pot released `never_ran` per C73.2 |
| 6b | queued | failed | `batch_no_capacity` | another job of the same batch just failed `NO_CAPACITY`: every job of the batch with `first_submitted_at IS NULL` fails `NO_CAPACITY` in the same transaction (`never_ran`; audit `runner:fail_remainder`) |
| 7 | queued, submitting, starting, generating | transferring | `result_received` | a received, verified `mm-result.json` with `status: "ok"` of any attempt (D-M3-2); an error `mm-result.json` is only an event; other live attempts get `Cancel` |
| 8 | submitting | starting | `submitted` | `/run` 2xx; `attempt.ref` persisted, attempt `submitted` |
| 8b | queued | starting | `ghost_started` | an `unknown` attempt of the job was promoted by its start announcement (C74.3) |
| 9 | submitting | queued | `submit_retry` | 429 / 409 `ENDPOINT_PAUSED` / 404 endpoint not found (attempt `rejected`, basis `not_accepted`, its `attempt_overhead` released `not_accepted`; the 404 also trips `provider_access`) or 5xx / network / timeout (attempt `unknown` + `topup_ambiguous` via `ReserveTopUp`; refused ⇒ the requeued job fails `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` through row 6 once the ghost's window has closed, unless its result completes the take first); `next_attempt_at = now + backoff` (429: `max(backoff, Retry-After)`) |
| 10 | submitting | failed | `submit_refused` | 401/403 (`PROVIDER_AUTH`; also trips `provider_access`), 403 1010 (`EDGE_BLOCKED`), 400/422 (`SUBMIT_REJECTED`); attempt `rejected`; `not_accepted` + `never_ran` releases |
| 11 | submitting | cancelling | `cancel` | after `/run` returned a ref |
| 12 | starting | generating | `running` | status `Running`, or the attempt announced itself (start handshake); `started_at`, attempt `in_progress_seen = 1` |
| 13 | starting | queued | `queue_expired` | C73.2 rule 4/5 queue expiry, or still `Queued` at `submitted_at + TTL + 5 min` (then `Cancel` first and wait for a terminal status); `queue_expiries + 1` (the 7th ⇒ row 6 `NO_CAPACITY` instead); backoff. No top-up here: the next attempt reserves its own overhead at claim, under the batch ceiling |
| 14 | starting, generating, transferring | cancelling | `cancel` / `trip` | `Cancel(ref)` issued; `CANCELLED_BY_USER` / `PROVIDER_TRIPPED` |
| 15 | starting, generating | failing | `deadline` / `lost` / `provider_requeue` | runner deadline passed (C75.4); `NotFound` with evidence of a run (C73.2 rule 5: attempt `unknown`); a provider requeue (rule 6); `Cancel(ref)` issued |
| 16 | starting, generating | failed | `provider_failed` / `provider_cancelled` | provider-terminal `Failed`/`TimedOut` (`WORKER_ERROR`, `WORKER_LOST`, `DEADLINE`) or `Cancelled` with no cancel intent (`PROVIDER_CANCELLED`: cancelled outside the app, e.g. a console cancel or `purge-queue`), and **no other live attempt**; settlement per C73.2 (no "after Running" guard: whether it ran comes from the record) |
| 16b | starting, generating | failing | `provider_failed` / `provider_cancelled` | as row 16 while **another attempt is still live**: `Cancel` it; the job ends through row 24 |
| 17 | generating | transferring | `completed_remote` | status `Completed` (the result is read from the output and/or `mm-result.json`) |
| 18 | transferring | ingesting | `verified` | every reported artifact received with matching sha; required artifacts present; truncation dict valid |
| 19 | transferring | failed | `artifacts_bad` | 60 s after `Completed` still missing/mismatched ⇒ `ARTIFACT_MISSING` / `ARTIFACT_MISMATCH`; worker contract violation ⇒ `WORKER_CONTRACT`; no other live attempt |
| 19b | transferring | failing | `artifacts_bad` | as row 19 while another attempt is still live (`Cancel` it first) |
| 20 | ingesting | completed | `ingested` | take transaction committed (C75.5); outcome `truncated` iff `any(truncated)` |
| 21 | ingesting | failed | `ingest_failed` / `duplicate_audio` | `INGEST_FAILED` (`details.ingest_code` = M1 C15 code); `DUPLICATE_AUDIO` (`details.track_id`, D-M3-32) |
| 22 | cancelling | cancelled | `cancel_confirmed` | every attempt terminal (provider `Cancelled`/other terminal, or window closed: `terminal_basis = time_bound`) |
| 23 | cancelling, failing | transferring | `cancel_lost_race` | a verified `mm-result.json` (`status: "ok"`) or a `Completed` status with a valid output arrived before the cancel took effect (the take is kept; job event) |
| 24 | failing | failed | `failure_confirmed` | every attempt terminal |

Resubmit backoff after attempt *k* (rows 9 and 13): `min(30 min, 1 min × 2^(k−1))`; the window is 24 h
from `first_submitted_at` and at most 6 queue expiries; past either the job fails `NO_CAPACITY` (row 6),
which also fails the batch's never-submitted jobs (row 6b). A queued job of a batch whose provider is
disarmed or tripped simply waits (no transition) until `NOT_SUBMITTED` (24 h after `queued_at`) releases
its reservation. `TestRunner_NoCapacityFailsBatchRemainder` (7 takes, `workers.max` 2, no capacity: the
first job hits `NO_CAPACITY` after 24 h and the 5 never-submitted ones fail with it, not ~4 × 24 h
later), `TestRunner_NotSubmittedAfter24h`.

**C75.3 Runner** (`runner.go`):
```go
type RunnerOptions struct {
    Store *store.Store; Backend provider.Backend; Engines engine.Registry
    Spend *spend.Service; Uploads *upload.Service; Ingest Ingester
    Notify notify.Notifier; Audit *audit.Store
    Clock clock.Clock; NewID func() string; Owner string /* random instance id */
    PublicURL string; TTL, MaxRuntime time.Duration; ColdStartS, KillSlackS int
    Expect provider.WorkerExpect; Log *slog.Logger
    IngestWorkers int                      // bounded pool for ingest + Ears, default 2 (design §3.2)
    Preflight func(ctx context.Context, inFlightMaxBytes int64) error // D-M3-33: disk + public upload route; nil in unit tests
    Fault func(point string) error         // test-only crash injection (C86.3); nil in production
}
func NewRunner(o RunnerOptions) *Runner
func (r *Runner) Run(ctx context.Context) error            // acquire/retry runner lease every 30 s; starts the heartbeat goroutine; ticks
func (r *Runner) Step(ctx context.Context) (StepReport, error) // one tick (tests drive it directly); never blocks on ingest
func (r *Runner) Drain(ctx context.Context) error          // waits until the ingest pool is idle (tests; shutdown)
func (r *Runner) Wake()                                    // non-blocking nudge; exposed to other packages only as an injected func()
func (r *Runner) Holding() bool                            // lease held right now (with ≥ 30 s left for network work)
```
**Concurrency.** The lease heartbeat runs in **its own goroutine** (every 30 s; it also renews the job
leases of every leased job, including those being ingested), so nothing inside `Step` can lose the
90 s lease. Ingest (`IngestTake`, up to 300 s for an MP3 encode) and `ears.AnalyzeTrack` run in a
bounded pool of `IngestWorkers` (2); `Step` hands a verified job to the pool and returns; the pool's
transactions carry the lease predicate like every runner write. Tests that need determinism call
`Step` then `Drain` (`TestRunner_StepDoesNotBlockOnIngest`, `TestRunner_HeartbeatDuringLongIngest`:
a 300 s stub ingest on the manual clock keeps the lease). **Fault points** (`Fault(point)` returning an
error aborts the step as a crash would, leaving exactly the committed state): `after_claim_commit`,
`after_submit_return`, `after_completed_seen`, `mid_settle`, `mid_ingest`, `after_cancel_post`,
`after_reserve_commit`.

Tick (every 1 s, or on `Wake`): move approved/rejected/expired batches' jobs (setting `queued_at`) →
promote announced attempts (C74.3) → apply cancel intents (awaiting ⇒ `cancelled`; queued without live
attempt ⇒ `cancelled`; queued with a ghost ⇒ `cancelling` (row 5b); live ⇒ `cancelling` + `Cancel`) →
claim and submit while capacity allows (C69 claim SQL; in-flight counts every `pending`, `submitted`
and `unknown` attempt of the active backend against `min(live workers.max, batch.workers_max)`) → poll
due attempts (intervals: `Queued` 5 s, `Running` 10 s, `transferring`/`cancelling`/`failing` 2 s; on 429
honour `Retry-After`/`RateLimit`) and classify (C73.2) → hand verified results to the ingest pool →
