settle terminal attempts (`SettleAttempt`, C72.5) → fail never-claimed jobs (`NOT_SUBMITTED`) and batch
remainders (row 6b) → finish batches (all jobs terminal, all attempts `done`/`rejected` and settled ⇒
overhead settle from the meter (C76.6) + `residual` row + `batches.state='finished'` + ntfy summary,
C82). **Every write of the tick carries the lease predicate**; `ErrLeaseLost` stops the tick and the
runner returns to acquiring (`TestRunnerLease_PausedRunnerCannotClaim`, synctest: runner A pauses for
120 s, runner B takes the lease and claims; A resumes mid-tick and its claim, submit-outcome and trip
statements affect 0 rows, A makes no network call, B's state is untouched).

**Claim-time checks** (all inside the claim transaction, no network): the provider of the **active
backend** is armed and not tripped; `jobs.backend` equals it (`TestRunner_NeverClaimsOtherBackend`:
jobs created under `fake` stay `queued` after `MM_BACKEND` changes and end `NOT_SUBMITTED`); the cached
quote (`provider_state.quote_at`) is ≤ `QuoteMaxAge` old and passes C77.5's fail-closed checks;
`Preflight` passed within the last 60 s (free disk under `DataDir` ≥ Σ `max_bytes` of the open uploads of
live attempts + this attempt + 1 GiB, and a `GET <PublicURL>/worker/v1/uploads/<random uuid>` answering
the expected 403 problem body through the public route; `TestRunner_NoClaimWhenDiskLow`,
`TestRunner_NoClaimWhenUploadRouteDown`). The live configuration is compared with the batch's frozen
quote: `workers.max` above the frozen value is ignored (the minimum wins, D-M3-14), and an `idleTimeout`
above the frozen value is **topped up**: the `attempt_overhead` reserve uses `max(live idle,
batch.idle_s)` (still under the batch ceiling) and the drift ntfy fires (C76.4).

Submit sequence for a claimed job (the crash windows are the point):
1. **Price check inside the claim transaction**, against the cached quote: a higher effective
   `PricePPH` than the batch's (or than the job's last top-up) ⇒ a `topup_price` reserve via
   `ReserveTopUp` in the same transaction; refused ⇒ the job is not claimed and goes `queued → failed`
   (`PRICE_CHANGED`, row 6). Then the claim itself: `attempt_overhead` reserve, attempt `pending` (with
   the price in force) + uploads `open`, committed **before** any network call, with the lease
   predicate and ≥ 30 s of lease left (`ErrLeaseShort` ⇒ no submit this tick).
2. Mint upload and start URLs in memory; `Submit` (`/run` timeout 60 s, never replayed: C77.4).
3. Outcome transaction (lease predicate): ref persisted + attempt `submitted` + job `starting` (row 8),
   or rows 9/10.
A crash after step 1 and before step 3 leaves a `pending` attempt with no ref: adoption (C76.1) turns it
into `unknown` with a `topup_ambiguous` reservation (or `BUDGET_EXCEEDED`) and requeues the job; if the
render starts, its start announcement promotes it (C74.3).

**C75.4 Runner deadlines (design §3.8 layer 4):** `submitting` 2 min; `starting` TTL + 5 min (row 13);
`generating` `timeout_s + KillSlackS` + 2 min since `started_at` (row 15, `DEADLINE`); `transferring` 60 s
after `Completed` (row 19) or 15 min since entering when no `Completed` was seen yet; `ingesting` 10 min
(`INGEST_FAILED`, `ingest_code = TIMEOUT`); `cancelling`/`failing`: `Backend.Cancel` (one POST, returns
after the provider's acknowledgement) is **re-issued every 30 s** by the runner while `Poll` does not
show a terminal status; after 5 min without confirmation a high-priority ntfy; the job stays
non-terminal until confirmation or the attempt windows close (time bound; a `submitted` attempt closed
this way settles `execution_unknown`, C72.5). `state_deadline_at` records the current deadline.
`TestRunner_CancelReissuedEvery30s` (the backend never confirms: one POST per 30 s, no wait inside
`Cancel`).

**C75.5 Ingest bridge** (`ingest.go`; implements `Ingester`):
```go
type Ingester interface {
    IngestTake(ctx context.Context, job JobRow, att AttemptRow, files []upload.Received, out provider.Output) (trackID string, err error)
}
func NewIngestBridge(o BridgeOptions) *IngestBridge // Data *os.Root, Pipeline *ingest.Pipeline, Library *library.Service, Ears *ears.Service, Lineage *lineage.Service, Store, Clock, NewID
```
Steps: verify output ↔ uploads (sha, bytes, required set per C73.4, `truncated` is an object with
exactly `abc` and `semantic` booleans and equals `result.json`'s `truncated`, else `WORKER_CONTRACT`);
open the received files through the Root (`incoming/uploads/<id>`) as `fs.File`s; **allocate a fresh
UUIDv7 `TrackID` for every ingest attempt** (never the job id: M1 C15 step 1 needs a work dir that does
not exist yet and step 14 fails `EEXIST` ⇒ `IO` on an existing `takes/<TrackID>`); `ingest.Pipeline.Ingest`
(M1 C15 unchanged: `Source` = `audio.flac`, `Artifacts` = every other received artifact incl.
`mm-result.json`, `Title`, `Slug = yue2.Slug(title)`, `RecipeID`, `RequestID`, `Seed`, `RecordedAt =
now`); M1's audio-identity check finding an existing track (`tracks_audio_identity`) ⇒ `ingest.Discard`,
job `failed DUPLICATE_AUDIO` with `details.track_id` (row 21, D-M3-32), the attempt settled normally;
on success one transaction (lease predicate): env fingerprint (`INSERT … ON CONFLICT(env_id) DO
NOTHING`), RTF update, `library.InsertTake` with `NewTake{Origin: "job", ImportRule: "job",
StatusSetBy: "system:job", JobID, EnvID}` (D-M3-35; never `Reference` or `Lossy`: `ingest.Request.Reference` stays
false and the worker returns FLAC only, D-M2c-11), parent/root/generation from `jobs.source_track_id` (root
⇒ `root = id`, `generation 0`), the Operator Ledger row (C71.5, mutation `JobID` and `RecipePatchJSON`),
the score (`source='job'`) and sections (lyrics; plan when aligned), tag inheritance (M2a), draft
fulfilment (C69; `InsertTake` itself skips the importer's fulfilment for `origin='job'`), uploads →
`consumed`, job → `completed` with `track_id`, `env_id`, `audio_s`, truncation, `outcome`; on a DB error ⇒
`ingest.Discard` and row 21. After commit: `RemoveConsumedFiles`; `ears.AnalyzeTrack(ctx, trackID,
ears.AnalyzeOptions{})` (warnings only, job event `ears`); notify nothing per take (batch summary only).
A job retried after a crash between the ingest and the transaction re-ingests from the still-present
uploads under a **new** `TrackID`; files the crashed attempt left under `takes/<old TrackID>/` or
`audio/masters/` are reported by M1's `library_orphans` (D-M1-10) and its `incoming/ingest-*` work dir is
swept by M1's rule (`TestIngestBridge_RetryAfterCrashNewTrackID`, `TestIngestBridge_DuplicateAudio`).

**C75.6 Env fingerprint** (`env.go`): `gpu_class` = normalised GPU name (`NVIDIA RTX A5000` ⇒ `A5000`,
`NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090` ⇒ `RTX 3090`, `NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090` ⇒ `RTX 4090`, `NVIDIA L4` ⇒ `L4`,
unknown ⇒ the name without the `NVIDIA ` prefix); `env_id` per D-M3-26 over `canonjson.Canonical`;
`image_digest` = `attempt.image_digest`, filled before the attempt turns terminal from the worker record
matching the status's `workerId` (its `image` when it carries `@sha256:`), else `build:<worker_build>`
from the output (D-M3-26; fake: `sha256:` + 64 `0`s). `TestEnvID_Golden`, `TestGPUClass_Table`,
`TestEnv_ImageFromWorkerRecord` (an image release mid-batch: takes on old workers keep the old digest).

**C75.7 Events.** `event` ∈ `created`, `claim`, `submit`, `submitted`, `submit_retry`, `submit_refused`,
`status`, `running`, `queue_expired`, `resubmit`, `upload`, `result_received`, `completed_remote`,
`verified`, `ingested`, `ears`, `cancel_requested`, `cancel_sent`, `cancel_confirmed`,
`cancel_lost_race`, `deadline`, `lost`, `failed`, `adopted`, `ghost`, `topup`, `settled`, `trip`,
`start`, `ghost_started`, `start_conflict`, `provider_requeue`, `provider_cancelled`, `unknown_status`,
`status_lost`, `duplicate_audio`, `not_submitted`, `batch_no_capacity`, `preflight_failed`.
`detail_json` keys are allowlisted per event (`TestEvents_DetailAllowlist`): never `url`, `ref`, `sig`,
`exp`, `token`, `authorization`; provider error text only through `redact.String` and ≤ 500 bytes.

**C75.8 Reads and `wait_for_job`.** `jobs.Service` (`ListJobs`, `Job`, `ListBatches`, `Batch`,
`Wait`): `Wait(ctx, id, sinceState string, timeout time.Duration)` returns at once when the job's state
≠ `sinceState` or is terminal, else blocks on an in-process `Hub` notification or a 1 s DB re-check
(another process may run the runner), until `timeout` (0..55 s; design §4.1). Cancellation of the
request context returns promptly, and so does **server shutdown**: `Wait` also selects on the server's
`BaseContext` (cancelled when shutdown starts), returning the current job with `timed_out: true`, so
the 30 s drain never waits on long-polls (`TestWait_ReturnsOnShutdown`).

### C76. Reconciler: adoption, settlement, meter, trips, drift, billing, restore

`Reconciler.Pass(ctx)` runs at runner start and every 60 s, only while `Runner.Holding()`, and every
write carries the lease predicate (D-M3-30). The **sweep** part (`Backend.Sweep`: workers, endpoint,
`Date`-header skew) runs every **10 s** while any batch is `active` and until `finished_at + idle_s + 60 s`
of the last finished batch, else every 60 s (the canary and C76.4's post-batch check need fresh worker
counts). The actions listed in D-M3-22 write audit rows (principal `system:reconciler`, transport
`runner`). A pass also expires pending approvals older than 24 h (C72.6), refreshes the cached quote
(endpoint config and catalog list price; persisted in `provider_state.quote_json`/`quote_at`, never
used from the network by a request) — a failed refresh keeps the previous quote until it is
`QuoteMaxAge` old, after which estimates, creates and claims fail closed (`quote_stale`) — and
measures the clock skew from RunPod's `Date` header (C76.5).

**C76.1 Adoption.** C69 adoption SQL; then per adopted job: attempts `pending` (crash before `/run`
returned) ⇒ `unknown` + `ghost` event; if the job has no `submitted` attempt it goes back through row 9
(`topup_ambiguous`, refused ⇒ `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` via row 6 once the ghost window closed);
`submitted` attempts are polled by ref and classified per C73.2 (a 404 after downtime is `unknown`, not
a queue expiry). `TestAcceptance_Interrupt_ServerRestart` and `TestChaos_CrashBeforeRefPersisted`
cover both.

**C76.2 Attempt settlement.** Attempts `submitted` of terminal jobs keep being polled until
provider-terminal; `unknown` attempts become `done` (`time_bound`) when `window_ends_at` passes, and so
do `submitted` attempts whose status cannot be obtained (cancel never confirmed, `provider_access`
trip); every terminal attempt settles exactly once through `SettleAttempt` (C72.5: `execution` from the
record, or `execution_unknown` at `timeout_s + KillSlackS` for every time-bound close) and sets
`settled = 1` in the same transaction.

**C76.3 Trips** (design §3.8 layer 5). Sweep observations are kept per worker id (in memory for
timers; lifetimes in `worker_meter`). A worker's status falls back to the pod lifecycle when RunPod's
queue view is unavailable, so every worker-based condition needs **two consecutive observations**.
Trip conditions:
- `stuck_running`: more workers `RUNNING` (two consecutive sweeps ≥ 2 min apart) than our live attempts
  with evidence of running (seen `Running`, announced through the start handshake, or uploading) —
  workers whose job has announced itself are therefore never "stuck";
- `crash_loop`: a worker `INITIALIZING` or `UNHEALTHY` whose `uptimeSeconds` exceeds `ColdStartS + 60`
  in two consecutive sweeps (`uptimeSeconds` is null until placement, so unbilled image pulls never
  count) — at the defaults ~4 min of one worker instead of 10 min of two;
- `meter_exceeds_reserved`: C76.6;
- `provider_access`: 401, 403 or 1010 on `Poll`, `Cancel`, `Sweep`, `PurgeQueue` or `Billing`, or
  `ErrEndpointNotFound`; the affected attempts are closed by their time bound (C76.2) and the ntfy is
  **critical**;
- `spend_without_jobs`, `billed_exceeds_reserved`: from billing reconciliation and late adjustments
  (C76.5);
- `provisional_exceeds_reserved`: an `excess` settle row was written, or a `topup_requeue` was refused.
Trip actions, in order, each recorded in `provider_trips.actions_json`: insert the trip row (one open
trip per backend); every live attempt → `Cancel` (jobs → `cancelling` with `PROVIDER_TRIPPED`);
`PurgeQueue`; with `Capabilities().ScaleToZero` ⇒ `ScaleToZero` (PATCH `workers.max = 0`); **any**
`ScaleToZero` error (incl. a 401/403 after a key rotation, even when the embedded
`key-capabilities.json` claims PATCH is allowed) or no capability ⇒ **critical** ntfy "Set max workers
to 0 in the RunPod console" with `RunPodKeys.ConsoleURL` (C82); always a high-priority ntfy. No create
or claim happens while a trip is open (C72.4 step 1, C75.3). Only a human resets it
(`update_provider_state` / CLI `runpod reset-trip`). After a scale-to-zero, `workers.max` is restored
only by the operator (`runpod provision --activate` with a re-provided admin key, or the console),
because the admin key file is deleted after activation (runbook, C85). The fake backend's
`ErrNotPermitted` and `PatchFails` modes prove the fallback runs instead of a silent no-op
(`TestReconciler_TripWithoutWriteKeyAlerts`, `TestReconciler_ScaleToZeroErrorFallsBack`,
`TestReconciler_ProviderAccessTrips`, `TestReconciler_CrashLoopUsesUptime`,
`TestReconciler_StuckNeedsTwoObservations`).

**C76.4 Drift and scale-to-zero.** Desired endpoint config = `runpod.DesiredSpec` (C77.6) with the
operator's `workers.max` (0, 1 or 2). Drift-checked fields (`TestDrift_FieldList`): `type`, scaler type
and `queueDelay`, `timeout` (= `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME` in ms), `gpu.count`, `gpu.minCudaVersion` (and an
empty `allowedCudaVersions`), `gpu.pools` + `gpu.excludedTypes` (compared as one selection),
`flashboot`, `workers.min` (0), `workers.max` (≤ 2), `workers.idleTimeout` (present and ≤ 60), the image
digest, the worker env (pinned revisions; `MM_WORKER_DEADLINE` absent), and `requestUrls` (host = the
jobs host the client uses). Drift with `RevertDrift` capability ⇒
revert (a PATCH that sends `pools` **always** sends `excludedTypes` too — sending `pools` alone clears the
exclusions) + ntfy; without ⇒ ntfy only (design §3.8). A raised `idleTimeout` or `workers.max` relative
to a live batch's frozen quote is handled at claim time (C75.3) and named in the ntfy. After every
finished batch, at `finished_at + idle_s + 30 s`: any worker not terminal while no attempt is live ⇒
high-priority ntfy `WORKERS_NOT_ZERO` (design layer 7).

**C76.5 Billing reconciliation (quiet windows, D-M3-5).** With `Capabilities().Billing == "hour"`:
1. **Fetch** (at most every 15 min): `GET /v2/billing/serverless?serverlessId=<ep>&bucketSize=hour&
   startTime=<S>&endTime=<E>` with hour-aligned `S = min(last reconciled window_end − 72 h, oldest
   unreconciled batch's created_at floored to the hour)` and `E` = the start of the current hour —
   **never** the in-progress bucket (so never `lastN`, which ends with it). Granularity is a query
   parameter, not an account property: `bucketSize=hour` is always sent. Amounts are decoded with
   `UseNumber` and `ParseUSD` of the literal; each bucket whose `{total, gpu+cpu, disk}` differs from its
   latest `billing_buckets` observation appends a new observation.
2. **Skew guard:** when `|skew|` from the last `Date` header exceeds `SkewLimit` (30 s), no window closes
   and no residual is released; a high-priority ntfy names the skew (`TestBilling_PausesOnSkew`).
3. **Late adjustments:** for every already-reconciled window with `window_end > now − 72 h`, `delta = Σ
   latest bucket totals − (its provisional + Σ its earlier bill rows)`; `delta ≠ 0` ⇒ a `bill` row
   `late_adjust` (`window_start` of that window, so it counts in that window's month); when the window's
   latest compute total now exceeds its `reserved_micro_usd` ⇒ trip `billed_exceeds_reserved`
   (`TestBilling_LateIncreaseAdjustsAndTrips`).
4. **Window end** `B` = the latest bucket end such that `B + MM_BILLING_LAG ≤ now`; every batch
   created before `B` **that has at least one submitted attempt** is closed (`finished_at IS NOT NULL`)
   with `finished_at + idle_s + 120 s ≤ B`; no batch was created in `[B − 120 s, B)`; and `[last_end,
   B)` does not cross a calendar-month boundary of the process time zone (if it would, `B` = the first
   bucket end at or after that boundary, and a bucket belongs to the month containing its start).
   Batches that never reached the provider (rejected, expired, all jobs `NOT_SUBMITTED`) never block a
   window. No such `B` ⇒ wait.
5. Window = buckets in `[last_end, B)`; its batches = unreconciled closed batches created before `B`;
   `billed` = Σ `total`, `billed_compute` = Σ `gpu + cpu`; `provisional` = Σ `settle` rows of those
   batches; `reserved` = Σ `reserve` rows of those batches.
6. Verdict (compute only — disk is recorded, never tripped on): `billed_compute > reserved` ⇒
   `billed_exceeds_reserved` (trip); a bucket with compute > 0 overlapping no batch activity window
   `[created_at, finished_at + idle_s + 120 s]` ⇒ `spend_without_jobs` (trip); else `ok`.
7. One transaction (lease predicate): `billing_windows` row; one `bill` row (`billed − provisional`,
   `window_start`, principal `system:billing`, reason `reconcile`); for each batch a `release`
   (`reconciled`) of its outstanding; `batches.reconciled_at = now`.
8. No quiet window closed for 72 h while a finished batch is unreconciled ⇒ one high-priority ntfy per
   72 h ("billing reconciliation stalled") naming the blocking batch ids.
With `Billing == "none"`: batches release at `finished_at + ResidualHold` (`hold_elapsed`, one
transaction per batch) and a weekly low-priority ntfy "billing not reconciled" lists the count; the
meter's overhead settlement (C76.6) is then the final figure. `TestBilling_QuietWindowNeverOvershoots`
(a job straddling a bucket boundary: the window waits; the property holds at every commit),
`TestBilling_NeverReadsInProgressBucket`, `TestBilling_WindowNeverCrossesMonth`,
`TestBilling_RejectedBatchDoesNotBlock`.

**C76.6 Worker-lifetime meter** (design §3.3 "init and idle seconds attributed from /workers"). Every
sweep upserts one `worker_meter` row per worker whose `uptimeSeconds` and `startedAt` are non-null
(`worker_key` = HMAC of the id; C69 upsert). A lifetime's billed seconds `L` = its largest
`uptime_s`, plus one sweep interval once it has vanished from a sweep (conservative). Attempts record
the `worker_key` of their status's `workerId`. **Attribution:** a lifetime belongs to the unreconciled
batch of the earliest attempt that ran on it; a lifetime that served no attempt belongs to the batch
that was active at its `started_at` (earliest created), else it is unattributed. `overhead_s(B)` = Σ over
B's lifetimes of `max(0, L − Σ execution seconds of the attempts on it)`, where a running attempt counts
`now − started_at` (capped at `timeout_s + KillSlackS`) and a settled one its settled seconds.
- **Trip `meter_exceeds_reserved`** (every pass): `Σ_B (execution settles + ForSeconds(overhead_s(B),
  B.price)) + ForSeconds(unattributed L since the oldest unreconciled batch, max price) > Σ reserves of
  the unreconciled batches` — an overrun is detected within ~60 s instead of after the billing lag.
- **Overhead settlement:** the runner finishes a batch only when every lifetime attributed to it has
  ended or its last attempt ended ≥ `idle_s + 60 s` ago; it then writes `settle overhead =
  min(ForSeconds(overhead_s(B), B.price), Σ B's attempt_overhead reserves)`. No worker ever seen ⇒ 0.
  The unused part stays outstanding until reconciliation or the residual hold.
`TestReconciler_MeterTrip` (fake cold start 2 × `ColdStartS`: trip within one pass after the overrun),
`TestSettle_OverheadFromMeter`, `TestBatch_NothingRanSettlesZeroOverhead`, `TestMeter_WarmWorkerTwoBatches`
(attributed to the earlier batch only).

**C76.7 Restore (amends M2a C34; D-M3-31).** `metamusic restore --from SNAPSHOT --into DIR`, after
verifying the snapshot, opens the restored database and in one transaction: every `provider_state` row
`armed = 0`, `reason = 'restored'`, `changed_by = 'system:cli'`, `restored_at = now`; every `pending` or
`submitted` attempt → `unknown` (they are settled by their time bound, never assumed lost or finished);
audit `cli:restore-disarm`. Arming (`update_provider_state`, `runpod arm`) is refused with 409
`RECONCILE_REQUIRED` until `MAX(billing_windows.window_end) ≥ restored_at` (one reconciliation has
covered snapshot → now); with billing `none` only `runpod arm --after-restore --reason TEXT` (CLI,
explicit acknowledgment, audited) can arm. `TestRestore_DisarmsAndRequiresReconcile`.

### C77. RunPod v2 client and serverless backend (`internal/provider/runpod`)

**C77.1 Hosts and auth.** Management: `https://api.runpod.io` (paths carry `/v2/…`). Jobs:
`https://api.runpod.ai/v2/{endpointId}/…`. Every request: `Authorization: Bearer <key>` (the key chosen
per call, C77.3), `User-Agent: metamusic/<version> (+https://github.com/nicolasacchi/metamusic)`,
`Accept: application/json`; timeouts: `/run` **60 s** (short timeouts create ghosts under load), billing
30 s, everything else 15 s; bodies sent ≤ `MaxRunBody` (2 MiB) for `/run` and ≤ 64 KiB otherwise, ≤ 1 MiB
read (`ErrResponseTooLarge`). Every response's `Date` header updates the measured clock skew (C76.5).
**`/run` is never replayable:** it never carries `Idempotency-Key` or `X-Idempotency-Key` (Go's
transport treats a request with either header as idempotent and may resend it on a reused connection)
and its `Request.GetBody` is nil (`TestClient_RunNotReplayable`: the server drops the connection after
reading the body ⇒ exactly one `/run` reached it and the error is `Ambiguous()`).

**C77.2 Surface** (≈ 450 lines; `client.go`, `jobs.go`, `mgmt.go`, `errors.go`, `ratelimit.go`):
```go
type ClientOptions struct {
    MgmtBase, JobsBase string // tests point both at runpodtest
    HTTP *http.Client; UserAgent string; Keys config.RunPodKeys; Admin *config.AdminKey
    Clock clock.Clock; Recorder Recorder /* nil ⇒ none */
}
func NewClient(o ClientOptions) *Client
// Job API (jobs key)
func (c *Client) Run(ctx context.Context, input json.RawMessage, p Policy) (RunResult, error) // POST   /v2/{ep}/run
func (c *Client) Status(ctx context.Context, id string) (JobStatus, error)                    // GET    /v2/{ep}/status/{id}  (id, status, delayTime, executionTime, workerId, output, error)
func (c *Client) Cancel(ctx context.Context, id string) (JobStatus, error)                    // POST   /v2/{ep}/cancel/{id}
func (c *Client) PurgeQueue(ctx context.Context) error                                        // POST   /v2/{ep}/purge-queue
func (c *Client) Health(ctx context.Context) (Health, error)                                  // GET    /v2/{ep}/health
// (no Retry: POST /v2/{ep}/retry/{id} requeues a failed job outside the ledger — never implemented)
// Management API
func (c *Client) GetEndpoint(ctx context.Context, k KeyKind) (Endpoint, error)                // GET    /v2/serverless/{ep} (incl. requestUrls)
func (c *Client) PatchEndpoint(ctx context.Context, k KeyKind, p EndpointPatch) (Endpoint, error) // PATCH /v2/serverless/{ep}
func (c *Client) ListEndpoints(ctx context.Context, k KeyKind) ([]Endpoint, error)            // GET    /v2/serverless
func (c *Client) CreateEndpoint(ctx context.Context, s EndpointSpec) (Endpoint, error)        // POST   /v2/serverless (admin key only)
func (c *Client) Workers(ctx context.Context) ([]Worker, error)                               // GET    /v2/serverless/{ep}/workers (id, status, isStale, image, startedAt, uptimeSeconds, gpuTypeId)
func (c *Client) CatalogGPUs(ctx context.Context, k KeyKind) ([]GPUType, error)               // GET    /v2/catalog/gpus (bearer required: 401 without)
func (c *Client) BillingServerless(ctx context.Context, q BillingQuery) ([]BillingRecord, error) // GET /v2/billing/serverless?serverlessId&bucketSize=hour&startTime&endTime
func (c *Client) Balance(ctx context.Context) (money.MicroUSD, bool, error)                   // POST https://api.runpod.io/graphql {myself{clientBalance}} (D-M3-20)
```
Exact JSON field names for every management request and response are taken from the committed snapshot
`internal/provider/runpod/testdata/openapi.snapshot.json` (the public `https://api.runpod.io/v2/openapi.json`,
fetched 2026-10-12) and pinned by `TestClient_ManagementFieldsInSnapshot`: the client declares a table
of `{operation, JSON path}` it reads or writes and the test resolves every entry in the snapshot's
schemas. Facts from that snapshot that the plan relies on: `CreateEndpointRequest` requires `name`,
`type` and `scaling`; `scaling` is a union on `type` (`QUEUE_DELAY` + `queueDelay` ≥ 0.5, or
`REQUEST_COUNT` + `requestCount`); `workers.idleTimeout` is rejected and omitted for queue endpoints
scaling on `REQUEST_COUNT`; endpoint `timeout` is the "per-request execution timeout in milliseconds"
(create default 300 000); `workers.max` defaults to 3 on create; `gpu.minCudaVersion` (`"12.8"`) is
mutually exclusive with a non-empty `gpu.allowedCudaVersions`; `gpu.excludedTypes` are catalog GPU
**type ids** that must lie inside the listed `pools` (else 400), and a PATCH that sends `pools` without
`excludedTypes` clears them; `gpu.count` defaults to 1; `flashboot` ∈ `OFF | FLASHBOOT |
PRIORITY_FLASHBOOT`; workers carry `image`, `isStale`, `startedAt` (null until started) and
`uptimeSeconds` (null until placed); a worker's status falls back to pod lifecycle when the queue view
is unavailable; container changes roll out gradually ("in-flight workers keep the previous version");
the endpoint carries `requestUrls`; billing records are `{startTime, endTime, serverlessId, totalAmount,
gpuAmount, cpuAmount, diskAmount, feeAmount}` doubles "inclusive of platform charges" (`feeAmount`
deprecated, always 0) over half-open hourly buckets; `bucketSize` is a query parameter (default `day`),
`startTime`/`endTime` are snapped to bucket bounds and `lastN` ends with the in-progress bucket; the
catalog `price.serverless` is a per-GPU **list** price (discounts not reflected; absent when the type is
in no serverless pool available to the caller); error bodies are `{title, status, detail, errors[]}`;
rate-limit headers are `RateLimit` and `RateLimit-Policy` plus `Retry-After`. The job API is not in that
snapshot; its shapes are pinned by the probe's spend-free recordings (C77.8) and the canary's redacted
fixtures (`testdata/fixtures/*.json`, C77.7) through `TestClient_JobAPIFixtures`. Until M3-X3 records
them, job API tests use the shapes documented by RunPod (`id`, `status`, `delayTime`, `executionTime`,
`workerId`, `output`, `error`) and the fixture test is skipped with `t.Skip("fixtures arrive in M3-X3")`
— X3 removes the skip.

**C77.3 Keys per call** (`KeyKind`): `jobs` for the job API; `read` for GET endpoint/workers/catalog/
billing/GraphQL; `write` for PATCH (ScaleToZero, RevertDrift) when present; `admin` **only** for `runpod
provision` and never constructed by any other command (`TestServe_NeverLoadsAdminKey`: `serve`'s client
has `Admin == nil` and `CreateEndpoint` returns `ErrNotPermitted`; `TestCLI_OnlyProvisionReadsAdminKey`).

**C77.4 Errors and rate limits** (`errors.go`):
```go
type Code string
const (EdgeBlocked Code = "EDGE_BLOCKED"; Unauthorized = "UNAUTHORIZED"; Forbidden = "FORBIDDEN"
    NotFound = "NOT_FOUND"; EndpointPaused = "ENDPOINT_PAUSED"; RateLimited = "RATE_LIMITED"
    BadRequest = "BAD_REQUEST"; Upstream = "UPSTREAM"; Network = "NETWORK"; Decode = "DECODE")
type Error struct{ Op string; Status int; Code Code; Title, Detail string; Errors []string; RetryAfter time.Duration }
func (e *Error) Error() string     // "runpod: <op>: <code> (<status>)[: <redacted detail ≤ 200>]" — no URL, no key
func (e *Error) Retryable() bool   // EndpointPaused, RateLimited, Upstream, Network
func (e *Error) Ambiguous() bool   // Upstream, Network or a client timeout on Run: the job may exist (D-M3-4)
func (e *Error) Access() bool      // EdgeBlocked, Unauthorized, Forbidden ⇒ provider.ErrAccess outside Run (C76.3)
func Classify(op string, resp *http.Response, body []byte, err error) *Error
```
Classification (`TestClassify_Table`): 403 with body containing `error code: 1010` ⇒ `EDGE_BLOCKED`
(non-retryable); 401 ⇒ `UNAUTHORIZED`; other 403 ⇒ `FORBIDDEN`; 404 ⇒ `NOT_FOUND` (the backend tells a
missing job from a missing endpoint with `/health`, C73.2); 409 whose `title`/`detail` contains `paused`
(case-insensitive) ⇒ `ENDPOINT_PAUSED`; 429 ⇒ `RATE_LIMITED` (`Retry-After` integer seconds, else the
smallest `t` of an exhausted window in `RateLimit`, else 5 s); other 4xx ⇒ `BAD_REQUEST`; 5xx ⇒
`UPSTREAM`; transport errors and client timeouts ⇒ `NETWORK` (the `*url.Error`'s URL is dropped: only
`Op` and the unwrapped cause survive, through `redact.Error`); undecodable 2xx ⇒ `DECODE`. Error bodies
`{title, status, detail, errors[]}` are parsed; `detail` and each `errors[]` entry pass `redact.String`
and are cut to 500 bytes in total. **Rate limits** follow the snapshot: `RateLimit: "minute";r=0;t=12,
"hour";r=2800;t=1812, …` (structured-field list: remaining `r`, seconds to reset `t` per window) and
`RateLimit-Policy: "minute";q=60;w=60, …`; when any window has `r == 0` the client waits its `t`
(bounded by the ctx) before the next request to that host. GETs (status, health, endpoint, workers,
catalog, billing) retry up to 3× on retryable codes with backoff 1 s/2 s/4 s (or `RetryAfter`); `Run`,
`Cancel`, `PurgeQueue`, `PatchEndpoint`, `CreateEndpoint` never retry inside the client (the
runner/reconciler decide).

**C77.5 Serverless backend** (`backend.go`): implements `provider.Backend` with the client. `Quote`
(reconciler only): `GetEndpoint` (read key) + `CatalogGPUs` (read key, cached 1 h; decoded with
`UseNumber`, `ParseUSD` of the `price.serverless` literal) ⇒ `ListPricePPH` = max list price over the
GPU types of the endpoint's pools minus its excluded types (× `gpu.count`), plus `Endpoint`,
`GPUClasses`. **Fail-closed checks** (`QuoteCheck(q, now) error`, applied when the quote is cached and
again at every request and claim; `TestBackend_QuoteFailsClosed`): `workers.max == 0` ⇒
`endpoint_inactive`; `type ≠ QUEUE`, scaler ≠ `QUEUE_DELAY` with the provisioned `queueDelay`,
`idleTimeout` absent, `workers.max > 2`, `gpu.count ≠ 1`, `timeout ≠ MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME` in ms, or a
`requestUrls` host ≠ the jobs host ⇒ `endpoint_config`; a missing or zero `price.serverless` for any
allowed type ⇒ `catalog_unreadable`; `quote_at` older than `QuoteMaxAge` ⇒ `quote_stale`; the read key
cannot GET the endpoint or list workers ⇒ `sweep_unreadable`. `Submit` = `Run` with C73.3 input and
policy; `Poll` = `Status` (+ `Health` on a 404, C73.2); `Cancel` = one `Cancel` POST, returns after the
acknowledgement (confirmation comes from later polls; the runner re-issues every 30 s, C75.4);
`Sweep` = `Workers` + `GetEndpoint` + drift (C76.4) + skew; 401/403/1010 ⇒ `ErrAccess`; `ScaleToZero`
and `RevertDrift` = `PatchEndpoint(write, …)` that **always sends complete nested objects** (`workers`
with `min`, `max`, `idleTimeout`; `gpu` with `pools`, `excludedTypes`, `count`, `minCudaVersion`;
`scaling` with `type` and `queueDelay`) because whether nested objects are merged or replaced is not
pinned (the PATCH description even lists `idleTimeout` under `scaling`), followed by a GET that must
show the intended values (else trip `endpoint_config` drift, critical ntfy); `Billing` =
`BillingServerless(serverlessId, bucketSize=hour, aligned startTime/endTime)` ⇒ `BillBucket{Total,
Compute = gpu + cpu, Disk}` via `UseNumber` + `ParseUSD` (round up); `Capabilities` from key presence and
`key-capabilities.json` (embedded copy; absent ⇒ conservative: no ScaleToZero/RevertDrift, billing
`none`). The endpoint ID never appears in logs, events, audit rows or API responses (`<endpoint>`
placeholder); `internal/redact` also replaces the configured endpoint id **literally** wherever it
appears (not only in `/v2/<id>/` paths; `TestRedact_EndpointIDLiteral`).

**C77.6 Provisioning** (`provision.go`, CLI C79; `runpod.DesiredSpec(cfg, weights, digest, maxWorkers,
catalog)`): the complete create body, every field pinned (`TestProvision_SpecFieldsInSnapshot`,
`TestProvision_CreateBodyValid` validates it against the snapshot's `CreateEndpointRequest`):
```json
{"name":"mm-yue2","type":"QUEUE",
 "scaling":{"type":"QUEUE_DELAY","queueDelay":240},
 "workers":{"min":0,"max":0,"idleTimeout":20},
 "gpu":{"count":1,"pools":["AMPERE_24","ADA_24"],"excludedTypes":["<catalog id of L4, only if its pool is listed>"],
        "minCudaVersion":"12.8"},
 "flashboot":"FLASHBOOT","timeout":900000,
 "image":"ghcr.io/nicolasacchi/metamusic-worker@sha256:<digest>",
 "env":{"MODE":"serverless","HF_HUB_OFFLINE":"1","MM_YUE2_3B_REVISION":"<weights.go>",
        "MM_YUE2_VAE_REVISION":"<weights.go>","MM_YUE2_VAE_DIR":"<from --vae-source>"}}
```
- **Scaler: `QUEUE_DELAY` with `queueDelay = ColdStartS + 60`** (240 s at the defaults). `REQUEST_COUNT`
  would make RunPod reject and omit `idleTimeout`, leaving the quote's `IdleS` undefined. A queue-delay
  scaler may start a second worker for a job that waits longer than `queueDelay`; with `queueDelay =
  ColdStartS + 60` that happens only when a cold start overruns its whole allowance — the same moment the
  `crash_loop` trip fires (C76.3) — and the meter detects it (C76.6). The canary measures how many workers
  start for a single job (M3-X3); M3-X4 revisits the choice.
- `workers.max`: 0 (inactive, default) or `--activate --max N` with N ∈ {1, 2} (default 2; the canary uses
  1); `idleTimeout` 20; `timeout` = `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME` in **milliseconds** (D-M3-16).
- `gpu`: `count 1`, pools `AMPERE_24` first and `ADA_24` as fallback (or `--pools AMPERE_24`), exclusions
  computed from the catalog (read with the admin key — the catalog answers 401 without a bearer token):
  the type whose name is `L4` is excluded by its catalog **id** only when its `pool` is in `pools`
  (otherwise the exclusion would be a 400); `minCudaVersion "12.8"`, no `allowedCudaVersions`.
- `flashboot: "FLASHBOOT"`; image by digest only (tags refused); env as above (`--vae-source
  image|cached|volume:<path>`, default `image`: D-M3-38).
Idempotent: list by name ⇒ create when absent, else diff and PATCH only differing top-level fields, each
nested object complete (C77.5); `--dry-run` prints the diff with the endpoint id redacted. Cached models
are set in the console when the snapshot has no field for them (`provision` prints the exact console
step). `TestProvision_IdempotentDiff`, `TestProvision_ExcludedTypeOnlyInsidePools`,
`TestBackend_EndpointTimeoutEqualsMaxRuntime` (the drift check flags `timeout ≠ MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME`, and
a quote with it fails closed).

**C77.7 Fixture recorder.** With `MM_RUNPOD_RECORD_DIR` set, every client exchange is written to
`<dir>/<seq>-<op>.json` as `{op, method, path_template, status, headers (allowlist: content-type,
ratelimit, ratelimit-policy, retry-after, date), request_body, response_body}` after redaction: endpoint
id ⇒ `<endpoint>`, job ids ⇒ `<job-N>`, worker ids ⇒ `<worker-N>`, any URL ⇒ its path without query
(upload and start URLs ⇒ `<upload-url>`/`<start-url>`), `input` objects ⇒ `{"redacted":true}`, any
string ≥ 32 chars of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` ⇒ `<redacted>`; no `Authorization` header is ever written.
`TestRecorder_Redacts` (canary keys, ids and URLs absent from the files). The operator reviews the
files, the gate scans them, and M3-X3 commits them under
`internal/provider/runpod/testdata/fixtures/`.

**C77.8 Probe** (`probe.go`, CLI `runpod probe`; never reads the admin key): `--schema` (no keys): fetch
the live `https://api.runpod.io/v2/openapi.json`, canonicalise, compare with the snapshot (sha and the
client's field table), report `cached_model_field`, `flashboot_values`, and verify the pinned facts of
C77.2 (`gpu.minCudaVersion`, the scaler union, `timeout` in ms). `--keys`: for each key kind present
(`jobs`, `read`, `write` from `runpod.env`) classify each call as `allowed` (2xx, or a 400/404/422 that
proves authentication passed), `forbidden` (401/403), `edge_blocked`, `error`:
- **Spend-free guard:** GET the endpoint first; `workers.max ≠ 0` ⇒ every `run` is `skipped: endpoint
  active`. `workers.max == 0` is **re-checked by a GET immediately before each `/run`**.
- Job API: `health`; `status(<random uuid>)` (records the job-not-found shape); `health` on a random
  endpoint id (records the endpoint-not-found shape); `cancel(<random uuid>)`; `retry(<random uuid>)`
  (records only whether the jobs key may call it — the app never implements `/retry`); `purge_queue`.
- `run` (inactive endpoint only): two jobs, each with `policy {executionTimeout: 5000, ttl: 10000}` and
  body `{"input":{"contract":"probe"}}`. Job A is **not** cancelled: `/status` is polled every 2 s for
  ~30 s to record the TTL-expiry terminal status, its `executionTime` and the HTTP code (`ttl_expiry`);
  job B is cancelled at once to record the cancelled-while-queued shape (`cancelled_shape`); then
  `purge_queue` and `health`. Together they pin C73.2 rules 4/5 (`never_ran_shapes`) before any spend.
- `endpoint_patch` (write and read keys): the write test is `{"name": <current name>}` only, with a GET
  before and after; **any** difference between the two GETs ⇒ abort with a critical message (the probe
  never sends `workers`, `scaling` or `gpu`: if nested objects were replaced, `workers.max` would fall
  back to its default 3 and activate the endpoint).
- `workers`, `catalog`, `billing` (`serverlessId`, `bucketSize=hour`, the last ≥ 24 h of the inactive
  endpoint: must sum to 0 — confirms nothing accrues with zero workers, spend-free), `graphql_balance`.
- Clock skew from the `Date` headers (`skew_ms`).
Output (`format: "mm.runpod-probe/2"`, written to `--out`, default
`internal/provider/runpod/key-capabilities.json`): keys ⇒ calls ⇒ verdict, schema facts, `ttl_expiry`,
`cancelled_shape`, `never_ran_shapes`, `job_not_found`, `endpoint_not_found`, `retry_allowed`,
`billing_readable`, `idle_billing_zero_24h`, `skew_ms`, and `recommendation` (`write_key_scoped`,
`fallback: "manual-max-0" | "patch"`, `vae: "second-cached-model" | "network-volume"` (informational; the decision is D-M3-38: image)). No key
material, endpoint id or account data. `TestProbe_ClassifiesAgainstFake` (runpodtest permission
modes), `TestProbe_NameOnlyPatch` (runpodtest replaces nested objects: the probe sends only `name` and
detects a changed GET), `TestProbe_RunRecheckedBeforeEachCall`, `TestProbe_RecordsQueueShapes`,
`TestKeyCapabilities_FileSafe`.

### C78. Fake backend (`internal/provider/fake`) and the HTTP fake (`runpodtest`)

**C78.1 In-process fake** (`provider.Backend`; used by `dev`, E2E, servertest rigs, acceptance and the
property suite):
```go
type Options struct {
    Clock clock.Clock; Speed Speed /* Fast: queue 0.2 s, run 1 s | Slow: run = d */
    Worker *SimWorker; PricePPH money.MicroUSD /* 690000 list */; WorkersMax int /* 2 */; IdleS int /* 20 */
    ColdStartS int /* 3 in fast mode; the property suite uses the measured canary value, C86.3 */
    KillLatencyMS int64 /* executionTime overshoot at a timeout kill; default from canary-measured.json */
    Permissions Permissions /* PatchForbidden, PatchFails, BillingForbidden, AccessRevoked */
    Adversarial Adversarial // C86.3 (b); zero value = the friendly fake used by dev/E2E
}
type Adversarial struct {
    NoWarmReuse, ExtraWorkersPerJob, CancelAckButKeepRunning, Requeue, PurgeResultsAfter30m bool
    AcceptThenFailRun float64 /* fraction of /run that 5xx/timeout after the job was accepted */
    RevisedBucketsAfter time.Duration; PriceRiseAt *time.Time; IdleDriftTo, MaxDriftTo int
}
func New(o Options) *Backend
func (b *Backend) Counters() Counters    // Submit, Poll, Cancel, PurgeQueue, ScaleToZero, PATCH calls
func (b *Backend) AccruedCost(t time.Time) money.MicroUSD             // ground truth: Σ worker lifetimes × price up to t (C86.3)
func (b *Backend) AccruedCostByBatch(t time.Time) map[string]money.MicroUSD
```
Simulated workers (≤ `WorkersMax`, or ≤ `WorkersMax` per job with `ExtraWorkersPerJob`): cold start
(init; `uptimeSeconds` null while "pulling", then counting) → execution (≤ `timeout_s`, killed at it like
RunPod `executionTimeout`, reported `executionTime` = actual + `KillLatencyMS`) → idle `IdleS` → stop;
billed per started second of each worker lifetime; `Workers` reports `startedAt`/`uptimeSeconds`/`image`;
`Status` carries `workerId`; `Billing` returns hourly buckets of those costs (rounded up per bucket,
optionally revised later). Image digest `sha256:` + 64 `0`. The friendly defaults (cold start, kill
latency, idle billing, workers per job) equal the values the canary measured, committed in
`internal/provider/fake/canary-measured.json` (placeholders until M3-X4; `TestFakeDefaults_MatchCanary`).

**C78.2 Fault markers** (D-M3-19; tokens in the recipe `style`, case-sensitive, whitespace- or
comma-separated; `TestFakeFaults_Table`): `fake:slow=<s>` (run seconds), `fake:fail=worker_error`,
`fake:fail=worker_lost` (worker vanishes: `Failed` without output), `fake:truncate=abc|semantic`,
`fake:sha_mismatch` (reports a sha that differs from the uploaded bytes), `fake:queue_expire=<n>` (the
first n attempts expire in the queue), `fake:submit=429|5xx|paused|1010|network|accepted_5xx` (first
attempt; `accepted_5xx` runs the job and still answers 5xx), `fake:upload_closed` (uploads answer 410),
`fake:stuck_worker` (sweep reports a `RUNNING` worker with no job for the rest of the session),
`fake:cancel_ignored=<polls>`, `fake:cancel_ack_keeps_running`, `fake:requeue` (Running, then Queued
again on a new worker), `fake:run_between_polls` (the job runs and completes between two polls),
`fake:cold=<s>` (cold start seconds), `fake:no_start` (the simulated handler skips the start handshake),
`fake:same_audio` (a fixed tone, to provoke `DUPLICATE_AUDIO`).
Markers in a recipe on any other backend are plain style text (they only cost a lint warning
`fake_marker` from M3 lint when the backend is not `fake`).

**C78.3 SimWorker** (`simworker.go`; production code because `dev` uses it): given a C73.3 input,
calls the start URL first (410 ⇒ returns `ATTEMPT_CLOSED` without rendering; it re-polls the URL every
15 simulated seconds while "rendering"), then renders the exact artifact contract with
`internal/synth` — `StyleYuE2` FLAC 6 s 48 kHz/24-bit stereo, rendered by the new
`synth.FLACUncalibrated` (M3-B2: one render pass **without loudness calibration**, E2E time; the merged
`synth.FLAC` is a two-pass calibrated render that needs `Tone.LUFS`), with a **unique tone per attempt** (`Variant` derived from
`sha256(attempt_id)`, so two takes never share M1's audio identity, even with the same seed;
`TestSimWorker_DistinctAudioPerTake`), and the marker `fake:same_audio` forcing a fixed variant to test
`DUPLICATE_AUDIO` — `score.abc` when `cot ≠ off` (sections matching the lyrics headers, `M:4/4`,
`Q:1/4=120`, 2 bars each ⇒ timed), `plan.json`, `request.json` (YuE2 `asdict` order), `config.json`,
`result.json` (`truncated` dict per markers, `timing.e2e_seconds`, `audio_seconds`), `semantic.npy` (written with
the existing `synth.SemanticNPY`, an `<i8` array as M2b's token-loop analyzer reads); validates the input with `provider.ParseWorkerInput` (hand-written,
production; `TestParseWorkerInput_MatchesSchema` checks it against the JSON schema with the test-only
`jsonschema-go`); PUTs every artifact through an `Uploader` (real HTTP client in dev/E2E; direct
`http.Handler` invocation in the property suite — no sockets) with `Content-Digest`, `mm-result.json`
last; returns the C73.5 output. Needs ffmpeg (heavy tests); unit tests of the runner and the property
suite use a stub Ingester and a header-only `SimWorker` mode (`flactest` bytes) that never reaches real
ingest.

**C78.4 `runpodtest`** (test-only; added to `TestNoTestHelpersInBinary` together with
`providertest`): an `httptest.Server` serving both hosts (management under `/v2/serverless…`,
`/v2/catalog…`, `/v2/billing…`, `/graphql`; jobs under `/v2/<endpoint>/…`) built from the snapshot shapes
and the recorded fixtures; per-key permission modes (`jobs`, `read`, `write`, `admin`, each with an
allowlist; `PatchForbidden`; `PatchReplacesNested` — a PATCH replaces nested objects and fills defaults,
e.g. `workers.max = 3`); fault injection (1010, 429 with `RateLimit`/`RateLimit-Policy`/`Retry-After`,
5xx, 409 paused, TTL expiry, worker death, accepted-then-5xx, connection drop after the body, results
purged after 30 min); the catalog answers 401 without a bearer; call counters per path (including
`/run`); and a simulated worker that delegates to `fake.SimWorker` over real HTTP uploads and the start
handshake.

### C79. CLI (`cmd/metamusic`)

New subcommands (stdlib `flag`, `run(args, env)` testable; non-serving commands use
`config.LoadOffline`; every mutation writes an audit row with principal `system:cli`, transport `cli`,
op per D-M3-22):
- `runpod probe [--schema] [--keys] [--out FILE]` (C77.8; never reads the admin key).
- `runpod provision [--admin-env FILE] [--image REF@sha256:…] [--activate [--max 1|2] | --deactivate]
  [--pools AMPERE_24[,ADA_24]] [--vae-source image|cached|volume:PATH] [--timeout-case] [--dry-run]` (C77.6;
  `--timeout-case` adds the worker env `MM_WORKER_DEADLINE=provider` for the canary's timeout case only)
  — the **only**
  command that reads the admin key. `--deactivate` (max = 0) is the operator's manual kill switch; the
  timeout it writes is the current `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME`.
- `runpod status [--json] [--job ID [--repoll]]` — read-only: endpoint (redacted), workers, `/health`
  counts, armed/trip state, last reconcile, billing summary, meter summary; `--job` adds redacted job
  events and per-attempt timings; `--repoll` calls `/status` once more for each of the job's attempt
  refs (jobs key) and prints the HTTP code and status only (the canary's result-retention check).
- `runpod arm --endpoint-name mm-yue2 --reason TEXT [--after-restore]`, `runpod disarm --reason TEXT`,
  `runpod reset-trip --reason TEXT` — same effects as `update_provider_state` (D-M3-8); `arm` refuses
  unless the endpoint name typed matches, `runpod.env` loads and the cached quote passes C77.5; after a
  restore it refuses with `RECONCILE_REQUIRED` unless a reconciliation covered the restore, or billing is
  `none` and `--after-restore` is given (C76.7).
- `runpod canary --url URL [--max-usd 0.25] [--case main|cot-full|terminate|timeout|kill-switch]
  [--verify-billing BATCH] [--keep]` — a scripted agent over REST with `MM_TOKEN` (a canary PAT with
  scopes **read + write + generate**: the reads, `update_track` and `cancel_job` need them). Each case
  is one batch of one `compose_new` take with a built-in synthetic recipe whose length is **bounded by an
  explicit `abc` score** (YuE2 has no duration control): id `mm-canary-<yyyymmdd>-<case>-<n>`, style
  `instrumental, ambient, 80 BPM, no vocals`, lyrics `[Intro]\n\n[Outro]\n`, seed 1, and
  - `main`: `cot: melody` + an 8-bar two-voice score (`M:4/4`, `Q:1/4=120`; expected 30 s after the clamp);
  - `cot-full`: the same score with `cot: full` (records the artifact set of a real `full` render, C73.4);
  - `terminate`: a 32-bar score (≈ 64 s); cancels as soon as `get_job` shows `starting` **or**
    `generating` (whichever is seen first; a short take may skip the `generating` poll);
  - `timeout`: a 64-bar score (≈ 128 s, ≈ 56 s of generation) on a server and endpoint configured with
    `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME=30s` and provisioned with `--timeout-case` (worker env
    `MM_WORKER_DEADLINE=provider`, which disables the worker's own generation deadline so that RunPod's
    `executionTimeout` kill is what is observed; the drift check reports the variable until the operator
    re-provisions without it); the case refuses unless `get_provider_status.max_runtime_s == 30`; asserts
    `failed DEADLINE` with provider `TIMED_OUT` and `30 000 ≤ executionTime ≤ (30 + KillSlackS) × 1000`
    (this also pins that `executionTime` is in milliseconds), and fails with "endpoint not provisioned
    with --timeout-case" when the worker stopped itself first;
  - `kill-switch`: the 64-bar score; once `generating`, prints "run `metamusic runpod provision
    --deactivate` now" for the operator (the canary never reads the admin key) and records what
    `workers.max = 0` does to the in-flight job (completes / cancelled / failed / requeued) and to the
    worker's lifetime (the documented manual fallback).
  Every case: `estimate_generation`; refuses (exit 3) when the estimate's **ceiling** > `--max-usd`
  (default $0.25 = the largest per-case ceiling at the $1.10/h ADA_24 list price + 10 % margin with
  `workers.max = 1`, P.5) or the provider is disarmed; `create_generation`; then `wait_for_job`, and
  **aborts** (cancel, exit 4, prints `metamusic runpod provision --deactivate`) when the job is not
  `generating` within `ColdStartS + 120 s`; asserts the case's outcome, every artifact `verified`, and
  `audio_s ≤ 1.5 × expected_audio_s`; polls `get_provider_status` (sweeps every 10 s while active) until
  running + initializing workers == 0 within `idle_timeout_s + 30 s`. It records (JSON, into the private
  notes): cold start (`announced_at − worker startedAt`, from `get_job` timings), the maximum number of
  workers seen for the single job, worker uptime at cancel and 60 s later (`terminate`), executionTime
  and kill latency (`timeout`), and the kill-switch outcome. `--verify-billing BATCH` (after
  `MM_BILLING_LAG`): `reconciled`, `billed_compute ≤ reserved`, the ratio billed ÷ (list price × metered
  worker-seconds), and whether the idle tail was billed (billed vs metered seconds with and without
  `idle_s`). Unless `--keep`, discards the canary takes (`update_track status=discarded`). Prints JSON;
  exit 0 pass, 1 fail, 2 usage, 3 refused (cost/disarmed), 4 aborted.
- `serve --runner=false` (design §3.2); `serve` starts runner + reconciler when `Cfg.Runner`, the
  backend is not `none` and the backend's keys are present (`keys_absent` ⇒ no runner, reason logged);
  shutdown: stop claiming, cancel `BaseContext` (long-polls return), drain HTTP 30 s, `Runner.Drain` for
  the rest of the grace period, release leases (C6 amended; compose `stop_grace_period: 90s`).
  `mcp-stdio` never starts them and never takes the runner lease.
- `dev --fake-provider` ⇒ `MM_BACKEND=fake` (default in `dev`).
- M2a's `restore` gains the C76.7 disarm step (amends M2a C34).

### C80. Registry, gate and audit amendments (amends M0 C4, M2a C28/C29)

```go
type Meta struct { // M2a C28 form + M2b MCPImage, plus:
    AliasOf string `json:"alias_of,omitempty"` // a human-only alias of another operation (design §4.1)
}
func ValidateAliases(a huma.API) // called at the end of api.New; panics on a dangling alias or schema mismatch
```
`validate` additions (each a `TestRegisterPanics` row): `Cost == "spend"` requires `Method == POST`,
`Idempotency == "create"`, `Scope == generate`; `Cost == "approve"` requires `HumanOnly`; `AliasOf` set
requires `HumanOnly` and `MCP == "exclude:human-only"`. `ValidateAliases`: the target exists and its
request-body and 2xx response schemas are identical (`TestAliases_SchemasEqual`).

**In-transaction idempotency and audit** (D-M3-21):
```go
// internal/idem
type Begun struct{ Outcome Outcome; Status int; ContentType string; Body []byte; Gen string } // + Gen: the created_at Begin already scans from its upsert and discards today (lease generation; second-granular RFC 3339 — a reclaim needs lease_until <= now, i.e. 2 min, so two generations of one key never share a value)
func CompleteTx(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, principalKey, key, gen string, status int, contentType string, body []byte) error
// UPDATE idempotency SET state='done', status=?, content_type=?, response=?
//  WHERE principal_key=? AND key=? AND state='pending' AND created_at=?gen
// 0 rows ⇒ idem.ErrLeaseLost (not the runner's store.ErrLeaseLost: the row was reclaimed by another request —
// Begin's upsert already writes a new created_at on every reclaim in the merged M2a code, so no SQL change —
// or completed); the handler rolls back and answers 409 IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS. Success sets the gate's mark (below).
func KeyFrom(ctx context.Context) (principalKey, key, gen string, ok bool) // set by the gate for create ops
// internal/idem and internal/audit: mutable marks installed by the gate
type Marks struct{ /* atomic: completed, audited */ }
func WithMarks(ctx context.Context) (context.Context, *Marks) // the gate calls it BEFORE invoking the handler
func (m *Marks) Completed() bool; func (m *Marks) Audited() bool
// internal/audit
func PendingFrom(ctx context.Context) (Entry, bool) // the gate's precomputed entry (principal, transport, client, op, method, args sha, idempotency outcome)
func InsertTx(ctx context.Context, tx *sql.Tx, e Entry) error // sets Marks.audited via the ctx; the gate then skips its own insert
// internal/api
func EncodeResponse(v any) ([]byte, error) // byte-identical to the body Huma writes for v (TestEncodeResponse_MatchesHumaBody)
```
The gate installs the mutable `Marks` with `WithMarks` before calling the inner handler and reads them
after it returns: a `context.WithValue` made inside the handler could never reach the gate, so
`CompleteTx`/`InsertTx` set fields of the struct the gate already holds (`TestGate_MarksVisibleAfterHandler`).
Marks are set only after the statement succeeded; because the handler's transaction may still roll back,
the handler clears them on any error path (`Marks.Reset()` in the deferred rollback) — the gate then
audits the refusal and releases the key as in M2a. **Inside the spend transaction every read uses the
`*sql.Tx`** (`Usage`, `UsedSeeds`, `ReusableDraftRecipe`, draft and recipe lookups) — never `st.W`
(one connection: deadlock) or `st.R` (stale snapshot). `audit.Entry` gains **`CostMicroUSD
money.MicroUSD`** (M2a's `audit_log.cost_micro_usd` column already exists; the struct field did not); for
spend ops it is the batch's ceiling, for `runner:*`/`reconciler:*` rows the amount of D-M3-22, else 0.
`TestIdem_CompleteTx` (pending ⇒ done inside the tx; rollback ⇒ still pending; reclaimed by another
request ⇒ new `created_at` ⇒ `ErrLeaseLost`), `TestCreate_AuditAndIdempotencyInTransaction` (a "crash" injected after
the commit and before the response: the retry replays the stored 201; one batch, one reserve set, token
consumed once) and `TestGate_SpendOpRefusalAudited` (402 ⇒ one gate audit row with `error_code =
BUDGET_EXCEEDED`, idempotency released).

**Merged gate behaviour this builds on.** `gate.ServeHTTP` already holds the response of every audited
registry operation (`rec.hold`) until the audit row is durable (`audit.InsertRetrying`, budget 15 s), so a
plain operation never answers before its row exists; `audit.Store.Insert` writes a literal cost of 0 and
`audit.Entry` has no cost field. M3 changes: `WithMarks` is installed in `ServeHTTP` for every matched,
audited operation (so `approve_batch` and the other ledger ops can audit in-transaction too), the gate's
`commit()` closure inserts its own row only `if audited && !marks.Audited()`, and `serveIdempotent` skips
`g.Idem.Complete` (and sets `entry.Idempotency = stored`) when `marks.Completed()`. `Store.Insert` and
`audit.InsertTx` share one SQL text that writes `e.CostMicroUSD`. The in-transaction row must equal what
the gate would have written: the handler completes the `PendingFrom` entry with status 201, idempotency
`stored`, created ids and the measured duration. A replay never reaches the handler, so the gate audits it
as before (`TestGate_ReplayAuditedByGate`). Runner and reconciler rows that accompany a ledger change use
`audit.InsertTx` in that transaction; rows for network actions after a commit (cancel POST, purge,
scale-to-zero) use `audit.InsertRetrying`.

**Transports and system principals** (D-M3-22): `reqinfo.Transport` gains the constant `Runner = "runner"`
(next to `rest spa mcp stdio cli pair`); `auth` gains the constructor `System(name string) Principal`
(`Kind` system, `ID` and `Name` = name, `Via` `"system"`, no scopes; `Key()` = `system:<name>`) for
`System("runner")`, `System("reconciler")`, `System("job")`, next to the existing `auth.CLI` variable; `api.RefFor` renders any `system:*` key as `{kind:"system", id:"system:<name>",
name:"<name>"}` for every viewer.

**Rate classes:** `estimate_generation`, `estimate_variant`, `create_generation`,
`create_generation_human`, `create_variant` are `rate: heavy` (design §4.4: 6/min for agents).

### C81. Operations (types in `internal/api/types_m3.go`; JSON snake_case)

> **M4a amendment (B2):** `CapsView` gains `connectors` (per-grant limits; M4a C106); the five connector and Subsonic-client management operations are admin-scope registry operations, not human-only, while `set_spend_caps`, `create_token` and `revoke_token` stay human-only (M4a D-M4a-24).

Legend (M2a C38): **C** = `idempotency: create`; **H** = `rate: heavy`; cost `spend`/`approve` as
noted (MCP `openWorldHint: true` for spend). Human-only ops use `mcp: exclude:human-only`.

| OperationID | Method + path | Scope | Cost | MCP | UI | Flags | Output |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `estimate_generation` | POST `/api/generations/estimate` | generate | free | tool | `/human/generate` | H | 200 `Estimate` |
| `create_generation` | POST `/api/generations` | generate | spend | tool | `none:agent-face (the SPA confirms through create_generation_human)` | C, H | 201 `Batch` |
| `create_generation_human` | POST `/human/api/generations` | generate | spend | exclude:human-only | `/human/generate` | C, H, alias_of `create_generation` | 201 `Batch` |
| `estimate_variant` | POST `/api/tracks/{id}/variants/estimate` | generate | free | tool | `/human/generate` | H | 200 `Estimate` |
| `create_variant` | POST `/api/tracks/{id}/variants` | generate | spend | tool | `none:agent-face (the SPA confirms through create_generation_human)` | C, H | 201 `Batch` |
| `request_approval` | POST `/api/batches/{id}/approval-request` | generate | free | generic | `none:agent-face (humans approve directly)` | | `ApprovalStatus` |
| `get_approval_status` | GET `/api/batches/{id}/approval` | read | free | generic | `none:agent-face (get_batch carries the approval for the SPA)` | | `ApprovalStatus` |
| `approve_batch` | POST `/human/api/batches/{id}/approve` | generate | approve | exclude:human-only | `/human/confirm/:batch` | | `Batch` |
| `reject_batch` | POST `/human/api/batches/{id}/reject` | generate | approve | exclude:human-only | `/human/confirm/:batch` | | `Batch` |
| `list_batches` | GET `/api/batches` | read | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `{items: BatchSummary[], next_cursor}` |
| `get_batch` | GET `/api/batches/{id}` | read | free | generic | `/batches/:id` | | `Batch` |
| `cancel_batch` | POST `/api/batches/{id}/cancel` | write | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `Batch` |
| `list_jobs` | GET `/api/jobs` | read | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `{items: JobSummary[], next_cursor}` |
| `get_job` | GET `/api/jobs/{id}` | read | free | tool | `/jobs/:id` | | `Job` |
| `wait_for_job` | GET `/api/jobs/{id}/wait` | read | free | tool | `/jobs/:id` | | `{job: Job, changed, timed_out}` |
| `cancel_job` | POST `/api/jobs/{id}/cancel` | write | free | tool | `/jobs` | | `Job` |
| `get_budget` | GET `/api/budget` | read | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `Budget` |
| `get_spend` | GET `/api/spend` | read | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `Spend` |
| `list_ledger` | GET `/api/ledger` | read | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `{items: LedgerEntry[], next_cursor}` |
| `get_provider_status` | GET `/api/provider` | read | free | generic | `/jobs` | | `ProviderStatus` |
| `set_spend_caps` | PUT `/human/api/spend/caps` | admin | free | exclude:human-only | `/human/spend` | | `CapsView` |
| `update_provider_state` | POST `/human/api/provider/state` | admin | free | exclude:human-only | `/human/spend` | | `ProviderStatus` |
| `get_lineage` | GET `/api/tracks/{id}/lineage` | read | free | generic | `/lineage/:id` | | `Lineage` |

23 operations: 7 first-class tools, 11 generic, 5 excluded (human-only) — `TestOps_RegisteredWithMetaM3`
and the `tools/list` arithmetic use these counts. (Revision 3 had 11/7/5; M3-B2 demoted `get_budget`, `list_jobs`, `get_batch` and
`request_approval` to generic under D-M3-36, see the outcome there.) After M3 the registry holds 58 + 23 = 81 operations;
M3-B2 re-pins `TestRegistry_NoNewOps_M2c` (58) at 81 and renames it `TestRegistry_OpCount`. Every agent counterpart of a
human-only op exists (`create_generation`/`create_variant` + `request_approval` + `get_approval_status`
+ `wait_for_job`; `get_budget` for `set_spend_caps`; `get_provider_status` for `update_provider_state`).

**Body limits** (`MaxBodyBytes`, all ≤ the gate's 8 MiB): `estimate_generation` 2 MiB (composed recipes
with inline ABC), `estimate_variant` 1 MiB, every other M3 op with a body 64 KiB
(`TestOps_BodyLimitsM3`).

**Inputs:**
- `estimate_generation`: `{engine?: "yue2", items: ItemIn[] (1..20)}` (C71.1); ≤ 50 takes after
  expansion ⇒ else 422 `BATCH_TOO_LARGE` (`details.takes`, `details.limit`).
- `estimate_variant`: path `id` + `{mode: new_seed|same_seed_edit|lyric_edit|style_edit, n_seeds?,
  seeds?, patch?, title?}` ⇒ one `ItemIn` with `parent_track_id = id`.
- `create_generation`, `create_generation_human`, `create_variant`: `IdemHeader` + `{confirmation_token:
  string (mmc_…), rationale?: string 1..1000}`.
- `request_approval`: no body. `approve_batch`: `{reason?: string 1..500}`. `reject_batch`: `{reason:
  string 1..500}`.
- `list_batches`: `state` (explode), `principal_kind` (explode), `since`, `until`, `limit` 1..100 (50),
  `cursor`. `list_jobs`: `state` (explode), `batch_id`, `track_id` (as parent or child), `since`,
  `limit` 1..100 (50), `cursor`.
- `wait_for_job`: `since_state?` (a job state; default = the state at call time ⇒ waits for any
  change), `timeout_s` 0..55 (default 25).
- `cancel_job`, `cancel_batch`: `{reason?: string 1..500}`; terminal ⇒ 200 unchanged (idempotent);
  an agent on another principal's batch ⇒ 403 `NOT_BATCH_OWNER`.
- `get_spend`: `month` (`YYYY-MM`, default current). `list_ledger`: `batch_id`, `kind` (explode),
  `since`, `until`, `limit` 1..200 (50), `cursor`.
- `set_spend_caps`: `{monthly_micro_usd?, batch_micro_usd?, human_daily_micro_usd?,
  agent_daily_micro_usd?, auto_approve_takes?, auto_approve_micro_usd?, tokens?: [{token_id,
  daily_budget_micro_usd: int|null, auto_approve_takes: int|null, auto_approve_micro_usd: int|null}]
  (≤ 50)}`; each amount 0..1_000_000_000 (≤ $1000); ≥ 1 field; one `spend_cap_changes` row per changed
  target; unknown token ⇒ 422 `UNKNOWN_TOKEN`.
- `update_provider_state`: `{armed?: bool, reset_trip?: bool, reason: string 1..500}` (≥ 1 of
  armed/reset_trip); arming a `runpod-serverless` backend whose generation is disabled ⇒ 409
  `GENERATION_DISABLED` (details.reason); arming after a restore before a reconciliation covered it ⇒
  409 `RECONCILE_REQUIRED` (C76.7); `reset_trip` without an open trip ⇒ 200 unchanged.
- `get_lineage`: `limit` 1..500 (default 200); no cursor — beyond `limit` or the 90 000-byte cap the
  tree is cut in pre-order with `truncated: true` (C71.6).
- Every list obeys the 90 000-byte page cap (M1 C17; each package keeps its own `maxPageBytes = 90000`, as `library`, `judgments` and `api` do).

**Shapes** (abridged; full in `types_m3.go`; every money amount is `<x>_micro_usd` + `<x>_usd`, D-M3-6;
every principal reference through `api.RefFor`):
```
Estimate {estimate_id, confirmation_token, expires_at, engine, backend, takes,
  cost: {expected_micro_usd, expected_usd, max_micro_usd, max_usd, retry_allowance_micro_usd,
         retry_allowance_usd, ceiling_micro_usd, ceiling_usd, ceiling_display ("$0.70", DisplayCeil),
         attempt_overhead_micro_usd, attempt_overhead_usd}, eta_s,
  price: {per_hour_micro_usd, per_hour_usd, gpu_pools[], observed_at},
  items: [{index, mode, takes: TakePlan[]}],
  warnings: [{take_index, code, field, severity, message}],
  requires_human_approval, approval_reasons[],
  budget: {fits, would_exceed: ("batch"|"monthly"|"daily")[], month: {cap, used, left}, day: {cap, used, left}, batch_cap},
  (`max` = Σ take + one attempt overhead per take, the no-retry worst case; `ceiling` = what the batch may
  ever reserve, the "Spend up to" amount; C72.1, D-M3-29)
  provider: {generation_enabled, disabled_reason|null, armed, tripped}}
TakePlan {take_index, request_id, seed, title, operator, parent_track_id|null, draft_id|null,
  expected_audio_s, timeout_s, max_micro_usd, max_usd}
Batch {id, state, via, created_by: PrincipalRef, created_at, finished_at|null, rationale|null, takes,
  counts: {awaiting, queued, running, completed, truncated, failed, cancelled, expired},
  cost: {expected, max, ceiling, reserved, settled, outstanding, reconciled: bool},
  approval: ApprovalStatus, jobs: JobSummary[] (≤ 50, take order), estimate_id}
BatchSummary  Batch minus jobs
JobSummary {id, batch_id, take_index, state, outcome|null, error_code|null, title, request_id, seed,
  operator, parent_track_id|null, draft_id|null, track_id|null, created_at, updated_at,
  cost: {expected_micro_usd, expected_usd, display_micro_usd|null, display_usd|null, basis: "estimate"|"provisional"|"reconciled"}}
Job JobSummary + {engine, backend, mode, attempts, resubmits, started_at|null, finished_at|null,
  deadline_at|null, timeout_s, expected_audio_s, audio_s|null, truncated: {abc, semantic}|null,
  env: Env|null, artifacts: [{name, bytes, sha256, verified}], cancel_requested_at|null,
  timings: {queue_delay_ms|null, execution_ms|null, cold_start_s|null (announced_at − worker startedAt)},
  error_message|null (a fixed English template per code, never provider text),
  events: [{at, from|null, to|null, event, code|null}] (newest 50)}
Env {env_id, engine, pkg_version, code_commit, weights: {repo: revision}, decoder, backend, gpu_class,
  image_digest, detail: {gpu_name, cuda, torch, worker_build, python}}
ApprovalStatus {batch_id, state, reasons[], via|null, decided_by: PrincipalRef|null, decided_at|null,
  reason|null, expires_at, last_requested_at|null, next_request_allowed_at|null, notified (request_approval only)}
Budget {caps: CapsView, month: {start, settled, bill_adjustments, outstanding, cap, left},
  day: {principal: PrincipalRef, settled, outstanding, cap, left},
  auto_approve: {takes, micro_usd, usd} (the caller's effective limits),
  balance: {micro_usd|null, usd|null, source: "graphql"|"unknown"},
  provider: {generation_enabled, disabled_reason|null, armed, tripped}}
CapsView {monthly, batch, human_daily, agent_daily (amounts), auto_approve_takes, auto_approve_micro_usd,
  updated_by: PrincipalRef, updated_at,
  tokens: [{token_id, name, daily_budget_micro_usd|null, auto_approve_takes|null, auto_approve_micro_usd|null}]}
  (human callers: every token; agent callers: their own token only)
Spend {month, days: [{date, settled}], batches: [{id, created_at, created_by, takes, state, expected, max, settled, reconciled}],
  totals: {settled, bill_adjustments, outstanding}}
LedgerEntry {id, at, kind, micro_usd, usd, batch_id|null, job_id|null, reason, principal: PrincipalRef}
ProviderStatus {backend, generation_enabled, disabled_reason|null, armed, armed_changed_by: PrincipalRef,
  armed_changed_at, trip: {id, tripped_at, reason, actions[]}|null, trips_recent: [..] (≤ 5),
  last_reconcile_at|null, workers: {running, idle, initializing, throttled, unhealthy, observed_at}|null,
  endpoint: {name, workers_min, workers_max, idle_timeout_s, gpu_pools[], excluded_types[], flashboot,
             image_digest_short, drift: [{field, want, got}]}|null,
  capabilities: {scale_to_zero, revert_drift, billing}, price: {per_hour_micro_usd, per_hour_usd, observed_at}|null,
  rtf: [{gpu_class, rtf_milli, samples}], cold_start_s, kill_slack_s, max_runtime_s, ttl_s,
  price_margin_ppm, meter: {lifetimes_24h, billed_seconds_24h, max_workers_seen_24h}|null,
  quote_observed_at|null, skew_ms|null, restored_unreconciled: bool, balance}
Lineage {root_id, total, truncated, nodes: [{id, parent_id|null, generation, title, status, seed|null,
  recipe_known, lossy, request_id, origin, operator|null, inferred, rationale|null, stars|null, critic_stars|null,
  outcome|null, cost: {display_micro_usd, display_usd, basis}|null, job_id|null, created_at, children_count}]}
```
`ProviderStatus` never contains the endpoint id, keys, refs or worker ids.

**New problem codes:**

| Code | Status | When |
|---|---|---|
| `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` | 402 | C72.3 (`details: cap, limit, used, requested`) |
| `CONFIRMATION_INVALID` | 409 | unknown, another principal's, consumed, or wrong-shape token (C72.4); consumed by the same principal ⇒ `details.batch_id` |
| `CONFIRMATION_EXPIRED` | 410 | token older than 10 min |
| `ESTIMATE_STALE` | 409 | request id taken / draft changed / reused recipe rendered / backend changed since the estimate (`details.request_ids`, `details.draft_ids`, `details.reason`) |
| `GENERATION_DISABLED` | 503 | C68 (`details.reason`) |
| `PROVIDER_DISARMED` | 503 | D-M3-8 |
| `PROVIDER_TRIPPED` | 503 | an open trip (`details.trip_id`, `details.reason`) |
| `RECONCILE_REQUIRED` | 409 | arming after a restore before a billing reconciliation covered it (C76.7) |
| `BATCH_TOO_LARGE` | 422 | > 50 takes or > 20 items |
| `INVALID_ITEM` | 422 | C71.1 (`details: item, field, rule`) |
| `UNKNOWN_DRAFT` | 422 | `details.draft_ids` |
| `RECIPE_UNKNOWN` | 422 | a mother is a reference track (stub recipe); M2c code, `details.track_id` (D-M3-34) |
| `LOSSY_MASTER` | 422 | a mother's master is a lossy MP3; M2c code, `details.track_id` (D-M3-34) |
| `UNKNOWN_TOKEN` | 422 | `set_spend_caps` token id unknown |
| `DRAFT_NOT_GENERATABLE` | 409 | fulfilled/archived/in-flight draft in an estimate |
| `DRAFT_IN_FLIGHT` | 409 | `update_draft` on a draft with a live job (amends M2a C32) |
| `NOT_BATCH_OWNER` | 403 | an agent acting on another principal's batch |
| `INVALID_TRANSITION` | 409 | existing M2a code (comments so far): approve/reject a decided batch (`details.from`, `details.to`) |
| upload codes | 400–413 | C74.2 (`UPLOAD_LINK_INVALID`, `UPLOAD_LINK_EXPIRED`, `LENGTH_REQUIRED`, `DIGEST_REQUIRED`, `LENGTH_MISMATCH`, `UPLOAD_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, `UPLOAD_CONFLICT`, `UPLOAD_IN_PROGRESS`, `UPLOAD_CLOSED`) |
| start codes | 403/409/410 | C74.3 (`ATTEMPT_LINK_INVALID`, `ATTEMPT_LINK_EXPIRED`, `ATTEMPT_REF_CONFLICT`, `ATTEMPT_CLOSED`) |

`PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` (revision 1) is dropped: no request path calls the provider, so a failing quote
refresh surfaces as `GENERATION_DISABLED quote_stale` once the cached quote is older than 1 h.

**Job error codes** (`jobs.error_code`, Go-validated): `CANCELLED_BY_USER`, `REJECTED`,
`APPROVAL_EXPIRED`, `NO_CAPACITY`, `BUDGET_EXCEEDED`, `PRICE_CHANGED`, `PROVIDER_AUTH`, `EDGE_BLOCKED`,
`SUBMIT_REJECTED`, `WORKER_ERROR`, `WORKER_LOST`, `DEADLINE`, `ARTIFACT_MISSING`, `ARTIFACT_MISMATCH`,
`WORKER_CONTRACT`, `INGEST_FAILED`, `DUPLICATE_AUDIO` (D-M3-32), `NOT_SUBMITTED` (C75.2 row 6),
`PROVIDER_TRIPPED`, `PROVIDER_CANCELLED` (row 16).

**C81.1 Amended earlier operations** (types and registrations M3-B2, behaviour by the owning lane;
records the changes in the M1/M2a/M2b plans):
- `search_tracks` gains `origin` (explode; `import` | `job`). `TrackSummary` gains `origin`,
  `cost_display_micro_usd|null`, `cost_display_usd|null`.
- `get_track` (`TrackDetail`) gains `generation: {job_id, batch_id, operator, rationale|null, cost:
  {display_micro_usd, display_usd, basis}, env: Env|null}|null` (null for imported takes).
- `get_track_context` (M2a C35): `provenance.cost_usd` is replaced by `provenance.cost` =
  `{micro_usd, usd, basis}|null`; `provenance.env` = `Env|null`; `provenance.job_id`; `next_actions`
  gains, when the caller has scope `generate` and generation is enabled, before the final "record your
  critique" entry: "try a new-seed variant: estimate_variant mode=new_seed n_seeds=3 (the estimate is
  free; spending needs create_variant)". Today `next_actions` has at most five entries and no cap (rate, open
  fix comments, untimed, truncated, critique; `internal/trackctx`); M3 adds a cap of 5: the generation hint
  has the **lowest** priority, so when all four conditional entries apply it is the entry dropped and
  "record your critique" stays last (`TestContext_NextActionsCapDropsGenerationHint`). No hint is added
  when `recipe.known` is false (D-M3-34). The `order` map of `internal/trackctx` changes `provenance` from
  `{origin, import_rule, truncated, cost_usd, env}` to `{origin, import_rule, truncated, cost, env,
  job_id}`, which changes every bundle golden by that one key (M3-B6 owns the golden update). The 24 000-character budget and `Fit` order are
  unchanged; M3-B6 owns the golden update.
- `get_stats` gains `generation: {jobs_by_state{}, takes_month, spend_month_micro_usd,
  spend_month_usd, outstanding_micro_usd, outstanding_usd}` and `breakdown: {by_style_term: [{term,
  tracks, rated, mean_stars}] (top 30 by tracks), by_seed: [{seed, tracks, rated, mean_stars}] (seeds
  used by ≥ 2 tracks, top 30)}` (design §4.1, the spec's Phase-3 report; `mean_stars` is a float of
  human stars, not money). Stubs are excluded from both lists
  (`recipes.engine <> 'unknown'`: their seed 0 is a placeholder, D-M3-34).
- `update_draft` (M2a) refuses a draft referenced by a non-terminal job with 409 `DRAFT_IN_FLIGHT`;
  `DraftSummary`/`DraftDetail` gain `in_flight: bool` and `generated_takes: int`.
- `list_drafts`/`get_draft` gain nothing else; `create_draft` unchanged.

### C82. Notifications (amends M2a C37)

`notify.Message` gains `Actions []Action` (`Action{Action: "view", Label, URL string}`, ≤ 3), published
as ntfy's JSON `actions` array — added in **Stage 1 (M3-B1)** with its record in M2a C37, so the
Generation lane's `TestRequestApproval_PushesOnce` and the Jobs lane both build on it and no Stage-2 lane
edits `internal/notify` or `docs/**`. Every "up to" amount is `DisplayCeil(ceiling)`. Events (bodies never contain tokens, URLs with query strings, endpoint
ids, keys or refs — `TestNotify_M3NoSecrets`; names pass M2a's sanitiser):

| Event | Priority | Title / body | Click / action |
|---|---|---|---|
| approval requested (`request_approval`) | 4 | "Approval needed" / "Agent <token name> asks to spend up to $0.70 for 7 takes" (the ceiling, rounded up) | view "Review" → `<PublicURL>/human/confirm/<batch>` |
| batch finished | 3 | "Batch done" / "6 completed (1 truncated), 1 failed — $0.15 provisional" | `<PublicURL>/batches/<id>` |
| provider tripped | 5 | "Generation stopped: <reason>" / actions taken | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| manual scale-to-zero needed (no write key, or `ScaleToZero` failed) | 5, tag `rotating_light` | "Set max workers to 0 now" / "The app cannot change the endpoint; open the console" | view "Open console" → `RunPodKeys.ConsoleURL` (C68) |
| provider access lost (`provider_access` trip) | 5, tag `rotating_light` | "RunPod keys refused" / "Polling/cancel/sweep refused (401/403/1010); jobs settled by their time bound" | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| meter above reservations (`meter_exceeds_reserved`) | 5 | "Worker time above reservation" | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| workers not at zero after a batch | 4 | "Workers still running" | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| cancel not confirmed after 5 min | 4 | "Cancel not confirmed yet" | `<PublicURL>/jobs/<id>` |
| config drift (incl. idle/max raised above a live batch's frozen quote) | 4 | "Endpoint drift: <fields>" (reverted or not) | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| clock skew above 30 s | 4 | "Clock skew <n> s — billing releases paused" | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| no quiet window for 72 h | 4 | "Billing reconciliation stalled" / blocking batch ids | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| claim preflight failing (disk / upload route) | 4 | "Generation paused: <disk low \| upload route down>" | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |
| billing unreadable (weekly) | 2 | "Billing not reconciled" / "<n> batches released after the hold" | `<PublicURL>/jobs` |

### C83. Python worker and image (`worker/`)

**C83.1 Layout:**
```
worker/
  pyproject.toml        name "metamusic-worker"; requires-python "==3.12.*"; runtime deps: runpod==<pin> only
                        (NO yue2 extra: uv locks every extra, which would pull yue2 and torch into uv.lock;
                        the Dockerfile and the contract job install yue2-infer explicitly);
                        dependency group "dev": pytest, pytest-timeout, pytest-cov, jsonschema, numpy, soundfile
  uv.lock               (dev environment; no torch, no yue2 — TestUVLock_NoTorch greps it)
  Dockerfile  Dockerfile.dockerignore  README.md (public-safe)
  src/mmworker/schema/mm-worker-1.input.schema.json  …/mm-worker-1.output.schema.json   (C73.3, C73.5;
                        shipped inside the package; Go tests read worker/src/mmworker/schema/)
  src/mmworker/__init__.py      __version__
  src/mmworker/__main__.py      MODE=serverless ⇒ serverless.main(); MODE=selftest ⇒ selftest.main();
                                MODE=agent ⇒ exit 2 "MODE=agent arrives in M6"; anything else ⇒ exit 2
  src/mmworker/contract.py      ARTIFACTS, REQUIRED(cot), parse_input(dict) -> TakeInput (hand-written, no
                                jsonschema at runtime; raises BadInput)
  src/mmworker/pipeline.py      Pipeline protocol; load_yue2(env) imports yue2 lazily inside the function
                                (outside the handler); FakePipeline (tests, selftest)
  src/mmworker/start.py         StartClient: announce(job_id) and poll() against the start URL (D-M3-28)
  src/mmworker/handler.py       process(take, pipe, uploader, clock) -> dict (C83.2)
  src/mmworker/upload.py        Uploader (C83.3)
  src/mmworker/env.py           env fingerprint (C73.5 "env")
  src/mmworker/redact.py        strip URL query strings and bearer-looking tokens from any text
  src/mmworker/serverless.py    main(): require HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1, PIPE = load_yue2(os.environ) once, runpod.serverless.start({"handler": handle})
  src/mmworker/selftest.py      FakePipeline + a local http.server sink; exits 0 when the full handler path passes
  tests/                        pytest, no torch (C83.5)
  contract/                     pytest against the pinned yue2 + CPU torch, no weights (C83.4)
  contract/testdata/{artifacts.json, batch_cases.jsonl, batch_cases.expect.json}
```

**C83.2 Handler (`process`; exact order):**
1. `parse_input` (contract version `mm.worker/1`, schema, artifact names ⊆ `ARTIFACTS`, `mm-result.json`
   and `start` present) else `BAD_INPUT`/`CONTRACT_VERSION`. Two deadlines (D-M3-13):
   `upload_deadline = min(handler_start + timeout_s − 10 s, deadline_unix − 60 s)` and
   `generation_deadline = upload_deadline − upload_budget_s` (with `MM_WORKER_DEADLINE=provider`, set
   only by `provision --timeout-case`, the generation deadline is disabled so RunPod's own kill is
   observed).
1a. **Start handshake:** `StartClient.announce(job["id"])` (POST, retried with backoff for ≤ 60 s on
   network errors/5xx): 200 ⇒ continue; 410 ⇒ return `ATTEMPT_CLOSED` at once (no render, no upload);
   still failing after 60 s ⇒ `START_FAILED` (no render: an attempt the app cannot see must not spend).
2. `expect.weights` must equal the revisions the pipeline was loaded with, else `WEIGHTS_MISMATCH`
   (cheap failure before any GPU work).
3. Validate the request with yue2's own `request_kwargs` (or the pinned commit's equivalent) ⇒
   `BAD_INPUT` on rejection.
4. `result = pipe(style=…, lyrics=…, cot=…, seed=…, id=…, abc=… (if present), cfg_scale=… (if
