   present), cancelled=cancelled)` where `cancelled()` is true when `now() > generation_deadline` **or**
   the start URL answered 410 on its last poll (polled at most every 15 s from inside the callback, on a
   background thread so the callback never blocks the GPU loop); a deadline stop ⇒ `DEADLINE`, a 410 ⇒
   `ATTEMPT_CLOSED` (no uploads); exceptions ⇒ `PIPELINE_ERROR` (message redacted).
5. `result.save_artifacts(tmpdir)`.
6. `truncated = result.truncated`; not a `dict` with exactly the keys `abc`, `semantic` of `bool` values
   ⇒ `TRUNCATED_NOT_DICT`; `is_truncated = any(truncated.values())` (never `if result.truncated`,
   never `== False` — the spec bug, design §3.3).
7. Collect artifacts: every allowlisted file present in `tmpdir` (never `latent.npy`); required ones
   missing ⇒ `ARTIFACT_MISSING`; any file > its `max_bytes` ⇒ `ARTIFACT_TOO_LARGE`; sha256 + bytes each.
8. Upload in this order: `request.json`, `config.json`, `result.json`, `score.abc`, `plan.json`,
   `plan_manifest.json`, the `.npy` files, `audio.flac`; then build the output (C73.5) and upload it as
   `mm-result.json` **last**.
9. Return the output (RunPod job output). On any error after step 1: best-effort `mm-result.json` with
   `status: "error"`, then return `{"error": "<CODE>: <redacted message>", "mm": output}`.
The model is loaded once per worker process, outside the handler (`serverless.main`); a second job on a
warm worker reuses it (`test_model_loaded_once`).

**C83.3 Uploads** (`Uploader.put(name, path, target, deadline)`): stdlib `http.client` over TLS
(`ssl.create_default_context()`), method `PUT`, headers `Content-Length`, `Content-Type:
application/octet-stream`, `Content-Digest: sha-256=:<base64>:`, `User-Agent:
metamusic-worker/<version>`; streamed from the file. Retries with backoff 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 30, 30… s
(`Retry-After` honoured) on connection errors, timeouts (60 s per attempt), 5xx, 408, 429 and 422
(`UPLOAD_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, at most 3 times); immediate abort on 403 (`UPLOAD_FAILED`), 409
(`UPLOAD_CONFLICT`), 410 (`UPLOAD_CLOSED`), 411/413/400; every retry checks `upload_deadline`
(D-M3-13: `timeout_s − 10 s`, not the generation deadline) and stops with `DEADLINE` when the next
attempt would pass it. 200 and 201 are success. Every log line
and error message passes `redact.text` (URL query strings ⇒ `?<redacted>`).

**C83.4 Worker-contract job** (`contract/test_yue2_contract.py`; runs in CI with the pinned yue2 commit
and **CPU torch**, no weights, no GPU):
- `inspect.signature(YuE2Pipeline.__call__)` ⊇ {`style`, `lyrics`, `cot`, `seed`, `abc`, `cfg_scale`,
  `id`, `cancelled`}; `YuE2Pipeline.from_pretrained` ⊇ {`model`, `vae`, `revision`, `vae_revision`,
  `local_files_only`, `device`, `cache_dir`}.
- A tiny `SongResult` (1 s of zeros, built the way `pipeline.py` builds it; the construction is
  documented in the test) has `truncated` returning a dict with keys exactly {`abc`, `semantic`}.
- `save_artifacts(tmp)` on it writes, per `cot` ∈ {full, melody, off}, the file set recorded in
  `contract/testdata/artifacts.json`; the test fails on any difference. Because the `SongResult` is hand
  built, this file only proves what `save_artifacts` writes **for that object**; it never decides the
  required set — that comes from `artifacts.observed.json`, recorded from real GPU renders in M3-X3
  (C73.4). `TestArtifacts_MatchWorkerContract` checks that the observed set ⊆ the contract set and that
  `latent.npy` is never allowlisted.
- Hub allowlist: with `huggingface_hub` download entry points monkeypatched to record and raise,
  `from_pretrained(model="m-a-p/YuE2-3B", vae="m-a-p/YuE2-Vae", revision=PIN_3B,
  vae_revision=PIN_VAE, device="cpu")` requests only those two repo ids at those revisions; with
  `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` it raises within 5 s and opens no socket (a socket guard fixture).
- Batch-validator agreement (closes M2a's "batch-validator drift" risk): every line of
  `contract/testdata/batch_cases.jsonl` (written by Go `TestWriteBatchContractCases -update` from
  M2a's `ValidateBatch` table) is passed through yue2's real `request_kwargs`; accepted/rejected must
  equal `batch_cases.expect.json` (Go's ModeYuE2 verdicts; codes are not compared). Without `-update`,
  `TestWriteBatchContractCases` regenerates both files in memory and **fails** when the committed files
  differ (stale cases can never pass silently).
- Prints `yue2.__version__`, the commit, `torch.__version__` to the job summary.

**C83.5 Unit tests** (`tests/`, no torch; the fake pipeline writes a 1 s sine FLAC with `soundfile`
and a `truncated` dict): `test_parse_input_*` (contract version, unknown artifact, missing
`mm-result.json`, schema), `test_truncation_any` (`{"abc":False,"semantic":True}` ⇒ truncated;
both False ⇒ clean; `True` (bool) ⇒ `TRUNCATED_NOT_DICT`; extra key ⇒ `TRUNCATED_NOT_DICT`),
`test_artifacts_exclude_latent`, `test_required_missing`, `test_too_large`, `test_upload_order_result_last`,
`test_upload_retry_then_ok` (local server: 503, 503, 201 ⇒ 3 PUTs, sleeps 1 s and 2 s via an injected
sleep), `test_upload_retry_after`, `test_upload_conflict_aborts`, `test_upload_closed_aborts`,
`test_upload_digest_header` (server recomputes), `test_upload_deadline_stops_retries`,
`test_errors_redacted` (an exception text containing `https://mm.example.com/worker/v1/uploads/x?exp=1&sig=abc`
⇒ no `sig=`/`exp=` anywhere in output, logs or returned error), `test_weights_mismatch_fails_fast`
(pipe never called), `test_model_loaded_once`, `test_serverless_requires_hf_offline` (unset ⇒ exit
code 2 before loading), `test_output_matches_schema` (`jsonschema` against the packaged output schema),
`test_parse_input_matches_schema` (the hand-written `parse_input` accepts/rejects exactly what the
input schema does over a table of cases), `test_artifacts_match_go` (ARTIFACTS equals the schema enum),
`test_mode_agent_refused`, `test_selftest_passes`, `test_yue2_imported_lazily` (importing `mmworker`
does not import `yue2`), `test_start_410_skips_render` (announce ⇒ 410: pipe never called, no upload,
`ATTEMPT_CLOSED`), `test_start_failure_skips_render` (60 s of 503 ⇒ `START_FAILED`, pipe never called),
`test_cancel_poll_stops_pipeline` (the fake pipeline calls `cancelled()` in a loop; the start URL turns
410 after 20 s ⇒ stop within 15 s + one loop, `ATTEMPT_CLOSED`, at most one poll per 15 s),
`test_generation_deadline_leaves_upload_window` (a slow fake pipeline stops at `timeout_s −
upload_budget_s − 10 s`; uploads still complete before `timeout_s − 10 s`), `test_worker_deadline_provider_mode`
(`MM_WORKER_DEADLINE=provider` ⇒ no generation deadline). Coverage gate: `pytest --cov=mmworker
--cov-fail-under=90`.

**C83.6 Image** (`worker/Dockerfile`; built by `worker-image.yml`, C85):
```
FROM python:3.12-slim@sha256:<pinned> AS build
  apt-get install --no-install-recommends git ca-certificates; COPY --from=ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:<pinned>@sha256:<…> /uv /bin/uv
  ARG YUE2_COMMIT  (40 hex, ≥ the Apache-2.0 relicensing commit; checked by a RUN test)
  uv venv /opt/venv; uv pip install "yue2-infer @ git+https://github.com/multimodal-art-projection/YuE@${YUE2_COMMIT}" "runpod==<pin>"
  uv pip uninstall torchvision torchaudio (ignore "not installed"); COPY . /src/worker; uv pip install --no-deps /src/worker
  uv pip freeze > /opt/venv/freeze.txt
FROM python:3.12-slim@sha256:<pinned>
  COPY --from=build /opt/venv /opt/venv
  ENV PATH=/opt/venv/bin:$PATH HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 MODE=serverless PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
  ARG YUE2_COMMIT GIT_SHA; ENV MM_YUE2_COMMIT=$YUE2_COMMIT MM_WORKER_BUILD=$GIT_SHA
  ENTRYPOINT ["python", "-m", "mmworker"]
```
The build context is **`worker/`** (`docker build -f worker/Dockerfile worker/`), so
`worker/Dockerfile.dockerignore` (BuildKit's per-Dockerfile ignore file) applies and nothing outside
`worker/` can enter the image; the root `.dockerignore` additionally lists `worker/` and `**/runpod*.env`
so the app image never contains either. No 3B weights in the image (CC BY-NC 4.0; design §3.3); the VAE is baked in at build time at a pinned,
sha256-verified revision with a `NOTICE` (D-M3-38). Runtime env set by `runpod provision`:
`MM_YUE2_3B_REVISION`, `MM_YUE2_VAE_REVISION`, `MM_YUE2_VAE_DIR` (`/opt/mm/hf-cache/hub` for
`--vae-source image`, the default; RunPod's cached dir or a network volume otherwise); the 3B model loads from RunPod's cached-model directory
(`/runpod-volume/huggingface-cache/hub`) with `local_files_only=True`. CI smoke (in the image):
`python -c "import transformers, yue2, mmworker"`; `torchvision` and `torchaudio` absent;
`torch.version.cuda` printed and asserted ≥ 12.8; `MODE=selftest` passes.

### C84. Web (`web/`; amends M2a C40, M2b C54)

Routes (added by M3-W1 to `APP_ROUTES` in `web/src/lib/router.ts` with their `App.svelte` branches and nav
entries; specific before parametric): `/jobs` · `/jobs/:id` ·
`/batches/:id` · `/lineage/:id` · `/human/generate` · `/human/confirm/:batch` · `/human/spend`.
`/human/*` routes stay full-page navigations (M2a C40).

```
web/src/
  lib/money.ts                  pure: formatMicroUSD(µ) (= MicroUSD.Display), formatMicroUSDCeil(µ) (= DisplayCeil, every "up to" amount), parseUSDInput("0.50") → µ (integers only; no float math)
  lib/genIntent.ts              pure: GenerationIntent ⇄ sessionStorage['mm.genIntent'] ⇄ estimate request items; ≤ 50 takes check
  lib/jobState.ts               pure: state → label/colour/terminal; canCancel(job, viewer)
  lib/lineageTree.ts            pure: nodes → rows {depth, connector glyphs, collapsed}
  lib/components/GenerateButton.svelte    "Variant ▾" menu (New seed ×3 · Edit lyrics · Edit style/BPM · Same seed edit); hidden for devices, without generate scope and for tracks with `recipe_known = false` (the M2c `RecipeUnknownPanel` explains why)
  lib/components/ModeForm.svelte          mode-specific fields: n_seeds, seeds, patch (reuses M2a DraftEditor field components), title, template_mode
  lib/components/EstimateView.svelte      takes table, warnings, expected / max / retry allowance / ceiling, ETA, budget bars, approval notes, disabled reasons
  lib/components/ConfirmSpend.svelte      "Spend up to $X" with X = formatMicroUSDCeil(ceiling) (one idempotency key per click, kept across retries: `idempotentAction` from `web/src/api/idem.ts`); 402/409/410/503 rendering; a 409 `CONFIRMATION_INVALID` with `details.batch_id` navigates to that batch instead of re-estimating
  lib/components/BudgetBar.svelte         month/day settled + reserved vs cap
  lib/components/ProviderBanner.svelte    generation disabled / disarmed / tripped, with a link to /human/spend
  lib/components/JobRow.svelte            state chip, cost, Terminate (always visible on non-terminal jobs), link
  lib/components/BatchCard.svelte         counts, cost, approval state, Terminate batch
  lib/components/JobTimeline.svelte       events
  lib/components/ProvenancePanel.svelte   origin, operator, rationale, cost (basis), env, truncation badge, "A/B with parent" + "Compare" (M2b) for children
  lib/components/LineageTree.svelte       tree rows; node actions: open, Variant, A/B with parent, Compare
  lib/components/SpendChart.svelte        per-day settled bars (inline SVG, no library)
  routes/{Jobs,Job,Batch,Lineage,HumanGenerate,HumanConfirm,HumanSpend}.svelte
```
**C84.1 Three-click variant (F7):** Track page `GenerateButton` → (1) "Variant" → (2) "New seed ×3"
writes the intent `{mode: new_seed, parent_track_id, n_seeds: 3}` and `location.assign("/human/generate")`
→ the page calls `estimate_variant` immediately and shows the estimate → (3) "Spend up to $X" ⇒
`create_generation_human` ⇒ `/batches/:id`. Edit modes open `ModeForm` first (one extra click for the
edit itself). Multi-item intents (drafts, template, compose) call `estimate_generation`.

**C84.2 Backlog and library sources:** Backlog list gains row checkboxes, "Select all in this filter
(≤ 50)", and "Generate (N)" (intent `from_draft` with `draft_ids`); the Draft page gains "Generate this
draft"; the Library gains a selection mode with "Template edit…" (intent `from_template` with
`parent_track_ids`, 2..50; reference and lossy rows are not selectable: "recipe unknown", D-M3-34) and a "New song" button (intent `compose_new`, the form reuses M2a's draft
editor fields with live `lint_recipe`). A selection over 50 is refused in the UI with "generate in
batches of at most 50" (D-M3-7).

**C84.3 Jobs & costs (`/jobs`, F8):** `ProviderBanner` (from `get_provider_status`, fetched on every visit); `BudgetBar` (month: settled + reserved vs cap;
today: the viewer's own); active jobs (`list_jobs state=…live states`) with **Terminate** on every
non-terminal row (`cancel_job`) and per batch (`cancel_batch`); batches (`list_batches`); a month
selector with `SpendChart` and batch costs (`get_spend`); a "Ledger" tab (`list_ledger`, paged); a
provider tab (`get_provider_status`: workers, endpoint, drift, RTF, price, balance); `get_budget` for
the header. Live updates: the page polls `list_jobs` every 5 s while any job is live (stops when none);
`/jobs/:id` uses `wait_for_job` (timeout 25 s, loop while mounted and not terminal).

**C84.4 Human pages:** `/human/generate` (C84.1); `/human/confirm/:batch` (`get_batch`: who asked
(`created_by` — humans see names), rationale, per-take request id/seed/title/parent and recipe excerpts
via `get_draft`/`get_track` links, warnings, cost expected/max, budget impact; Approve (optional reason)
/ Reject (required reason) ⇒ `approve_batch`/`reject_batch`); `/human/spend` (caps form ⇒
`set_spend_caps` with dollar inputs converted by `money.ts`; per-token limits table from M2a
`list_tokens`; provider section ⇒ `update_provider_state` arm/disarm/reset trip with a required reason;
trip history from `get_provider_status`).

**C84.5 Track and lineage:** Track page gains `GenerateButton`, `ProvenancePanel` (from `get_track`'s
`generation`), and a "Lineage" link; `/lineage/:id` renders `get_lineage` with `LineageTree` (pre-order,
indentation by generation, operator chip, inferred badge, stars, status, cost, truncated badge; nodes
over the 200 default are paged by "show more" ⇒ `limit=500`).

Unit tests (`bun test`): `money.test.ts` (326217 ⇒ "$0.33"; 5800 ⇒ "$0.0058"; 0 ⇒ "$0.00"; ceil: 324999 ⇒
"$0.33", 691731 ⇒ "$0.70", 5801 ⇒ "$0.0059"; "0.50" ⇒ 500000; "0.1234567" ⇒ refused; never produces
`0.30000000000000004`; the Go and TS outputs agree on a shared table `web/src/lib/money.cases.json`
read by `TestMoney_SharedCases` too), `genIntent.test.ts` (round trip; 51 drafts refused;
variant intent ⇒ `estimate_variant` body), `jobState.test.ts` (canCancel: terminal ⇒ false; agent-only
UI never shown), `lineageTree.test.ts` (3-generation tree ⇒ expected depth/connectors), `confirm.test.ts`
(one idempotency key per click, reused on retry after a network error; 402 renders the cap details; 410
offers "re-estimate"; 409 with `details.batch_id` opens that batch; the button text is the ceiling rounded
up).

### C85. Tooling, CI, routing, coverage, docs (amends M1 C23/C24, M2a C41)

- **`ci.yml` job (required):** `worker-py` (setup-uv pinned; `uv sync --frozen --group dev` in
  `worker/`; `uv run pytest --cov=mmworker --cov-fail-under=90`; no path filter — it is fast, ≈ 1 min).
  `ci.yml` gets **no `paths:` filter** of any kind (a `paths:` filter applies to a whole workflow, never
  to one job, and would skip the Go tests).
- **`worker-contract.yml` (own workflow, not a required check):** triggers `pull_request` and `push`
  with `paths: [worker/**, internal/engine/yue2/**, .github/workflows/worker-contract.yml]` plus
  `schedule` (weekly) and `workflow_dispatch`: `uv venv`; `uv pip install torch==2.10.0 --index-url
  https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu`; `uv pip install "yue2-infer @ git+…@<commit>"`; `uv pip
  install -e worker --no-deps` + dev group; `uv run pytest worker/contract`; uv cache keyed on
  `worker/uv.lock` + the commit; ≈ 4 min. Exact uv flags are verified with context7 at implementation.
- **`worker-image.yml`:** `push` to `main` with `paths: [worker/**, .github/workflows/worker-image.yml]`
  (builds, smokes, pushes, attests); `pull_request` with the same paths (builds and smokes **without
  pushing** — the dry run of a workflow that exists only on a branch, since `workflow_dispatch` cannot
  start it there); `workflow_dispatch` on `main`. Two jobs: `build-smoke` (`contents: read`; every
  event) and `publish` (`needs: build-smoke`, `if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'`; `contents: read,
  packages: write, id-token: write, attestations: write`); free-disk-space step (pinned SHA);
  buildx with context `worker/` (C83.6); `docker/build-push-action` (pinned SHA) with
  `ghcr.io/nicolasacchi/metamusic-worker:<git sha>` and `:main`, gha cache, provenance; in-image smoke
  (C83.6); `actions/attest-build-provenance`; the digest is written to the job summary. The digest used
  by the endpoint is committed in **`deploy/worker-image.digest`** (outside `worker/**`, so committing it
  never re-triggers a 15-minute build with a new digest) by the PR that follows (M3-X2) — never edited by
  CI.
- **`weekly.yml`** (new; no weekly workflow exists yet): RunPod management OpenAPI drift (`curl -fsS
  https://api.runpod.io/v2/openapi.json | jq -S .` vs the canonicalised snapshot; failure opens nothing,
  it just fails the run). The weekly contract run lives in `worker-contract.yml`.
- **Routing contract** (M1 C24 / M2a C41 test extended): `/worker/v1/uploads/x` **and
  `/worker/v1/attempts/x/start`** route to `mm-media` (no forward-auth call; access log off; no
  compression; `Remote-*`/`X-MM-Edge` stripped); with the stub at one factor `POST
  /human/api/batches/x/approve`, `POST /human/api/generations`, `PUT /human/api/spend/caps` and `POST
  /human/api/provider/state` are rejected, at two factors allowed; `mm-device` never reaches
  `/human/api/generations`.
- **Privacy gate:** `scripts/public-gate.sh` already refuses `*.env` and `.env*` paths, which covers `runpod.env`
  and `runpod-admin.env` (no new glob; the selftest gains the cases); `.gitleaks.toml` already carries the
  `mmp_`/`mmc_` token rules and gains a custom rule
  `(?i)RUNPOD_[A-Z_]*KEY\s*[=:]\s*\S{8,}`. Tests never contain such a literal: `config` tests build
  `RUNPOD_*_KEY=<value>` lines at runtime from canary placeholders (`"RUNPOD_" + "JOBS_KEY=" + canary`),
  so `public-gate --history` passes at M3-X1 without an allowlist (`TestNoRunPodKeyLiterals` greps
  `*_test.go`). `internal/provider/runpod/testdata/fixtures/*.json` are scanned by
  `TestFixtures_Redacted` (no string ≥ 32 chars of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` outside placeholders, no `?`-query
  URLs, no `Authorization`); `.gitignore` gains `runpod*.env` (it ignores `.env` and `.env.*` today); the root `.dockerignore` gains `worker/`
  and `**/runpod*.env`.
- **Coverage thresholds:** `internal/spend 95`, `internal/jobs 95`, `internal/upload 95`,
  `internal/money 95`, `internal/generation 90`, `internal/lineage 90`, `internal/provider/runpod 90`,
  `internal/provider/fake 85`; Python worker ≥ 90 % lines (`pytest-cov`, `worker-py` job).
- **Go CI tiers (M2c C67):** CI runs `go-fast` and `go-media` through `scripts/ci-go-test.sh`; M3-I1 adds
  `./internal/jobs` and `./internal/provider/fake` (ingest bridge, SimWorker, property and chaos suites) to the
  `media` array of that script, so every other new package lands in `go-fast` automatically and the union
  stays the whole module. `coverage` keeps `needs: [go-fast, go-media, e2e]`.
- **Makefile / loop:** targets `worker` (`cd worker && uv run pytest`), `contract`
  (`scripts/worker-contract.sh`, same steps as CI) and `canary`, all three in the `scripts/loop` allowlist (its
  `case` list and `scripts/loop-selftest.sh`) and `metrics/loop.schema.json` — CLAUDE.md forbids bypassing the
  wrapper, so even `make canary` goes through it; it only **prints** the documented command (the canary is run
  by the operator, M3-X3); `fixture-db` gains a third step (`servertest TestWriteFixtureDB_M3`).
- **Deploy preflight:** new `scripts/deploy-preflight.sh` asks the running instance (`metamusic runpod
  status --json` inside the container) for live attempts and **exits non-zero while any exist** unless
  `--force`; M1-X2's host `deploy.sh` calls it as its first step (M3-X2 adds the call), so a deploy
  refuses while renders are in flight. The runbook order is **disarm → drain** (wait until `status` shows
  no live attempt) **→ deploy → arm** (D-M3-33). `scripts/deploy-preflight-selftest.sh` covers the
  refusal.
- **`tools/list` ceiling:** D-M3-36. The merged constant is `toolsCeilingBytes = 56200` (25 tools, 56 122 compact
  bytes after M2c; golden baseline `toolsListBaselineBytes = 132866`); M3-B2 measures all 11 new tools, reports
  separately the bytes of the largest output schemas (`Estimate`, `Batch`, `Job`), sets the ceiling to the
  measured value rounded up with a one-line justification and re-baselines the golden. If the M3 tools alone
  exceed 16 000 bytes: (1) demote M3 first-class tools to `generic` in this order — `get_budget`,
  `list_jobs`, `get_batch`, `request_approval` (never `estimate_*`, `create_*`, `wait_for_job`, `get_job`,
  `cancel_job`); (2) then M2c's order (`export_jsonl`, `lint_recipe`; M2a's `get_audit`, `update_playlist`,
  `upload_batch_jsonl`, `update_draft` are already generic); (3) then shorten M3 tool descriptions to ≤ 200
  characters each; (4) if it still does not hold, **stop and ask the operator** (no further automatic
  demotion). Every demotion is recorded in C81 and the `next_actions` texts.
- **Docs:** `docs/architecture.md` M3 section (public-safe), `docs/runbook.md` (extended: probe → provision
  (inactive) → scoped keys → probe keys → activate → arm → canary; trip reset; manual kill switch
  `runpod provision --deactivate`; console "max workers = 0" fallback; **restoring `workers.max` after a
  trip's ScaleToZero** — the admin key file is deleted after activation, so the operator either re-provides
  the key file and runs `runpod provision --activate --max N`, or sets max workers in the console, then
  `runpod reset-trip` and arm; deploy order disarm → drain → deploy → arm; restore ⇒ disarmed until a
  reconciliation covered it; billing check; never run the canary without approval),
  `docs/device-checklist.md` M3 items (C86.6), `worker/README.md`.
- **Compose example:** no RunPod secret ever passes through compose `env_file`/`environment`;
  `runpod.env` lives in the secrets dir; `stop_grace_period: 90s`; `MM_BACKEND=none` in the example.

### C86. Test harness, fixtures, property and chaos suites (amends M2a C42)

**C86.1 `servertest`** gains `Options.Backend` (`"none"` default | `"fake"` | `"runpod"` with a
`runpodtest` server), `Options.Runner` (start runner + reconciler; default false — tests drive
`Rig.Runner.Step` / `Rig.Runner.Drain` / `Rig.Reconciler.Pass` themselves), `Options.FakeSpeed`,
`Options.Caps` (`spend.Caps` override), `Options.M3` (plus `librarytest.FixtureM3`; requires `Fixture`, like
`Options.M2c`), and `Rig.Fake *fake.Backend`, `Rig.RunPod *runpodtest.Server`,
`Rig.Runner`, `Rig.Reconciler`, `Rig.Ntfy` (M2a fake). Rigs keep the M2a clock rule: injected clock
everywhere; **`Rig.Clock` is the one manual clock** (`servertest.Clock` = `clocktest.Manual`, C68), so
`Rig.Clock.Advance` drives runner, reconciler, `Wait`, spend and upload expiry. `app.Options` gains
`Rand io.Reader` (confirmation tokens and instance ids; `nil` ⇒ `crypto/rand`); rigs inject a seeded
deterministic reader so the three parity rigs mint identical tokens.
**Parity runner (amends M2a C42):** `ParityCase.Args` (in `internal/mcpgen/parity_test.go`, today
`func(*servertest.Rig) map[string]any`, with the `fixedArgs` adapter) becomes `func(r *servertest.Rig, prev
map[string]any) map[string]any`, where `prev` is the normalised response of the previous case on the same rig (so a
`create_*` case reads `prev["confirmation_token"]` from its `estimate_*` case; tokens stay masked in the
compared output); every existing M2a case ignores `prev`. Parity rigs use `Backend: "fake"`, `Runner:
false` (no job ever runs during parity; creates stop at `queued`).

**C86.2 Fixtures:** `librarytest.Fixture` keeps its frozen row counts (many suites count them; M2c added
`FixtureM2c` for the same reason). `librarytest.FixtureM3(tb, st, now)` = `FixtureM2c` (so it also carries a
reference FLAC and a lossy reference for the D-M3-34 tests) plus (M3-B2, through the real write paths): one completed job batch (1 job, `reroll_seed`
child of fixture track 1 with a `mutations` row, `env_fingerprints` row, ledger `take`/`attempt_overhead`
reserves, `execution`/`overhead` settles, `residual` and `reconciled` release rows, a reconciled
`billing_windows` row with its `billing_buckets`), one `awaiting_confirmation` agent batch (2 jobs), one
`failed` job (`NO_CAPACITY`, released `never_ran`), `recipe_keys` for every `yue2` recipe (none for stubs), caps at defaults,
provider_state `fake` armed. `archivetest.GenerateArchive(tb) Template` (heavy; `sync.Once`, next to
`SyntheticArchive`): `SyntheticArchive(tb, "acceptance")` plus 6 drafts created through `drafts.Service.Create` with
`parent_track_id` = 6 distinct roots other than syn-song-01's, so together with the imported varianti
draft there are **7 drafts with 7 distinct mothers** (the acceptance profile has 7 lineage roots).
Every test that generates from it takes its own `Template.Copy` (never a shared DB). `make fixture-db`
gains the M3 rows through a third step (`servertest TestWriteFixtureDB_M3`). The E2E profile is unchanged; the M3 Playwright setup creates its drafts through
`create_draft` (C86.5).

**C86.3 Spend property suite** (`internal/jobs/property_test.go`, heavy; as built it runs on a manual clock that the test advances
(deterministic, no `synctest` bubble; the plan's original `synctest.Test` design, with the store opened **inside** the bubble so
`database/sql`'s channels belong to it, was replaced by it); fake backend in-process; the
**stub ingester** and the **header-only SimWorker** — no ffmpeg inside the bubble; the SimWorker's
`Uploader` calls the upload and start handlers directly — no sockets; ntfy is the in-process fake; crash
injection through `RunnerOptions.Fault` at the C75.3 points, after which a **new** runner instance with
a new owner adopts). `TestProperty_SpendNeverExceedsCap` runs `MM_PROPERTY_TIMELINES` timelines (default
**10** as built — the plan's 60 exceeded the 4-minute budget of the `go-media` CI job (`-race`, no `-short`, job timeout raised from 10 to 20 minutes and `go test -timeout 18m` in `scripts/ci-go-test.sh` by the B11 review fix, because the jobs package alone now runs about 7 minutes under `-race`);
the measured times are in `metrics/REPORT.md`; the RunPod profile runs half and the adversarial profile a third of the default;
**300** in the `spend-property` job of `weekly.yml`; skipped under `-short`) from fixed seeds (`math/rand/v2.NewPCG(seed, 0)`), each with 1–8 batches of 1–20 takes
from 1–3 principals (human and agents with daily budgets), random caps (monthly 0.2–3 USD) and **a cap
lowered mid-run** in some timelines, and random events:
- slow runs that hit `timeout_s`, queue expiries (≤ 6 per job), 429/5xx/paused/network/accepted-then-5xx
  submits, `WORKER_LOST`, provider requeues, cancels at random times, `CANCELLED` acknowledged while the
  handler keeps running until its next start poll, runs completing between two polls, results purged
  after 30 min of runner downtime;
- runner crashes between any two steps; runner downtime longer than `IdleS` (and than 30 min); a runner
  **paused past its lease** while a second instance takes over; disarm/arm mid-batch; 429 gaps longer
  than the idle timeout (each later claim cold-starts again);
- revised billing buckets (late adjustments), late billing (hourly buckets with lag), month boundaries
  (timelines that cross a local month end), clock skew spikes.
The fake runs in one of two profiles. **`within`** (default; parameters inside every allowance of the
quote: cold start ≤ `ColdStartS`, kill latency ≤ `KillSlackS`, ≤ 1 worker per job, no warm reuse
required, price rises ≤ the margin) asserts: after **every committed ledger transaction** (the
`spend.Observer` hook) `MonthSettled + Outstanding ≤ MonthlyMicro`, for every principal `DaySettled +
Outstanding ≤ its daily cap` (with the cap in force when each reserve was written), per pot/job/batch `Σ
settle (excl. excess) + Σ release ≤ Σ reserve`, per batch `Σ reserve ≤ ceiling`; **at every fake event**
(worker start/stop, execution start/end) the **reality check** `fake.AccruedCost(t) ≤ settled(t) +
outstanding(t)` per month and `fake.AccruedCostByBatch(t)[b] ≤ settled_b(t) + outstanding_b(t)` per batch
(the fake attributes a cold start to the job that caused it and an idle tail to the last job served),
where `settled` and `outstanding` come from a **test-side model over the raw ledger rows** (its own SQL,
not `spend.Usage`, so the check is not a restatement of `CheckCaps`); per quiet window `billed_compute ≤
reserved`; no `excess` row and no trip; after a final reconciliation every batch is reconciled,
`Outstanding == 0` and `MonthSettled == Σ fake billed`; every job is terminal and every attempt
`done`/`rejected`; the fake reports zero running workers. **`adversarial`** (cold start above the
allowance, extra workers for one job, kill latency above the slack, idle/max raised mid-batch, a price
rise above the margin) asserts the same ledger invariants plus the **detection bound**: whenever the
reality check fails a trip (`meter_exceeds_reserved`, `provisional_exceeds_reserved` or
`billed_exceeds_reserved`) follows, and `billed − reserved ≤ workers.max × (sweep interval + trip
latency) × price` at every instant. A **RunPod profile** runs 30 timelines (`within`; weekly 100)
through the real serverless backend against `runpodtest` (`TestProperty_SpendNeverExceedsCap_RunPod`).
A failing seed is printed for replay. `TestProperty_ColdStartAboveAllowanceTrips` (detection, not
prevention). Named timelines also run as plain tests: `TestRunner_BackoffGapReservesNewColdStart` (429
with `Retry-After` 120 s > `IdleS`: the next claim writes its own `attempt_overhead`, the fake's second
cold start stays within reservations), `TestRunner_UnknownAttemptsCountInFlight` (one ghost + `workers.max
= 2` ⇒ only one more attempt in flight).

**C86.4 Chaos suite** (`internal/jobs/chaos_test.go`; fake backend and, where marked, the serverless
backend against `runpodtest`): `TestChaos_CrashBeforeRefPersisted` (fault `after_submit_return`: the
adopted attempt becomes a ghost, a `topup_ambiguous` reserve is written, the ghost's start announcement
promotes it, one track, spend within reserves) · `TestChaos_GhostAdoptedViaStart` (C74.3) ·
`TestChaos_UploadShaMismatch` (`ARTIFACT_MISMATCH`, settled at the attempt's execution) ·
`TestChaos_SubmitErrors` (table: 1010 ⇒ `failed EDGE_BLOCKED` with zero further submits; 429 ⇒ resubmit
after `Retry-After`; 5xx ⇒ ghost + top-up + resubmit; `ENDPOINT_PAUSED` ⇒ backoff; 401 ⇒ `PROVIDER_AUTH`
and a `provider_access` trip; 404 on `/run` ⇒ `provider_access`; both backends) ·
`TestChaos_QueueExpiryResubmitThenNoCapacity` (fresh upload ids and a fresh `attempt_overhead` per
attempt, backoff 1, 2, 4 … min, the 7th expiry ⇒ `NO_CAPACITY` with `never_ran` release and the batch's
never-submitted jobs failed with it; the batch's reserves never exceed its ceiling) ·
`TestChaos_StuckWorkerTrips` (with write key: cancel all, purge, PATCH max=0, trip, ntfy priority 5) ·
`TestReconciler_TripWithoutWriteKeyAlerts` (PATCH 403: cancel, purge, trip and the **critical**
manual-max-0 ntfy; no silent no-op) · `TestChaos_CancelIgnored` (the fake's `cancel_ignored` ignores only the first cancel, so the "stays
`cancelling`, `Cancel` re-issued every 30 s, the 5-minute ntfy fires; never `cancelled` early" check runs on the scripted provider that never confirms, and a fake subtest checks the re-issue is honoured) ·
`TestChaos_ResultsPurgedDuringDowntime` (runner down 40 min while the job ran and completed: 404 ⇒
`unknown`, time-bound settle, no `never_ran`) · `TestSpend_ConcurrentCreatesNeverExceedCap` (`-race`; 16
goroutines creating batches against a cap that fits 5: exactly 5 succeed, 11 get 402) ·
`TestRunnerLease_Exclusive` (a second runner never claims while the lease is held; takes over within
90 s after the first stops heartbeating) · `TestRunnerLease_PausedRunnerCannotClaim` (C75.3).

**C86.5 Playwright** (M3-W4): fake backend `fast`, proxy mode, `MM_RATE_SCALE=100`; global setup creates
a read+write+generate PAT for the agent-approval spec through the human `create_token` op. **Isolation:**
every spec uses its own source tracks and drafts, created by the setup through `create_draft` or chosen
from disjoint fixture tracks: `m3-batch` gets 6 drafts with mothers (+ the imported varianti draft = 7);
`m3-approval` uses `new_seed` variants of its own two tracks (7 and 2 takes), never `m3-batch`'s drafts;
`m3-variant`, `m3-jobs`, `m3-lineage` and `m3-compose` each have their own track. `m3-spend` (global cap
change, disarm/arm) runs in a **serial project that depends on every other M3 project** (Playwright
`dependencies`), so it never changes caps or arming under another spec.

**C86.6 Device checklist (M3):** DC-M3-1 an agent batch's ntfy "Review" action opens
`/human/confirm/<batch>` on the phone, passes 2FA, approves; DC-M3-2 three-click new-seed variant on a
phone (iPhone + Android); DC-M3-3 Terminate a running job from a paired phone (device principal, write
scope) and see it `cancelled`; DC-M3-4 the trip banner and the "Set max workers to 0" push arrive on the
phone (provoked with the fake backend's `fake:stuck_worker` on a dev instance).

## Lanes and tasks

Stage 1 (branch `m3` from merged `main`, main worktree, `MM_LANE=m3-found`): **M3-B1 → M3-B2 → M3-B3**.
Stage 1 lands every shared contract: config and key files, leaves, migration `0007`, registry/gate
amendments, the engine and provider interfaces, the worker JSON schemas, all 23 registrations with
final input/output types (`STUB(M3-Bn)` handlers answer 501 `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`), every service
constructor and its frozen method set, `app.Build` wiring, the scripted test backend
(`internal/provider/providertest`), `servertest`/`librarytest` extensions, **every library write
amendment** (D-M3-35: `InsertRecipe(…, createdBy)`, the `NewTake` fields `Origin`/`StatusSetBy`/`JobID`/
`EnvID`, the `NewMutation` fields `Params`/`JobID`/`RecipePatchJSON`, score source `job`, the `recipe_keys`
insert, the importer-fulfilment skip for `origin='job'`, all conditional so older-schema fixtures still
build), `synth.FLACUncalibrated`, `notify.Message.Actions`, the spend core (quote, tokens, caps, usage, ledger,
`ReserveTopUp`, `SettleAttempt`, approvals, batch transitions), `openapi.json` and `schema.d.ts`.
**`openapi.json` and `schema.d.ts` are frozen after M3-B2.**
Stage 2 (parallel worktrees under `../metamusic-wt/`, branched from M3-B3):
**Generation** (`m3-gen`: M3-B4), **Jobs** (`m3-jobs`: M3-B5 → M3-B6), **Provider**
(`m3-provider`: M3-B7 → M3-B8 → M3-B9), **Worker** (`m3-worker`: M3-P1 → M3-P2), **Web** (`m3-web`:
M3-W1 → M3-W2 → M3-W3), **Infra** (`m3-infra`: M3-I1 → M3-I2).
Stage 3 (`m3-int`): **M3-B10** (wiring) → **M3-B11** (acceptance, property, chaos, leaks, parity) →
**M3-W4** (Playwright) → **M3-X1** integration → final review → **M3-X2** deploy (spend-free) →
**M3-X3** canary (**operator approval required**) → **M3-X4** real-use validation (operator).
**File ownership:** in Stage 2 no lane edits `internal/api/api.go`, `internal/api/types_m3.go`,
`internal/app/**`, `internal/httpserver/server.go`, `internal/store/migrations/**`,
`internal/provider/provider.go`, `internal/spend/**`, `internal/engine/**` (except the Worker lane's
`contract_cases_test.go`), `internal/library/write.go`, `internal/notify/**`, `internal/store/lease.go`, `internal/synth/**`,
`internal/{idem,audit,gate}/**` (Stage-1 owned; a needed change goes back through this document and a Stage-1-owner fix commit that
every lane rebases onto),
`cmd/metamusic/{main.go,cmd_serve.go,cmd_mcpstdio.go}` (`dev` lives in `cmd_serve.go`), `openapi.json` or
`web/src/api/schema.d.ts`. Each lane replaces only the stubs in its own `internal/api/ops_*_m3.go`
file(s) and owns its packages: Generation — `internal/{lineage,generation}/**`,
`ops_generation_m3.go`, `ops_budget_m3.go`; Jobs — `internal/{jobs,upload}/**`, `ops_jobs_m3.go`,
`ops_provider_m3.go`, and the M1/M2a/M2b **read-path** files named in M3-B6's **Files** (never
`docs/**`); Provider —
`internal/provider/{fake,runpod}/**`, `cmd/metamusic/cmd_runpod.go`; Worker — `worker/**` and
`internal/engine/yue2/contract_cases_test.go`; Web — `web/**` except `schema.d.ts`; Infra —
`.github/**`, `deploy/**`, `scripts/**`, `docs/**`, `Makefile`, `coverage.thresholds`, `.gitignore`,
`.gitleaks.toml`, `metrics/loop.schema.json`.

### Task M3-W4 (Stage 3, Web): Playwright M3 specs

**Files:** `web/e2e/{m3-variant,m3-batch,m3-compose,m3-jobs,m3-approval,m3-spend,m3-lineage}.spec.ts`,
`web/e2e/global-setup.ts` (C86.5 setup; the server starts with `MM_BACKEND=fake` and `MM_FAKE_BACKEND=fast`),
`web/playwright.config.ts` (an `ignoreM3(keep)` helper in the style of `ignoreM2a/b/c`: M3 specs run on
desktop Chrome; `m3-variant` and `m3-jobs` also on the Pixel 7 and iPhone 15 projects; the skip-accounting
comment is updated). **Isolation (M2c lesson):** M3 specs add tracks, jobs and spend rows to the shared e2e
library, which count-based specs (`m1-library`, `m1-pwa`, `m1-import` hard-code 9 tracks; the audit and
export specs count rows) must never see, so every M3 project is a third phase that `dependencies` on
phase 1 and on the `listen-*` projects; inside it `m3-spend` runs last (C86.5).
Specs:
- variant: Track ⇒ Variant ⇒ New seed ×3 ⇒ the estimate shows 3 takes with request ids `…-s<seed>` and a
  max cost ⇒ Spend ⇒ `/batches/:id` ⇒ 3 completed ⇒ the lineage shows 3 children with costs; a child's
  page shows the provenance panel and "A/B with parent".
- batch: Backlog ⇒ select the 7 drafts with mothers ⇒ Generate ⇒ one estimate (7 takes) ⇒ one
  confirmation ⇒ 7 completed ⇒ each child's parent is its draft's mother; drafts show `fulfilled`.
- compose: Library ⇒ New song ⇒ fill style/lyrics (live lint) ⇒ estimate ⇒ Spend ⇒ a root track with
  cost.
- jobs: a `fake:slow=60` take ⇒ `/jobs` shows it generating ⇒ open `/jobs/:id` (live via `wait_for_job`)
  ⇒ Terminate ⇒ `cancelled` appears without a reload; a two-take slow batch ⇒ Terminate batch ⇒ both
  cancelled; month spend, the Ledger tab and the provider tab render.
- approval: an agent batch of 7 `new_seed` takes of the spec's own track (REST with the setup PAT; never
  `m3-batch`'s drafts) is `awaiting_confirmation` ⇒ `/human/confirm/:batch` ⇒ Approve ⇒ runs; a second
  agent batch (2 takes of another own track) ⇒ Reject with a reason ⇒ budget outstanding drops.
- spend (serial project, runs after every other M3 project): `/human/spend` lowers the monthly cap below
  the next estimate ⇒ the generate page shows "would exceed: monthly" and Confirm disabled; raising it
  re-enables; disarm ⇒ banner "disarmed" ⇒ arm.
- lineage: `/lineage/:id` renders the tree; "A/B with parent" opens a non-blind duel; "Compare" opens
  `/compare?a=…&b=…`.
Served-operation coverage: zero unserved ui ops; `/__e2e/leaks` = `[]`.
Done: `make e2e` green locally (chrome + firefox); CI e2e ≤ 6 min (record the measured time).

### Task M3-X1 (Integration)
Merge lane branches into `m3`; resolve D-M3-37 (month-end straddling batch) and remove the property suite's `straddlesMonthEnd` skip; `make tools hooks check test e2e gate cover routing worker contract`
green; `grep -rn 'STUB(M3-' internal` empty; regenerate `openapi.json` + `schema.d.ts` (must be
byte-identical to the frozen ones); update `metrics/REPORT.md` (check/e2e/CI wall times, the property
suite's share of the `go-media` job, worker CI times, coverage, `tools/list` bytes and the largest output
schemas); review (spec + quality + security: money integer-only, every ledger write in its causing
transaction and fenced by the lease predicate, every attempt reserving its overhead, every top-up under
the batch ceiling, idempotency before token consumption, upload channel and start handshake, key
handling, no URL/ref/key in events/logs/responses, transition triggers, trip fallback, restore disarm);
copy D-M3-1 … D-M3-33 into the private design document (§3.3, §3.4, §3.6, §3.7, §4.3 amendments); record
in the roadmap that M2b is on the Release 1 path (D-M3-1) and add the roadmap's missing M2c entry.

### Task M3-X2 (Deploy, spend-free; operator-assisted; host details only in private notes)
1. **Sign-offs (operator):** D-M3-8 (new human-only op `update_provider_state`) and design D20
   (interruption mapping) — recorded in the private notes.
2. Merge to `main`; CI green; `worker-image.yml` publishes the worker image; the operator makes the GHCR
   package public; a follow-up PR commits `deploy/worker-image.digest` (outside `worker/**`, so no
   rebuild is triggered).
3. Add the `scripts/deploy-preflight.sh` call to the host `deploy.sh` (private notes); deploy the app as
   in M1-X2/M2a-X2 (`deploy.sh` from a clean worktree; never a bare `docker compose up -d`/`config`).
   `MM_BACKEND=runpod`; no `runpod.env` yet ⇒ the Jobs page shows "generation disabled: keys_absent", no
   runner starts, and everything else works.
4. `metamusic runpod probe --schema` (no keys) ⇒ no drift, or the snapshot is refreshed by a PR first.
5. **Operator:** places the all-access key file where `--admin-env` points (agents never open it);
   `metamusic runpod provision --image <ref@digest> --dry-run` (`--vae-source image` is the default), review (the
   body pins `type`, `scaling`, `gpu.count`, `minCudaVersion`, `flashboot`, `timeout` = 900 000 ms, and
   excludes L4 only inside a listed pool), then without `--dry-run` ⇒ endpoint `mm-yue2` created
   **inactive** (`workers.max = 0`); cached-model console steps if printed. (No real quote exists before
   this point: the catalog answers 401 without a bearer.)
6. **Operator:** creates the scoped keys in the RunPod console (jobs: endpoint-scoped read/write; read:
   read-only; write: endpoint-scoped PATCH only if the console offers it) and writes `runpod.env` in the
   secrets dir (mode 600) without echoing values; master copies in the password manager.
7. `metamusic runpod probe --keys` (the endpoint is inactive and re-checked before every `/run`, so
   nothing can spend; the write test is a name-only PATCH) ⇒ review the TTL-expiry, cancelled,
   not-found shapes, the 24 h zero billing of the inactive endpoint and the skew ⇒ commit
   `key-capabilities.json` through a PR (gate green).
8. Restart; `metamusic runpod status` ⇒ endpoint inactive ⇒ estimates answer `GENERATION_DISABLED
   endpoint_inactive`; provider disarmed. Smoke: `/worker/v1/uploads/<random>` and
   `/worker/v1/attempts/<random>/start` ⇒ 403 problem through the edge; `/human/api/provider/state` with
   a bearer ⇒ refused; Jobs page renders.
**Nothing in M3-X2 can spend:** the endpoint stays at `workers.max = 0` and the provider disarmed.

### Task M3-X3 (Canary — first real spend; **operator approval required before step 1**)
**Stop here and ask the operator.** Proceed only after the operator has written an explicit approval of
the canary spend into the private notes, stating the per-run and session ceilings, the true worst case
and the prepaid balance as the backstop (P.5). **The operator types every command below**; an agent
may prepare them and review the outputs.
1. Operator: `metamusic runpod provision --activate --max 1` with the admin key (keep the admin key file
   until step 6); arms the provider on `/human/spend` (or `runpod arm --endpoint-name mm-yue2 --reason
   …`); creates a canary PAT (scopes **read + write + generate**, daily budget per P.5) on `/human/tokens`.
2. Set `MM_RUNPOD_RECORD_DIR`; inside the container with `MM_TOKEN` exported from a file by the
   operator: `metamusic runpod canary --url http://127.0.0.1:8080 --case main` ⇒ pass (sha verified,
   `audio_s ≤ 1.5 × expected`, workers 0 within idle + 30 s; cold start, workers-per-job recorded).
3. `… --case cot-full` ⇒ pass; the observed artifact sets of the `melody` and `full` renders are written
   to `internal/provider/artifacts.observed.json`.
4. `… --case terminate` ⇒ pass (`cancelled`, workers 0; worker uptime at cancel and 60 s later recorded).
5. ≥ 35 min after step 2: `metamusic runpod status --job <main job> --repoll` ⇒ record the result-retention
   answer (expected 404).
6. `… --case kill-switch` ⇒ the operator runs `metamusic runpod provision --deactivate` when prompted;
   record what happened to the in-flight job and worker; then `provision --activate --max 1` again.
7. Timeout case: set `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME=30s`, restart, `provision --activate --max 1 --timeout-case`;
   `… --case timeout` ⇒ pass (`TIMED_OUT`, `executionTime` in ms ≤ 30 s + `KillSlackS`); then restore
   `MM_JOB_MAX_RUNTIME`, restart and `provision --activate --max 1` (the drift check must show no
   `MM_WORKER_DEADLINE`). The operator deletes or moves the admin key file afterwards (P.2).
8. After `MM_BILLING_LAG`: `… --verify-billing <batch>` for every canary batch ⇒ reconciled,
   `billed_compute ≤ reserved`; record billed ÷ (list × metered seconds) and whether the idle tail is
   billed.
9. Review the recorded fixtures, run `make gate` on them, commit them under
   `internal/provider/runpod/testdata/fixtures/` together with `artifacts.observed.json`, and remove the
   `TestClient_JobAPIFixtures` skip in the same PR; confirm the C73.2 mapping against the fixtures (or
   open a follow-up to adjust).
10. Unset `MM_RUNPOD_RECORD_DIR`; disarm or keep armed per the operator's choice; `--max 2` only after
   M3-X4's review of the measurements.
**Pass criteria** (each recorded in the private notes; a miss stops M3-X4 until the defaults are
updated): cold start (`announced_at − startedAt`) ≤ `MM_COLD_START_S`; exactly one worker started for a
single job; idle tail billed as assumed (≤ `idle_s` + one billing second per lifetime); `executionTime`
in milliseconds and kill latency ≤ `KillSlackS`; worker uptime stops within `idle_s + 30 s` of the
cancel; the kill-switch behaviour matches the runbook's manual fallback; results gone (404) after ≥ 35
min; billed ÷ (list × worker-seconds) ≤ 1 + margin.

### Task M3-X4 (Real-use validation; operator; results only in private notes)
Update the measured defaults first: `MM_COLD_START_S`, `MM_KILL_SLACK_S`, `MM_PRICE_MARGIN_PPM` and the
crash-loop threshold (`ColdStartS + 60`) from the X3 pass criteria, commit
`internal/provider/fake/canary-measured.json` (public-safe numbers only) so `TestFakeDefaults_MatchCanary`
pins the fake to them, and revisit the scaler (C77.6) if more than one worker started for a single job.
Then: GPU validation of a known imported recipe and seed (`same_seed_edit` with an empty patch =
re-render; **a bit-identical result ends `DUPLICATE_AUDIO` with the original's track id — that is the
"identical" answer, not a failure**): compare by ear and by loudness/bandwidth/Tier A with the original;
the pilot re-run through the app compared with the earlier pod pilot's cost; the real v2 batch (one
estimate, one confirmation) from the UI; device checklist DC-M3-1…4; Release 1 gate check (every [S]
item of M1, M2a, M2b, M3 green as named tests + device checklist) and tag per the Definition of done.

## Definition of done (M3)

- All named tests above green; `make check` < 30 s warm; `make test` green with media tools; `make e2e`
  green (chrome + firefox) with zero unserved ui ops and `/__e2e/leaks` empty; `make gate`, `make
  routing`, `make worker`, `make contract` green; coverage thresholds (C85) met on merged data.
- Parity: every non-excluded M3 op has a parity case (REST = MCP = stdio after normalisation); the
  `tools/list` golden is re-baselined under the new ceiling (D-M3-36).
- Money: integer micro-USD everywhere (no float in `money`, `spend`, `runpod` money paths); the property
  suite (60 timelines per CI run, 300 weekly, `within` + `adversarial` + RunPod profiles) and the chaos
  suite green; `TestNoSecretLeak` green.
- The worker image is built by CI, public on GHCR, pinned by digest on the endpoint; the worker-contract
  workflow is green against the pinned yue2 commit.
- Spend-free deploy done (M3-X2) with the probe committed; the canary (M3-X3) passed every pass
  criterion **only after** the operator's written approval, with redacted fixtures and the observed
  artifact sets committed and billed ≤ reserved; the measured defaults updated (M3-X4).
- Every roadmap M3 acceptance item maps to a green named test or a signed-off device-checklist item;
  Release 1 gate status recorded (`v0.3.0-m3`; `v1.0.0` when the gate passes).
- No real titles, artists, lyrics, hostnames, endpoint ids, request-id prefixes, balances or personal
  data in any tracked file; the public plan copy contains nothing below the private-appendix marker.

## Risks / open questions

- **RunPod v2 field drift.** The management fields come from the 2026-10-12 snapshot; the weekly drift
  job and `TestClient_ManagementFieldsInSnapshot` catch renames, but a silent semantic change needs the
  canary. What `workers.max = 0` does to an in-flight job is measured by the canary's `kill-switch` case;
  trips also cancel, purge and (through the start poll) stop the handler, so either way spend stops
  within one start-poll interval or one `executionTimeout`.
- **PATCH nested-object semantics** (merge vs replace) are not pinned; every PATCH sends complete nested
  objects and verifies with a GET (C77.5), and the probe never PATCHes anything but `name`.
- **Cancel semantics.** Whether `/cancel` kills the handler is unverified; the start-URL poll (≤ 15 s)
  stops the render either way, the `terminate` case measures the real behaviour, and the C75.4 5-minute
  alert covers a slow confirmation.
- **Queue-side status shapes** (TTL expiry, cancelled-while-queued, job/endpoint not found) are pinned
  by the spend-free probe before any spend; until then every such case is settled by its time bound
  (conservative, C73.2).
- **Scaler choice.** `QUEUE_DELAY` with `queueDelay = ColdStartS + 60` may still start a second worker
  for a job whose cold start overruns its allowance; the crash-loop trip and the meter catch it. If the
  canary sees more than one worker per job, M3-X4 revisits (C77.6).
- **Billing lag** may exceed `MM_BILLING_LAG` (6 h); late adjustments over the last 72 h correct it, and
  residuals hold cap until reconciliation. The UI shows outstanding vs settled so a "full" month is
  explainable; the operator can raise the monthly cap.
- **Cold-start allowance** (180 s) is an assumption until measured (fresh-host loads of the 7.3 GB model
  and the VAE); too low makes `meter_exceeds_reserved` trips within ~60 s (detected, not prevented —
  `TestProperty_ColdStartAboveAllowanceTrips`). M3-X4 updates it from the canary.
- **List vs billed price**: the catalog price is a list price; the +10 % margin covers a mismatch until
  the canary measures the ratio.
- **VAE location** is decided (D-M3-38): baked into the image. A network volume would tie the endpoint to
  one datacenter (fewer GPUs, more `NO_CAPACITY`); RunPod offers no second cached model.
- **CUDA wheel** of torch 2.10 from PyPI must be ≥ 12.8 (the image smoke asserts it); `gpu.minCudaVersion`
  is pinned to `12.8` to match.
- **Upload reachability**: if the app is unreachable for the whole upload window, the take fails and its
  GPU time is paid (design §10.14; R2 fallback in M6); the claim preflight and the deploy preflight make
  the common causes (full disk, broken route, a deploy) refuse new claims instead.
- **Determinism** of same-seed re-renders across GPU classes is best-effort; env fingerprints make the
  comparison honest, not equal; a bit-identical re-render reports `DUPLICATE_AUDIO`.
- **synctest with SQLite**: the property suite opens the store inside the bubble; if a future
  `database/sql`/modernc change breaks durable blocking, the suite falls back to the manual clock driving
  `Runner.Step`/`Runner.Drain`/`Reconciler.Pass` (the code supports both; only the harness changes).
- **`tools/list` growth** (+11 tools) is bounded by the ceiling and the demotion order (C85), which ends
  with "stop and ask the operator".
- **Large backlogs** need several batches of ≤ 50 and enough monthly cap (D-M3-7); a "generate in
  chunks" helper is deferred until the real use shows it is needed.
- **New parity exception** (`update_provider_state`, D-M3-8) needs the operator's sign-off in M3-X2;
  without it the arm/reset actions would be CLI-only (the op would be dropped and the UI would show the
  CLI command instead).
